# VizDAW Engine (Sub-project 2b) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Make a core-model sequence play sample-accurately through Apple's built-in DLS synth (and CoreMIDI), with a real-time path that never blocks or allocates: crates `core-engine` (platform-neutral compiler, transport, renderer, offline backend), `platform-macos` (HAL IOProc, DLS synth node, CoreMIDI output) and the `vizdaw-play` CLI.

**Architecture:** `Engine::load` (control thread) flattens a sequence with 2a's `flatten`, routes each event to a `TargetIdx`, converts ticks to frames with 2a's `TempoMap::tick_to_seconds`, translates MIDI 2.0 → 1.0 and sorts the result into an immutable `Box<Schedule>`, which travels to the `Renderer` through an `rtrb` command ring; retired schedules come back through a second ring and are freed on a collector thread. `Renderer::process` (RT) drains commands, steps a `Transport` that splits each block at loop/sequence ends, emits entries at exact frame offsets to sinks (`InstrumentNode` = DLS synth, `MidiOut` = wait-free ring to a CoreMIDI sender thread), mixes, clamps and publishes atomics. Backends implement `AudioBackend`: `OfflineBackend` (tests, `--offline`) and `CoreAudioBackend` (HAL IOProc).

**Tech Stack:** Rust stable (1.99.1 installed; workspace `rust-version = "1.85"`), edition 2024, Cargo workspace (resolver 3, `members = ["crates/*"]`); `core-model` (2a, path), `rtrb 0.4`, `thiserror 2`, `objc2-core-audio`/`objc2-core-audio-types`/`objc2-audio-toolbox`/`objc2-core-midi`/`objc2-core-foundation` `0.3.2`, `block2 0.6`, `clap 4`; dev `proptest 1`, `insta 1`, `im 15`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-engine-design.md` (binding, its Rulings accepted); 2a API from `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-core-model.md`; demo content from `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-app-shell-design.md` §6. Executors read all three.

- Every shell command runs after `export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH" DEVELOPER_DIR=/Library/Developer/CommandLineTools` (Rust lives in `~/.cargo/bin`; the Xcode license is not accepted, so `git`, `cc` and the linker work only with the Command Line Tools). Every bash block below starts with that line.
- Work in `/Users/lattawb/workspace/vizdaw` on branch `overnight`. The 2a plan (`crates/core-model`, Tasks 1–18) must be complete and green before Task 1. Commit at the end of every task, adding only the files the task lists; never push. If your harness requires an attribution trailer on commits, append it to the messages given.
- The root `Cargo.toml` already has `members = ["crates/*"]`, so the three new crates join the workspace without editing it.
- Rust stable, edition 2024 (`edition.workspace = true`, `license.workspace = true`, `rust-version.workspace = true` in every crate manifest).
- `core-engine` has no OS dependencies and builds and tests on Linux. `platform-macos` is `#![cfg(target_os = "macos")]` with its dependencies under `[target.'cfg(target_os = "macos")'.dependencies]`; `vizdaw-play`'s live playback is cfg-gated the same way.
- `core-engine` and `platform-macos` deny `clippy::unwrap_used`, `expect_used`, `indexing_slicing` and `panic` in library code via `#![cfg_attr(not(test), deny(...))]` (the same lints as 2a) and `#![deny(missing_docs)]`. Tests may `unwrap`.
- `platform-macos` keeps all `unsafe` in `hal.rs`, `dls.rs` and `midi.rs`, with a `// SAFETY:` comment per block. (The only other `unsafe` in the plan: the forwarding `GlobalAlloc` impl in `core-engine/src/rt_check.rs` and the `signal(2)` declaration in `vizdaw-play/src/signal.rs`, each commented.)
- Everything reachable from the render callback is marked `// RT`. Code marked `// RT` must not allocate, free, lock, do I/O, log, or make a syscall other than CoreAudio/CoreMIDI render-time calls.
- Crate versions are exactly those in the Tech Stack line; no other runtime crates. objc2 crates use `default-features = false` with only the features listed in Task 10.
- Event frames come only from 2a's `TempoMap::tick_to_seconds`; frame 0 is the sequence origin (`-start_offset`). Offline renders run at 48 kHz in 512-frame blocks.
- Each task ends green: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace`. The code below is already `rustfmt`-formatted; run `cargo fmt --all` anyway.
- Automated steps never play audio for more than 3 seconds; long listening is the human smoke test in Task 14.

## Review Focus

The five failure modes most likely to bite a user that the spec implies but does not spell out, each pinned by a named test in its owning task:

1. **Editing while playing across a tempo change** — the swap keeps the musical position (same tick), cuts sounding notes at the swap frame and never retriggers notes that started earlier. Pinned: Task 8 `schedule_swap_mid_play_keeps_position_and_cuts_notes`.
2. **Loops shorter than a block, and loop ends exactly on a block boundary** — every wrap releases notes before the loop start replays, and the event log does not depend on block size. Pinned: Task 6 `block_splits_exactly_at_loop_end`, `loop_end_on_a_block_boundary_wraps_in_the_next_block`; Task 8 `logs_do_not_depend_on_block_size` (loops on and off).
3. **Steep tempo ramps (5 ↔ 999 BPM within a bar)** — compiled frames and real-time frame ↔ tick lookups stay within ±1 of 2a's conversion. Pinned: Task 3 `table_lookups_stay_within_one`; Task 5 `compiled_frames_match_2a`.
4. **Projects that can only partly play** (plugin instruments, missing MIDI destinations, lanes without a destination, MIDI 2.0-only data) — the rest plays and every skipped event is reported with a reason. Pinned: Task 2 `midi2_only_data_is_counted`; Task 5 `unroutable_events_are_reported_not_played`; Task 8 `unroutable_plugin_is_reported_and_absent`; Task 13 `several_top_level_sequences_list_and_exit_2`.
5. **Hanging notes on stop, locate, loop wrap and Ctrl-C, including a held sustain pedal** — every sounding note gets a note-off and every held pedal CC 64 = 0. Pinned: Task 6 `notes_and_pedals_are_released`; Task 8 `stop_and_locate_send_note_offs`, `loop_wraps_three_times_with_note_offs`; Task 14 smoke step 2.

## Plan Rulings

Decisions the spec leaves open, each implemented and tested as stated (format: Ruling — why — cost if wrong):

1. **Ruling:** A MIDI 2.0 note-on with velocity 0 is sent as MIDI 1.0 velocity 1, like any velocity that rounds to 0 — why: in MIDI 2.0 velocity 0 is a real note, in MIDI 1.0 it means note-off (M2-104 maps it to 1) — cost if wrong: an inaudible note becomes a faint one; one line in `midi1.rs`.
2. **Ruling:** `TempoTable` knots are at most 960 ticks apart inside ramps and are subdivided (down to 1 tick) until linear interpolation is within 0.25 tick (frame → tick) and 0.25 frame (tick → frame) of 2a's conversion — why: 960-tick knots alone break the spec's ±1 proptest both on steep ramps (tick error) and on slow ramps near 5 BPM, where one tick is 37.5 frames (frame error); both failures were reproduced — cost if wrong: more knots on extreme ramps (bounded by one per tick), a few KB per schedule.
3. **Ruling:** The default loop (`Engine::set_loop(true)`) runs from bar 1 (tick 0) to the sequence end, so a pickup plays only before the first pass; `Engine::set_loop_range` takes explicit frames — why: the model has no loop range and the spec's `SetLoop(Option<(u64, u64)>)` needs one — cost if wrong: a later model loop range replaces `Schedule.loop_range`, local to `compile.rs`.
4. **Ruling:** A destination's `default_patch` (bank CC 0/32, then program) is scheduled at frame 0, before same-frame patch events, for every destination that plays at least one event — why: 2a documents it as "sent when the destination is first used" — cost if wrong: extra program changes at start; one block in `compile.rs`.
5. **Ruling:** UMP groups are dropped for every target (entries are MIDI 1.0 bytes); events from groups ≠ 0 are counted in `CompileReport.group_folded` with one warning — why: spec §4's `Entry` has no group and §5 already folds groups on the built-in synth — cost if wrong: multi-group CoreMIDI gear gets group 0; a group byte fits in the 24-byte `Entry`.
6. **Ruling:** Sinks for new targets are made on the control thread by a `SinkFactory` during `Engine::load` and installed with `EngineCmd::AddSink`; targets are append-only — why: the renderer gets its DLS synth and MIDI outs without allocating, and no sink is ever freed on the audio thread — cost if wrong: unused targets (≤ 16) live until the engine is rebuilt.
7. **Ruling:** Play at or past the end restarts from frame 0; a locate during the 1 s tail resumes playing — why: spec silent, DAW convention — cost if wrong: two lines in `transport.rs`.
8. **Ruling:** At the sequence end and at loop wraps the active-note tracker sends the note-offs (velocity 0); scheduled note-offs exactly on the end frame are not emitted separately — why: `flatten` clips notes at the length, so those note-offs coincide with the release — cost if wrong: release velocity is lost for notes ending exactly at the end.
9. **Ruling:** The block is split iteratively (`Transport::next` returns one step at a time) instead of a pre-allocated sub-range list — why: a loop shorter than a block can wrap any number of times, and iteration needs no buffer — cost if wrong: none.
10. **Ruling:** A schedule swap also maps an active loop range through the old and new tempo tables (tick-preserving) — why: the loop stays on the same bars after a tempo edit — cost if wrong: loop points jump after tempo edits; local to `render.rs`.
11. **Ruling:** `CompileReport.compile_time` excludes sink creation (instantiating the DLS synth takes ~10 ms) — why: the spec's < 10 ms budget is for compilation — cost if wrong: none.
12. **Ruling:** `MidiDestination` targets carry the full `MidiPortInfo`; an `endpoint_ref` matches a destination by display name, then by its unique ID written in decimal — why: spec §5 says "by unique ID if stored" and 2a stores only a string — cost if wrong: renamed devices need the decimal-ID convention until the model stores IDs.
13. **Ruling:** The offline `EventLog` is one `rtrb` ring per recording sink, merged by `(frame, target, arrival)` on read; lines are `frame tTARGET hex-bytes` without padding, SysEx truncated after 16 bytes with `…` — why: no lock on the render path; unpadded lines keep insta inline snapshots exact — cost if wrong: regenerate `fixtures/demo.offline.txt` and the snapshots.
14. **Ruling:** Short fixture logs are snapshotted in full; the demo log is snapshotted as a digest (counts per status byte, first/last lines, FNV-1a hash), and `fixtures/demo.offline.txt` holds the full text — why: a 335-line inline snapshot hides what matters — cost if wrong: a hash change is explained by diffing the checked-in log.
15. **Ruling:** `Engine::pause_collector(bool)` is a public diagnostic hook — why: tests must fill the 8-slot retire ring deterministically to exercise deferred swaps — cost if wrong: one extra public method.
16. **Ruling:** `fixtures::demo_document()` returns `Result<Document, ModelError>` and the fixture `Builder` is public — why: library code may not panic; the builder is shared by tests, the CLI and 2c — cost if wrong: callers handle one `Result`.
17. **Ruling:** Demo content: the second Groove use (bar 5, transpose +5) transposes everything inside it, drums included (the kit plays toms in bars 5–8); that transposition is the "bass variation"; the chords follow (+5) in bars 5–8; Drums carry default patch 0 to exercise default patches — why: 2a's transpose applies to every note of a reference and §6 names no other variation mechanism — cost if wrong: edit `fixtures.rs`, rerun `--write-demo`/`--offline` and update the digest snapshot.
18. **Ruling:** `dls_renders_sound` checks the −80 dBFS decay after 3 s, not 2 s — why: measured: the DLS synth's built-in reverb tail is about −78 dBFS at 2 s for every GM program tried — cost if wrong: none; the threshold is unchanged.
19. **Ruling:** CoreMIDI round-trip timestamps are compared in host-clock ticks — why: ns → host → ns loses up to one 41.7 ns tick on Apple Silicon while ticks round-trip exactly — cost if wrong: none.
20. **Ruling:** The CoreMIDI sender sends one `MIDIEventList` per ring packet (one UMP; SysEx7 in 6-byte chunks) — why: simplest correct packing, off the audio thread — cost if wrong: more IPC calls for long SysEx; batching is local to `midi.rs`.
21. **Ruling:** `block2 0.6` is added to `platform-macos`, and `objc2-core-midi` enables its `objc2` feature — why: `MIDIInputPortCreateWithProtocol` takes a block, and block arguments need objc2 `Encode` impls; the test monitor (`MidiMonitor`) lives in `midi.rs` so all unsafe stays there — cost if wrong: one more crate from the same objc2 family, already in the dependency tree.
22. **Ruling:** `vizdaw-play` builds on every platform; only live playback is macOS-only (`--offline`, `--write-demo` and `--list` work on Linux) — why: Linux CI can run the offline diff — cost if wrong: none.
23. **Ruling:** Ctrl-C uses `signal(2)` through a three-line `extern "C"` declaration instead of the `libc` crate — why: the spec's crate table has no `libc`, and on macOS `signal` has sigaction (BSD) semantics — cost if wrong: switch to `libc::sigaction` with a new dependency.
24. **Ruling:** `--offline --loop` renders three passes (the loop is switched off during the third) and then the tail — why: an offline render must terminate — cost if wrong: none.
25. **Ruling:** On device loss, default-device change or rate change the CLI rebuilds backend + engine (new DLS at the new rate, recompile, locate to the old tick, resume); a buffer-size change stops, calls `Renderer::prepare` if needed, and restarts — why: spec §9 allows "stop and reopen" and this keeps the position — cost if wrong: a ~50 ms gap on device switches.
26. **Ruling:** `tests/rt_alloc.rs` is one test function — why: the allocation counter is global, and parallel tests in the same binary would race — cost if wrong: none.
27. **Ruling:** Audio regions are skipped and counted (`CompileReport.skipped_audio`, one warning); text events are not output and not counted (spec §6) — why: audio playback is deferred to sub-project 5 — cost if wrong: none.
28. **Ruling:** CI runs the ignored `hal_default_device_opens` on macOS runners but tolerates its failure (runners may lack an output device) — cost if wrong: a broken HAL path is caught only by the manual smoke test.

### Task 1: core-engine scaffold — errors and backend types

**Goal:** Create the `core-engine` crate with its lint gates, the two error enums of spec §11 and the platform-neutral backend types and traits of spec §7 (the `AudioBackend` trait arrives with the renderer in Task 7).

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/core-engine/Cargo.toml`
- Create: `crates/core-engine/src/lib.rs`
- Create: `crates/core-engine/src/error.rs`
- Create: `crates/core-engine/src/backend.rs`
- Test: `crates/core-engine/tests/backend.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `core_model::ModelError` (2a Task 1), with `ModelError::invalid(reason)`.
- Produces:
  - `core_engine::{BackendError, EngineError}` — `BackendError::{NoDevice, Format { detail: String }, Os { call: &'static str, status: i32 }, ComponentMissing}`; `EngineError::{Model(ModelError), UnknownSequence(String), Backend(BackendError), Busy, TooManyTargets}` with `From<ModelError>` and `From<BackendError>`; both `Debug + Clone + PartialEq + Display`.
  - `core_engine::backend::MAX_FRAMES: u32 = 4096`.
  - `backend::BlockTime { host_time_ns: u64, sample_rate: f64 }`, `AudioConfig { device: Option<String>, buffer_frames: Option<u32> }` (`Default`), `StreamInfo { device_name: String, sample_rate: f64, buffer_frames: u32, channels: u32 }`, `BackendEvent::{DeviceLost, DefaultDeviceChanged, SampleRateChanged(f64), BufferSizeChanged(u32)}`.
  - `backend::StereoBuf { pub left: Box<[f32]>, pub right: Box<[f32]> }` with `new(frames: u32)`, `capacity(&self) -> u32`, `clear(&mut self, frames: u32)` (RT, clamps).
  - `trait InstrumentNode: Send { fn midi(&mut self, offset: u32, msg: &[u8]); fn render(&mut self, frames: u32, out: &mut StereoBuf); fn reset(&mut self); }`
  - `trait MidiOut: Send { fn send(&mut self, host_time_ns: u64, msg: &[u8]) -> bool; }`
  - `MidiPortInfo { name: String, unique_id: Option<i32> }` (`Clone + Eq + Hash`), `trait MidiBackend { fn destinations(&self) -> Vec<MidiPortInfo>; fn open_virtual_source(&mut self, name: &str) -> Result<Box<dyn MidiOut>, BackendError>; fn open_destination(&mut self, port: &MidiPortInfo) -> Result<Box<dyn MidiOut>, BackendError>; }`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `crates/core-engine/tests/backend.rs`:

```rust
use core_engine::backend::{MAX_FRAMES, MidiPortInfo, StereoBuf};
use core_engine::{BackendError, EngineError};
use core_model::ModelError;

#[test]
fn stereo_buf_is_zeroed_and_clears_a_prefix() {
    let mut b = StereoBuf::new(8);
    assert_eq!(b.capacity(), 8);
    assert!(b.left.iter().chain(b.right.iter()).all(|x| *x == 0.0));
    b.left.fill(1.0);
    b.right.fill(-1.0);
    b.clear(3);
    assert_eq!(&b.left[..4], &[0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0]);
    assert_eq!(&b.right[..4], &[0.0, 0.0, 0.0, -1.0]);
    b.clear(100); // more than the capacity: clamps, never panics
    assert!(b.left.iter().all(|x| *x == 0.0));
    assert_eq!(MAX_FRAMES, 4096);
}

#[test]
fn errors_read_well() {
    let os = BackendError::Os {
        call: "AudioDeviceStart",
        status: -50,
    };
    assert_eq!(os.to_string(), "AudioDeviceStart failed with status -50");
    let e: EngineError = os.clone().into();
    assert_eq!(e, EngineError::Backend(os));
    let m: EngineError = ModelError::invalid("bad").into();
    assert!(matches!(m, EngineError::Model(_)));
    assert_eq!(
        EngineError::UnknownSequence("Song".into()).to_string(),
        "no sequence named \"Song\""
    );
    assert_eq!(
        EngineError::Busy.to_string(),
        "engine command queue is full"
    );
}

#[test]
fn port_info_compares_by_name_and_id() {
    let a = MidiPortInfo {
        name: "IAC Bus 1".into(),
        unique_id: Some(7),
    };
    assert_eq!(a.clone(), a);
    assert_ne!(
        a,
        MidiPortInfo {
            name: "IAC Bus 1".into(),
            unique_id: None
        }
    );
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/lib.rs`:

```rust
//! `core-engine`: compiles a core-model sequence into an immutable schedule
//! and plays it sample-accurately through platform backends, with a
//! real-time path that never blocks or allocates.
//!
//! Everything reachable from `render::Renderer::process` is marked
//! `// RT`; such code must not allocate, free, lock, do I/O or log.
#![deny(missing_docs)]
#![cfg_attr(
    not(test),
    deny(
        clippy::unwrap_used,
        clippy::expect_used,
        clippy::indexing_slicing,
        clippy::panic
    )
)]

pub mod backend;
pub mod error;

pub use error::{BackendError, EngineError};
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/error.rs`:

```rust
//! Engine and backend errors. Real-time faults are bits, never errors (see
//! `render::Faults`).

use core_model::ModelError;

/// Failures of an audio or MIDI backend.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum BackendError {
    /// No output device (or no device with the requested name).
    #[error("no audio output device")]
    NoDevice,
    /// The device's stream format is not f32 linear PCM.
    #[error("unsupported stream format: {detail}")]
    Format {
        /// What was found.
        detail: String,
    },
    /// An OS call failed.
    #[error("{call} failed with status {status}")]
    Os {
        /// The failing call.
        call: &'static str,
        /// Its `OSStatus` (or errno).
        status: i32,
    },
    /// The built-in synth's audio component is not installed.
    #[error("audio component missing")]
    ComponentMissing,
}

/// Failures of the non-real-time engine API.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum EngineError {
    /// The model refused something (e.g. an unknown sequence ID).
    #[error(transparent)]
    Model(#[from] ModelError),
    /// No sequence with this name.
    #[error("no sequence named {0:?}")]
    UnknownSequence(String),
    /// A backend call failed.
    #[error(transparent)]
    Backend(#[from] BackendError),
    /// The command ring to the audio thread is full; retry later.
    #[error("engine command queue is full")]
    Busy,
    /// The document needs more than 16 playback targets.
    #[error("more than 16 playback targets")]
    TooManyTargets,
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/backend.rs`:

```rust
//! Platform-neutral backend traits. `platform-macos` implements them with
//! CoreAudio/CoreMIDI; the `offline` module implements them for tests.

use crate::error::BackendError;

/// Largest block any node or renderer is prepared for.
pub const MAX_FRAMES: u32 = 4096;

/// Timing of one block: its first frame reaches the output at
/// `host_time_ns`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct BlockTime {
    /// Host time (nanoseconds) at which the block's first frame is heard.
    pub host_time_ns: u64,
    /// Stream sample rate.
    pub sample_rate: f64,
}

/// What the caller wants from an audio device.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct AudioConfig {
    /// Device name; `None` follows the system default output.
    pub device: Option<String>,
    /// Requested buffer size in frames (32–2048); `None` keeps the device's.
    pub buffer_frames: Option<u32>,
}

/// What a backend opened.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct StreamInfo {
    /// Device display name.
    pub device_name: String,
    /// Nominal sample rate.
    pub sample_rate: f64,
    /// IO buffer size in frames.
    pub buffer_frames: u32,
    /// Output channel count.
    pub channels: u32,
}

/// Something that happened to the device, reported by
/// [`AudioBackend::take_events`].
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum BackendEvent {
    /// The device disappeared.
    DeviceLost,
    /// The system default output changed (only when following it).
    DefaultDeviceChanged,
    /// The nominal sample rate changed.
    SampleRateChanged(f64),
    /// The IO buffer size changed.
    BufferSizeChanged(u32),
}

/// Planar stereo scratch buffers, allocated once.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct StereoBuf {
    /// Left channel.
    pub left: Box<[f32]>,
    /// Right channel.
    pub right: Box<[f32]>,
}

impl StereoBuf {
    /// Zeroed buffers of `frames` samples per channel.
    pub fn new(frames: u32) -> StereoBuf {
        let n = frames as usize;
        StereoBuf {
            left: vec![0.0; n].into_boxed_slice(),
            right: vec![0.0; n].into_boxed_slice(),
        }
    }

    /// Capacity in frames.
    pub fn capacity(&self) -> u32 {
        u32::try_from(self.left.len().min(self.right.len())).unwrap_or(u32::MAX)
    }

    /// Zeroes the first `frames` samples of both channels. // RT
    pub fn clear(&mut self, frames: u32) {
        let n = (frames as usize).min(self.left.len()).min(self.right.len());
        if let (Some(l), Some(r)) = (self.left.get_mut(..n), self.right.get_mut(..n)) {
            l.fill(0.0);
            r.fill(0.0);
        }
    }
}

/// An instrument hosted inside the engine (the built-in synth, later
/// plugins). Every method is called on the real-time thread.
pub trait InstrumentNode: Send {
    /// Queues one MIDI 1.0 message (or a whole `F0 … F7` SysEx) at a frame
    /// offset within the next `render` call. // RT
    fn midi(&mut self, offset: u32, msg: &[u8]);
    /// Renders `frames` frames into `out`, replacing its contents. // RT
    fn render(&mut self, frames: u32, out: &mut StereoBuf);
    /// All notes off and controllers reset. // RT
    fn reset(&mut self);
}

/// A timestamped MIDI output. `send` must be wait-free.
pub trait MidiOut: Send {
    /// Queues `msg` for delivery at `host_time_ns`; `false` means it was
    /// dropped because the queue is full. // RT
    fn send(&mut self, host_time_ns: u64, msg: &[u8]) -> bool;
}

/// A MIDI destination visible to the system.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub struct MidiPortInfo {
    /// Display name.
    pub name: String,
    /// CoreMIDI unique ID, when known.
    pub unique_id: Option<i32>,
}

/// The system MIDI service.
pub trait MidiBackend {
    /// Destinations that can be opened.
    fn destinations(&self) -> Vec<MidiPortInfo>;
    /// Creates VizDAW's own virtual source.
    fn open_virtual_source(&mut self, name: &str) -> Result<Box<dyn MidiOut>, BackendError>;
    /// Opens an existing destination.
    fn open_destination(&mut self, port: &MidiPortInfo) -> Result<Box<dyn MidiOut>, BackendError>;
}
```

### Task 2: MIDI 2.0 → 1.0 translation (`midi1`)

**Goal:** Pure, allocation-free translation of one 2a `EventBody` into up to three MIDI 1.0 messages (spec §6), each tagged with when it plays (event start or end) and its same-frame sort class, plus counts of MIDI 2.0-only data that cannot be carried.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/core-engine/src/midi1.rs`
- Modify: `crates/core-engine/src/lib.rs`
- Test: `crates/core-engine/tests/midi1.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `core_model::event::{EventBody, NoteAttribute, PerNoteCtl, PerNoteKind, TextKind}` (2a Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `midi1::Midi1Msg { pub len: u8, pub bytes: [u8; 3] }` (`Copy + Eq + Hash`), `const fn two(status, d1)`, `const fn three(status, d1, d2)`, `as_slice(&self) -> &[u8]`.
  - `midi1::Class::{NoteOff = 0, Patch = 1, Control = 2, NoteOn = 3}` (`Ord`), `Class::of(status: u8) -> Class`.
  - `midi1::When::{Start, End}`; `midi1::Out { when: When, class: Class, msg: Midi1Msg }`; `midi1::Drops { per_note: usize, attribute: bool, sysex8: bool }`.
  - `midi1::Translated<'a>` with `msgs(&self) -> &[Out]`, `pub sysex: Option<&'a [u8]>` (7-bit payload without F0/F7), `pub drops: Drops`.
  - `midi1::translate(body: &EventBody, channel: u8) -> Translated<'_>`; helpers `note_on_velocity(u16) -> u8` (never 0), `release_velocity(u16) -> u8`, `value7(u32) -> u8`, `bend14(u32) -> (lsb, msb)`.

Create `crates/core-engine/src/midi1.rs`:

```rust
//! MIDI 2.0 → MIDI 1.0 translation (M2-104 default rules), done at compile
//! time. Pure functions; no allocation.

use core_model::event::EventBody;

/// One MIDI 1.0 channel message of 1–3 bytes.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub struct Midi1Msg {
    /// Number of valid bytes (1–3).
    pub len: u8,
    /// The bytes; unused ones are zero.
    pub bytes: [u8; 3],
}

impl Midi1Msg {
    /// A two-byte message (program change, channel pressure).
    pub const fn two(status: u8, d1: u8) -> Midi1Msg {
        Midi1Msg {
            len: 2,
            bytes: [status, d1, 0],
        }
    }

    /// A three-byte message.
    pub const fn three(status: u8, d1: u8, d2: u8) -> Midi1Msg {
        Midi1Msg {
            len: 3,
            bytes: [status, d1, d2],
        }
    }

    /// The valid bytes.
    pub fn as_slice(&self) -> &[u8] {
        let n = usize::from(self.len.min(3));
        self.bytes.get(..n).unwrap_or(&[])
    }
}

/// Sort class for events on the same frame: note-offs first, then patch
/// changes, then controllers, then note-ons.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
pub enum Class {
    /// Note off.
    NoteOff = 0,
    /// Bank select and program change.
    Patch = 1,
    /// CC, pitch bend, pressure, SysEx.
    Control = 2,
    /// Note on.
    NoteOn = 3,
}

impl Class {
    /// The class of a MIDI 1.0 message by its status byte.
    pub fn of(status: u8) -> Class {
        match status & 0xF0 {
            0x80 => Class::NoteOff,
            0x90 => Class::NoteOn,
            0xC0 => Class::Patch,
            _ => Class::Control,
        }
    }
}

/// When a translated message plays relative to its event.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum When {
    /// At the event's tick.
    Start,
    /// At `tick + duration` (note-offs).
    End,
}

/// MIDI 2.0-only data that a translation could not carry.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Drops {
    /// Per-note controllers and per-note pitch bend on the note.
    pub per_note: usize,
    /// A note attribute was present.
    pub attribute: bool,
    /// 8-bit SysEx was skipped.
    pub sysex8: bool,
}

/// One translated message with its timing and sort class.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Out {
    /// Start or end of the event.
    pub when: When,
    /// Sort class (bank-select CCs belong to [`Class::Patch`]).
    pub class: Class,
    /// The message.
    pub msg: Midi1Msg,
}

/// The result of translating one event.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Translated<'a> {
    msgs: [Out; 3],
    len: usize,
    /// A 7-bit SysEx payload (without `F0`/`F7`).
    pub sysex: Option<&'a [u8]>,
    /// What was dropped.
    pub drops: Drops,
}

impl<'a> Translated<'a> {
    fn empty() -> Translated<'a> {
        Translated {
            msgs: [Out {
                when: When::Start,
                class: Class::Control,
                msg: Midi1Msg::two(0, 0),
            }; 3],
            len: 0,
            sysex: None,
            drops: Drops::default(),
        }
    }

    fn push(&mut self, when: When, msg: Midi1Msg) {
        let class = Class::of(msg.bytes[0]);
        self.push_class(when, class, msg);
    }

    fn push_class(&mut self, when: When, class: Class, msg: Midi1Msg) {
        if let Some(slot) = self.msgs.get_mut(self.len) {
            *slot = Out { when, class, msg };
            self.len += 1;
        }
    }

    /// The channel messages, in output order.
    pub fn msgs(&self) -> &[Out] {
        self.msgs.get(..self.len).unwrap_or(&[])
    }
}

/// 16-bit note-on velocity to 7 bits; never 0 (0 would mean note-off).
pub fn note_on_velocity(v: u16) -> u8 {
    ((v >> 9) as u8).max(1)
}

/// 16-bit release velocity to 7 bits.
pub fn release_velocity(v: u16) -> u8 {
    (v >> 9) as u8
}

/// 32-bit controller or pressure value to 7 bits.
pub fn value7(v: u32) -> u8 {
    (v >> 25) as u8
}

/// 32-bit pitch bend to 14 bits as `(lsb, msb)`.
pub fn bend14(v: u32) -> (u8, u8) {
    let b = v >> 18;
    ((b & 0x7F) as u8, ((b >> 7) & 0x7F) as u8)
}

/// Translates one event body on `channel` (0–15).
pub fn translate(body: &EventBody, channel: u8) -> Translated<'_> {
    let ch = channel & 0x0F;
    let mut t = Translated::empty();
    match body {
        EventBody::Note {
            pitch,
            velocity,
            release_velocity: rel,
            attribute,
            per_note,
            ..
        } => {
            let p = pitch & 0x7F;
            t.push(
                When::Start,
                Midi1Msg::three(0x90 | ch, p, note_on_velocity(*velocity)),
            );
            t.push(
                When::End,
                Midi1Msg::three(0x80 | ch, p, release_velocity(*rel)),
            );
            t.drops.per_note = per_note.len();
            t.drops.attribute = attribute.is_some();
        }
        EventBody::Cc { index, value } => {
            t.push(
                When::Start,
                Midi1Msg::three(0xB0 | ch, index & 0x7F, value7(*value)),
            );
        }
        EventBody::PitchBend { value } => {
            let (lsb, msb) = bend14(*value);
            t.push(When::Start, Midi1Msg::three(0xE0 | ch, lsb, msb));
        }
        EventBody::ChannelPressure { value } => {
            t.push(When::Start, Midi1Msg::two(0xD0 | ch, value7(*value)));
        }
        EventBody::PolyPressure { pitch, value } => {
            t.push(
                When::Start,
                Midi1Msg::three(0xA0 | ch, pitch & 0x7F, value7(*value)),
            );
        }
        EventBody::Program { program, bank } => {
            if let Some((msb, lsb)) = bank {
                let (msb, lsb) = (msb & 0x7F, lsb & 0x7F);
                t.push_class(
                    When::Start,
                    Class::Patch,
                    Midi1Msg::three(0xB0 | ch, 0, msb),
                );
                t.push_class(
                    When::Start,
                    Class::Patch,
                    Midi1Msg::three(0xB0 | ch, 32, lsb),
                );
            }
            t.push(When::Start, Midi1Msg::two(0xC0 | ch, program & 0x7F));
        }
        EventBody::SysEx { data, eight_bit } => {
            if *eight_bit {
                t.drops.sysex8 = true;
            } else {
                t.sysex = Some(data);
            }
        }
        EventBody::Text { .. } | EventBody::Reference { .. } | EventBody::AudioRegion { .. } => {}
    }
    t
}
```

```rust
//! `core-engine`: compiles a core-model sequence into an immutable schedule
//! and plays it sample-accurately through platform backends, with a
//! real-time path that never blocks or allocates.
//!
//! Everything reachable from `render::Renderer::process` is marked
//! `// RT`; such code must not allocate, free, lock, do I/O or log.
#![deny(missing_docs)]
#![cfg_attr(
    not(test),
    deny(
        clippy::unwrap_used,
        clippy::expect_used,
        clippy::indexing_slicing,
        clippy::panic
    )
)]

pub mod backend;
pub mod error;
pub mod midi1;

pub use error::{BackendError, EngineError};
```

```rust
use core_engine::EngineError;
use core_engine::backend::MidiPortInfo;
use core_engine::schedule::TempoTable;
use core_engine::schedule::targets::{
    BuiltinRoute, TargetIdx, TargetKind, TargetTable, find_port, kinds_for,
};
use core_model::document::DestKind;
use core_model::tempo::{Bpm, TempoMap, TempoPoint};
use core_model::time::Tick;
use proptest::prelude::*;

const SR: f64 = 48_000.0;
const Q: i64 = 15_360;
const BAR: i64 = 4 * Q;

#[test]
fn tempo_table_is_exact_for_constant_tempo() {
    let tempo = TempoMap::constant(Bpm::whole(120)).unwrap();
    let t = TempoTable::build(&tempo, Tick::ZERO, SR);
    assert_eq!(t.tick_to_frame(Tick(Q)), 24_000);
    assert_eq!(t.frame_to_tick(24_000), Tick(Q));
    assert_eq!(t.frame_to_tick(10 * 96_000), Tick(40 * Q));
    assert_eq!(t.tick_to_frame(Tick(-5)), 0);
}

#[test]
fn ramp_knots_are_at_most_a_sixteenth_apart() {
    let tempo = TempoMap::new(vec![
        TempoPoint {
            tick: Tick(0),
            bpm: Bpm::whole(120),
            ramp: true,
        },
        TempoPoint {
            tick: Tick(4 * BAR),
            bpm: Bpm::whole(60),
            ramp: false,
        },
    ])
    .unwrap();
    let t = TempoTable::build(&tempo, Tick::ZERO, SR);
    let knots = t.knots();
    assert!(knots.len() > (4 * BAR / 960) as usize);
    for w in knots.windows(2) {
        assert!(w[1].tick - w[0].tick <= 960, "{:?}", w);
        assert!(w[1].frame > w[0].frame);
    }
}

#[test]
fn target_table_interns_and_caps_at_16() {
    let mut t = TargetTable::new();
    assert_eq!(t.intern(TargetKind::BuiltinSynth).unwrap(), TargetIdx(0));
    assert_eq!(
        t.intern(TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource).unwrap(),
        TargetIdx(1)
    );
    assert_eq!(t.intern(TargetKind::BuiltinSynth).unwrap(), TargetIdx(0));
    for i in 0..14 {
        let p = MidiPortInfo {
            name: format!("P{i}"),
            unique_id: None,
        };
        t.intern(TargetKind::MidiDestination(p)).unwrap();
    }
    assert_eq!(t.len(), 16);
    let p = MidiPortInfo {
        name: "one too many".into(),
        unique_id: None,
    };
    assert_eq!(
        t.intern(TargetKind::MidiDestination(p)),
        Err(EngineError::TooManyTargets)
    );
    assert_eq!(t.get(TargetIdx(1)), Some(&TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource));
    assert_eq!(t.iter().count(), 16);
}

#[test]
fn endpoint_refs_map_to_target_kinds() {
    let ports = vec![
        MidiPortInfo {
            name: "IAC Bus 1".into(),
            unique_id: Some(11),
        },
        MidiPortInfo {
            name: "Synth".into(),
            unique_id: Some(-5),
        },
    ];
    let ep = |r: &str| DestKind::MidiEndpoint {
        endpoint_ref: r.into(),
        group: 0,
        channel: 0,
    };
    assert_eq!(
        kinds_for(&ep("builtin:synth"), BuiltinRoute::Builtin, &ports),
        Ok(vec![TargetKind::BuiltinSynth])
    );
    assert_eq!(
        kinds_for(&ep("builtin:synth"), BuiltinRoute::Virtual, &ports),
        Ok(vec![TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource])
    );
    assert_eq!(
        kinds_for(&ep("builtin:synth"), BuiltinRoute::Both, &ports),
        Ok(vec![
            TargetKind::BuiltinSynth,
            TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource
        ])
    );
    assert_eq!(
        kinds_for(&ep("virtual:out"), BuiltinRoute::Both, &ports),
        Ok(vec![TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource])
    );
    assert_eq!(
        kinds_for(&ep("Synth"), BuiltinRoute::Builtin, &ports),
        Ok(vec![TargetKind::MidiDestination(ports[1].clone())])
    );
    assert!(
        kinds_for(&ep("Nope"), BuiltinRoute::Builtin, &ports)
            .unwrap_err()
            .contains("Nope")
    );
    let plugin = DestKind::PluginInstrument {
        plugin_ref: "au:x".into(),
    };
    assert!(
        kinds_for(&plugin, BuiltinRoute::Builtin, &ports)
            .unwrap_err()
            .contains("au:x")
    );
    assert_eq!(find_port("11", &ports), Some(&ports[0]));
    assert_eq!(find_port("-5", &ports), Some(&ports[1]));
    assert_eq!(find_port("12", &ports), None);
}

fn arb_tempo() -> impl Strategy<Value = TempoMap> {
    prop::collection::vec((1i64..4 * BAR, 5_000u32..=999_000, any::<bool>()), 0..6).prop_map(
        |steps| {
            let mut points = vec![];
            let mut tick = 0i64;
            let mut first = true;
            for (gap, bpm, ramp) in steps {
                if !first {
                    tick += gap;
                }
                first = false;
                points.push(TempoPoint {
                    tick: Tick(tick),
                    bpm: Bpm(bpm),
                    ramp,
                });
            }
            if points.is_empty() {
                points.push(TempoPoint {
                    tick: Tick(0),
                    bpm: Bpm(120_000),
                    ramp: false,
                });
            }
            TempoMap::new(points).unwrap()
        },
    )
}

proptest! {
    #![proptest_config(ProptestConfig::with_cases(64))]

    #[test]
    fn table_lookups_stay_within_one(tempo in arb_tempo(), probes in prop::collection::vec(0i64..16 * BAR, 1..40)) {
        let table = TempoTable::build(&tempo, Tick::ZERO, SR);
        for t in probes {
            let secs = tempo.tick_to_seconds(Tick(t));
            let frame_exact = secs * SR;
            let f = table.tick_to_frame(Tick(t));
            prop_assert!((f as f64 - frame_exact).abs() <= 1.0, "tick {} -> {} vs {}", t, f, frame_exact);
            let frame = frame_exact.round() as u64;
            let back = table.frame_to_tick(frame);
            let want = tempo.seconds_to_tick(frame as f64 / SR);
            prop_assert!((back.0 - want.0).abs() <= 1, "frame {} -> {:?} vs {:?}", frame, back, want);
        }
    }
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/schedule/mod.rs`:

```rust
//! Immutable playback schedules and their compiler.

pub mod targets;
pub mod tempo;

pub use targets::{BuiltinRoute, MAX_TARGETS, TargetIdx, TargetKind, TargetTable};
pub use tempo::TempoTable;

use crate::midi1::Midi1Msg;

/// A message on the wire: a MIDI 1.0 channel message or a SysEx stored in
/// the schedule's arena. A `Ump64` variant can be added for MIDI 2.0
/// destinations without touching sorting or scheduling.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum WireMsg {
    /// A MIDI 1.0 channel message.
    Midi1(Midi1Msg),
    /// `F0 … F7` bytes at `sysex[start..start + len]`.
    SysEx {
        /// Arena offset.
        start: u32,
        /// Length including `F0` and `F7`.
        len: u32,
    },
}

/// One scheduled message.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Entry {
    /// Frame from the sequence origin.
    pub frame: u64,
    /// Where it goes.
    pub target: TargetIdx,
    /// What is sent.
    pub msg: WireMsg,
}

/// A compiled sequence: everything the audio thread needs, immutable.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Schedule {
    /// Sample rate the frames were computed at.
    pub sample_rate: f64,
    /// Entries sorted by `(frame, class, source order)`.
    pub entries: Box<[Entry]>,
    /// SysEx arena.
    pub sysex: Box<[u8]>,
    /// Frame ↔ tick map.
    pub tempo: TempoTable,
    /// Frame of the sequence end.
    pub length_frames: u64,
    /// Default loop range: bar 1 (tick 0) to the end.
    pub loop_range: Option<(u64, u64)>,
    /// Per target, a bitmask of the channels the schedule uses.
    pub used_channels: [u16; MAX_TARGETS],
}

impl Schedule {
    /// The bytes of `msg`. // RT
    pub fn bytes<'a>(&'a self, msg: &'a WireMsg) -> &'a [u8] {
        match msg {
            WireMsg::Midi1(m) => m.as_slice(),
            WireMsg::SysEx { start, len } => {
                let s = *start as usize;
                let e = s.saturating_add(*len as usize);
                self.sysex.get(s..e).unwrap_or(&[])
            }
        }
    }

    /// Index of the first entry at or after `frame`. // RT
    pub fn cursor_at(&self, frame: u64) -> usize {
        self.entries.partition_point(|e| e.frame < frame)
    }
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/schedule/tempo.rs`:

```rust
//! `TempoTable`: a piecewise-linear frame ↔ tick map sampled from 2a's
//! `TempoMap::tick_to_seconds`, for real-time position lookups.

use core_model::tempo::TempoMap;
use core_model::time::{PPQ, Tick};

/// Largest knot spacing inside a ramp: one 1/16 note.
pub const RAMP_KNOT_TICKS: i64 = 960;
/// Inside ramps, knots are subdivided until linear interpolation is within
/// this many ticks (frame → tick) and frames (tick → frame) of 2a's
/// conversion.
const MAX_KNOT_ERROR: f64 = 0.25;

/// One knot: an exact `(frame, tick)` pair (frames are not rounded).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct Knot {
    /// Frames from the sequence origin.
    pub frame: f64,
    /// Absolute tick.
    pub tick: i64,
}

/// Frame ↔ tick map. Lookups are a binary search plus one interpolation,
/// with no allocation. // RT (lookups)
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct TempoTable {
    knots: Box<[Knot]>,
    /// Frames per tick before the first knot.
    head_slope: f64,
    /// Frames per tick after the last knot (the last tempo is constant).
    tail_slope: f64,
}

fn frames_per_tick(bpm: f64, sample_rate: f64) -> f64 {
    60.0 / (bpm.max(1.0) * PPQ as f64) * sample_rate
}

impl TempoTable {
    /// Samples `tempo` with the origin (frame 0) at tick `origin`.
    pub fn build(tempo: &TempoMap, origin: Tick, sample_rate: f64) -> TempoTable {
        let base = tempo.tick_to_seconds(origin);
        let frame_of = |t: i64| (tempo.tick_to_seconds(Tick(t)) - base) * sample_rate;
        let points = tempo.points();
        let mut ticks: Vec<i64> = vec![origin.0];
        for (i, p) in points.iter().enumerate() {
            if p.tick.0 > origin.0 {
                ticks.push(p.tick.0);
            }
            let next = points.get(i + 1);
            if let Some(q) = next.filter(|q| p.ramp && q.bpm != p.bpm) {
                let mut t = p.tick.0.max(origin.0);
                while t < q.tick.0 {
                    let end = (t + RAMP_KNOT_TICKS).min(q.tick.0);
                    subdivide(&frame_of, t, end, sample_rate, tempo, base, &mut ticks);
                    ticks.push(end);
                    t = end;
                }
            }
        }
        ticks.sort_unstable();
        ticks.dedup();
        let knots: Box<[Knot]> = ticks
            .iter()
            .map(|t| Knot {
                frame: frame_of(*t),
                tick: *t,
            })
            .collect();
        let first_bpm = points.front().map_or(120.0, |p| p.bpm.as_f64());
        let last_bpm = points.last().map_or(120.0, |p| p.bpm.as_f64());
        TempoTable {
            knots,
            head_slope: frames_per_tick(first_bpm, sample_rate),
            tail_slope: frames_per_tick(last_bpm, sample_rate),
        }
    }

    /// The knots, in order.
    pub fn knots(&self) -> &[Knot] {
        &self.knots
    }

    /// The tick at `frame`, rounded to the nearest tick. // RT
    pub fn frame_to_tick(&self, frame: u64) -> Tick {
        let f = frame as f64;
        let i = self.knots.partition_point(|k| k.frame <= f);
        let t = match (
            i.checked_sub(1).and_then(|j| self.knots.get(j)),
            self.knots.get(i),
        ) {
            (Some(a), Some(b)) => {
                let span = b.frame - a.frame;
                let x = if span > 0.0 {
                    (f - a.frame) / span
                } else {
                    0.0
                };
                a.tick as f64 + x * (b.tick - a.tick) as f64
            }
            (Some(a), None) => a.tick as f64 + (f - a.frame) / self.tail_slope,
            (None, Some(b)) => b.tick as f64 - (b.frame - f) / self.head_slope,
            (None, None) => 0.0,
        };
        Tick(t.round() as i64)
    }

    /// The frame at `tick`, rounded to the nearest frame (0 before the
    /// origin). // RT
    pub fn tick_to_frame(&self, tick: Tick) -> u64 {
        let t = tick.0;
        let i = self.knots.partition_point(|k| k.tick <= t);
        let f = match (
            i.checked_sub(1).and_then(|j| self.knots.get(j)),
            self.knots.get(i),
        ) {
            (Some(a), Some(b)) => {
                let span = (b.tick - a.tick) as f64;
                let x = if span > 0.0 {
                    (t - a.tick) as f64 / span
                } else {
                    0.0
                };
                a.frame + x * (b.frame - a.frame)
            }
            (Some(a), None) => a.frame + (t - a.tick) as f64 * self.tail_slope,
            (None, Some(b)) => b.frame - (b.tick - t) as f64 * self.head_slope,
            (None, None) => 0.0,
        };
        if f <= 0.0 { 0 } else { f.round() as u64 }
    }
}

/// Adds knots strictly inside `(a, b)` until linear interpolation between
/// neighbours is within [`MAX_KNOT_ERROR`] ticks and frames of 2a's
/// conversion in both directions.
fn subdivide(
    frame_of: &impl Fn(i64) -> f64,
    a: i64,
    b: i64,
    sample_rate: f64,
    tempo: &TempoMap,
    base: f64,
    out: &mut Vec<i64>,
) {
    if b - a < 2 {
        return;
    }
    let (fa, fb) = (frame_of(a), frame_of(b));
    // frame → tick at the frame midpoint.
    let fm = (fa + fb) / 2.0;
    let tick_linear = (a + b) as f64 / 2.0;
    let tick_exact = tick_at_seconds(tempo, base + fm / sample_rate);
    // tick → frame at the tick midpoint.
    let m = a + (b - a) / 2;
    let frame_linear = fa + (fb - fa) * (m - a) as f64 / (b - a) as f64;
    let frame_exact = frame_of(m);
    if (tick_exact - tick_linear).abs() <= MAX_KNOT_ERROR
        && (frame_exact - frame_linear).abs() <= MAX_KNOT_ERROR
    {
        return;
    }
    subdivide(frame_of, a, m, sample_rate, tempo, base, out);
    out.push(m);
    subdivide(frame_of, m, b, sample_rate, tempo, base, out);
}

/// Fractional tick at `secs`, refined from 2a's rounded inverse by one
/// Newton step on `tick_to_seconds`.
fn tick_at_seconds(tempo: &TempoMap, secs: f64) -> f64 {
    let t0 = tempo.seconds_to_tick(secs).0;
    let s0 = tempo.tick_to_seconds(Tick(t0));
    let s1 = tempo.tick_to_seconds(Tick(t0 + 1));
    let d = s1 - s0;
    if d > 0.0 {
        t0 as f64 + (secs - s0) / d
    } else {
        t0 as f64
    }
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/schedule/targets.rs`:

```rust
//! Playback targets: where scheduled events go, and how 2a destinations map
//! onto them.

use crate::backend::MidiPortInfo;
use crate::error::EngineError;
use core_model::document::DestKind;

/// Most targets one engine can drive.
pub const MAX_TARGETS: usize = 16;

/// Index into a [`TargetTable`].
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
pub struct TargetIdx(pub u8);

/// What a target is.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum TargetKind {
    /// The built-in synth (one instance, all 16 channels).
    BuiltinSynth,
    /// VizDAW's own CoreMIDI virtual source, "VizDAW Out".
    MidiVirtualSource,
    /// An existing MIDI destination.
    MidiDestination(MidiPortInfo),
}

/// Endpoint reference that selects the built-in synth.
pub const BUILTIN_SYNTH_REF: &str = "builtin:synth";
/// Endpoint reference that selects the virtual source.
pub const VIRTUAL_OUT_REF: &str = "virtual:out";

/// Where `builtin:synth` destinations play (CLI `--to`).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum BuiltinRoute {
    /// The built-in synth.
    #[default]
    Builtin,
    /// The virtual source instead.
    Virtual,
    /// Both.
    Both,
}

/// The targets of an engine, in creation order. Targets are only ever
/// appended, so an index stays valid for the engine's lifetime.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct TargetTable {
    kinds: Vec<TargetKind>,
}

impl TargetTable {
    /// An empty table.
    pub fn new() -> TargetTable {
        TargetTable::default()
    }

    /// The index of `kind`, if present.
    pub fn find(&self, kind: &TargetKind) -> Option<TargetIdx> {
        self.kinds
            .iter()
            .position(|k| k == kind)
            .and_then(|i| u8::try_from(i).ok())
            .map(TargetIdx)
    }

    /// Adds `kind` (or returns its existing index).
    pub fn intern(&mut self, kind: TargetKind) -> Result<TargetIdx, EngineError> {
        if let Some(i) = self.find(&kind) {
            return Ok(i);
        }
        if self.kinds.len() >= MAX_TARGETS {
            return Err(EngineError::TooManyTargets);
        }
        let i = u8::try_from(self.kinds.len()).map_err(|_| EngineError::TooManyTargets)?;
        self.kinds.push(kind);
        Ok(TargetIdx(i))
    }

    /// The kind at `idx`.
    pub fn get(&self, idx: TargetIdx) -> Option<&TargetKind> {
        self.kinds.get(usize::from(idx.0))
    }

    /// Number of targets.
    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.kinds.len()
    }

    /// True when there are no targets.
    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.kinds.is_empty()
    }

    /// `(index, kind)` pairs.
    pub fn iter(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (TargetIdx, &TargetKind)> + '_ {
        self.kinds
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .filter_map(|(i, k)| u8::try_from(i).ok().map(|i| (TargetIdx(i), k)))
    }
}

/// The target kinds a destination plays on (up to two, for
/// [`BuiltinRoute::Both`]), or why it cannot play.
pub fn kinds_for(
    kind: &DestKind,
    route: BuiltinRoute,
    ports: &[MidiPortInfo],
) -> Result<Vec<TargetKind>, String> {
    match kind {
        DestKind::PluginInstrument { plugin_ref } => Err(format!(
            "plugin instrument {plugin_ref:?} cannot play until plugin hosting exists"
        )),
        DestKind::MidiEndpoint { endpoint_ref, .. } => match endpoint_ref.as_str() {
            BUILTIN_SYNTH_REF => Ok(match route {
                BuiltinRoute::Builtin => vec![TargetKind::BuiltinSynth],
                BuiltinRoute::Virtual => vec![TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource],
                BuiltinRoute::Both => {
                    vec![TargetKind::BuiltinSynth, TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource]
                }
            }),
            VIRTUAL_OUT_REF => Ok(vec![TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource]),
            name => find_port(name, ports)
                .map(|p| vec![TargetKind::MidiDestination(p.clone())])
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("no MIDI destination named {name:?}")),
        },
    }
}

/// Finds a destination by display name, then by unique ID written as a
/// decimal number.
pub fn find_port<'a>(name: &str, ports: &'a [MidiPortInfo]) -> Option<&'a MidiPortInfo> {
    ports.iter().find(|p| p.name == name).or_else(|| {
        let id: i32 = name.parse().ok()?;
        ports.iter().find(|p| p.unique_id == Some(id))
    })
}
```

```rust
//! `core-engine`: compiles a core-model sequence into an immutable schedule
//! and plays it sample-accurately through platform backends, with a
//! real-time path that never blocks or allocates.
//!
//! Everything reachable from `render::Renderer::process` is marked
//! `// RT`; such code must not allocate, free, lock, do I/O or log.
#![deny(missing_docs)]
#![cfg_attr(
    not(test),
    deny(
        clippy::unwrap_used,
        clippy::expect_used,
        clippy::indexing_slicing,
        clippy::panic
    )
)]

pub mod backend;
pub mod error;
pub mod midi1;
pub mod schedule;

pub use error::{BackendError, EngineError};
```

### Task 4: Fixture builder and the demo project

**Goal:** A small command-driven `Builder` (feature `fixtures`) used by every later test, the CLI and 2c, and `demo_document()` with the content of the app-shell spec §6 (Plan Rulings 16–17).

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/core-engine/src/fixtures.rs`
- Modify: `crates/core-engine/src/lib.rs`
- Test: `crates/core-engine/tests/demo.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 2a `core_model::command::{apply, Command, Report, SequenceCmd::{New, SetTempo}, LaneCmd::{Add, Rename, SetDest}, EventCmd::Insert, ReferenceCmd::Insert, RoutingCmd::AddDestination, DrumCmd::{MarkDrumLane, AddRow}}`, `core_model::ids::AnyId` (to read created IDs from `Report.created`), `core_model::document::{DestKind, Patch}`, `core_model::event::{EventBody, EventSpec, LoopCount}`, `core_model::tempo::{TempoMap, TempoPoint, Bpm}`; queries `use_count`, `unused_sequences`, `seq_length`, `drum_view`, `rules::validate` in the test.
- Produces (all behind feature `fixtures`):
  - `fixtures::{Q: i64 = PPQ, BAR: i64 = 4 * PPQ}`, `fixtures::cc32(v7: u8) -> u32`.

```rust
use core_engine::fixtures::{BAR, demo_document};
use core_model::document::{DestKind, Document};
use core_model::ids::SeqId;
use core_model::query::{drum_view, seq_length, unused_sequences, use_count};
use core_model::rules::validate;
use core_model::time::Tick;

fn find_sequence(doc: &Document, name: &str) -> Option<SeqId> {
    doc.sequences()
        .values()
        .find(|s| s.name == name)
        .map(|s| s.id)
}

#[test]

    let doc = demo_document().unwrap();
    assert!(validate(&doc).is_empty(), "{:?}", validate(&doc));
    let beat = find_sequence(&doc, "Beat").unwrap();
    let groove = find_sequence(&doc, "Groove").unwrap();
    let song = find_sequence(&doc, "Song").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(use_count(&doc, beat), 1);
    assert_eq!(use_count(&doc, groove), 2);
    assert_eq!(unused_sequences(&doc), vec![song]);
    assert_eq!(seq_length(&doc, beat).unwrap(), Tick(BAR));
    assert_eq!(seq_length(&doc, groove).unwrap(), Tick(4 * BAR));
    assert_eq!(seq_length(&doc, song).unwrap(), Tick(12 * BAR));

    let channels: Vec<(String, u8)> = doc
        .instruments()
        .values()
        .map(|d| match &d.kind {
            DestKind::MidiEndpoint {
                endpoint_ref,
                channel,
                ..
            } => {
                assert_eq!(endpoint_ref, "builtin:synth");
                (d.name.clone(), *channel)
            }

            ("Built-in Bass".to_string(), 1),
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/fixtures.rs`:

```rust
//! Test and demo documents built through core-model commands (cargo feature
//! `fixtures`). Shared by engine tests, `vizdaw-play` and the app (2c).

use core_model::command::{
    Command, DrumCmd, EventCmd, LaneCmd, ReferenceCmd, Report, RoutingCmd, SequenceCmd, apply,
};
use core_model::document::{DestKind, Document, Patch};
use core_model::event::{EventBody, EventSpec, LoopCount};
use core_model::ids::{AnyId, DestId, EventId, LaneId, SeqId};
use core_model::tempo::{Bpm, TempoMap, TempoPoint};
use core_model::time::{PPQ, Tick};
use core_model::{ModelError, Result};

/// One quarter note.
pub const Q: i64 = PPQ;
/// One 4/4 bar.
pub const BAR: i64 = 4 * PPQ;

/// Builds documents with commands, returning the IDs they create.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Builder {
    doc: Document,
}

fn created<T>(r: &Report, pick: impl Fn(&AnyId) -> Option<T>) -> Result<T> {
    r.created
        .iter()
        .find_map(pick)
        .ok_or_else(|| ModelError::invalid("command created nothing"))
}

impl Builder {
    /// An empty document.
    pub fn new() -> Builder {
        Builder::default()
    }

    /// The document so far.
    pub fn doc(&self) -> &Document {
        &self.doc
    }

    /// The finished document.
    pub fn finish(self) -> Document {
        self.doc
    }

    /// Applies any command.
    pub fn run(&mut self, cmd: Command) -> Result<Report> {
        let a = apply(&self.doc, &cmd)?;
        self.doc = a.doc;
        Ok(a.report)
    }

    /// Adds a destination.
    pub fn dest_kind(
        &mut self,
        name: &str,
        kind: DestKind,
        patch: Option<Patch>,
    ) -> Result<DestId> {
        let r = self.run(Command::Routing(RoutingCmd::AddDestination {
            name: name.into(),
            kind,
            default_patch: patch,
        }))?;
        created(&r, |id| match id {
            AnyId::Dest(d) => Some(*d),
            _ => None,
        })
    }

    /// Adds a MIDI-endpoint destination on group 0.
    pub fn dest(&mut self, name: &str, endpoint_ref: &str, channel: u8) -> Result<DestId> {
        self.dest_kind(
            name,
            DestKind::MidiEndpoint {
                endpoint_ref: endpoint_ref.into(),
                group: 0,
                channel,
            },
            None,
        )
    }

    /// Adds a sequence whose first lane is named `lane` and plays on `dest`.
    pub fn sequence(
        &mut self,
        name: &str,
        lane: &str,
        dest: Option<DestId>,
    ) -> Result<(SeqId, LaneId)> {
        let r = self.run(Command::Sequence(SequenceCmd::New { name: name.into() }))?;
        let seq = created(&r, |id| match id {
            AnyId::Seq(s) => Some(*s),
            _ => None,
        })?;
        let first = created(&r, |id| match id {
            AnyId::Lane(l) => Some(*l),
            _ => None,
        })?;
        self.run(Command::Lane(LaneCmd::Rename {
            seq,
            lane: first,
            name: lane.into(),
        }))?;
        self.run(Command::Lane(LaneCmd::SetDest {
            seq,
            lane: first,
            dest,
        }))?;
        Ok((seq, first))
    }

    /// Adds a lane playing on `dest`.
    pub fn lane(&mut self, seq: SeqId, name: &str, dest: Option<DestId>) -> Result<LaneId> {
        let r = self.run(Command::Lane(LaneCmd::Add {
            seq,
            name: name.into(),
            index: None,
        }))?;
        let lane = created(&r, |id| match id {
            AnyId::Lane(l) => Some(*l),
            _ => None,
        })?;
        self.run(Command::Lane(LaneCmd::SetDest { seq, lane, dest }))?;
        Ok(lane)
    }

    /// Inserts events into a lane's active take.
    pub fn events(
        &mut self,
        seq: SeqId,
        lane: LaneId,
        events: Vec<EventSpec>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<EventId>> {
        let r = self.run(Command::Event(EventCmd::Insert { seq, lane, events }))?;
        Ok(r.created
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|id| match id {
                AnyId::Event(e) => Some(*e),
                _ => None,
            })
            .collect())
    }

    /// Inserts notes given as `(tick, pitch, velocity16, duration)`.
    pub fn notes(
        &mut self,
        seq: SeqId,
        lane: LaneId,
        notes: &[(i64, u8, u16, i64)],
    ) -> Result<Vec<EventId>> {
        let specs = notes
            .iter()
            .map(|(t, p, v, d)| EventSpec::new(Tick(*t), EventBody::note(*p, *v, Tick(*d))))
            .collect();
        self.events(seq, lane, specs)
    }

    /// Inserts one event body at `tick`.
    pub fn event(
        &mut self,
        seq: SeqId,
        lane: LaneId,
        tick: i64,
        body: EventBody,
    ) -> Result<EventId> {
        let ids = self.events(seq, lane, vec![EventSpec::new(Tick(tick), body)])?;
        ids.first()
            .copied()
            .ok_or_else(|| ModelError::invalid("no event created"))
    }

    /// Inserts a reference to `target`.
    pub fn reference(
        &mut self,
        seq: SeqId,
        lane: LaneId,
        tick: i64,
        target: SeqId,
        loop_count: u32,
        transpose: i8,
    ) -> Result<EventId> {
        let r = self.run(Command::Reference(ReferenceCmd::Insert {
            seq,
            lane,
            tick: Tick(tick),
            target,
            length: None,
            loop_count: LoopCount::Count(loop_count),
            transpose,
        }))?;
        created(&r, |id| match id {
            AnyId::Event(e) => Some(*e),
            _ => None,
        })
    }

    /// Replaces a sequence's tempo map; points are `(tick, whole BPM, ramp)`.
    pub fn tempo(&mut self, seq: SeqId, points: &[(i64, u32, bool)]) -> Result<()> {
        let tempo = TempoMap::new(
            points
                .iter()
                .map(|(t, b, r)| TempoPoint {
                    tick: Tick(*t),
                    bpm: Bpm::whole(*b),
                    ramp: *r,
                })
                .collect(),
        )?;
        self.run(Command::Sequence(SequenceCmd::SetTempo { seq, tempo }))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Makes a lane a drum lane and adds `(name, note)` rows.
    pub fn drum_rows(&mut self, seq: SeqId, lane: LaneId, rows: &[(&str, u8)]) -> Result<()> {
        self.run(Command::Drum(DrumCmd::MarkDrumLane {
            seq,
            lane,
            drum: true,
        }))?;
        for (name, note) in rows {
            self.run(Command::Drum(DrumCmd::AddRow {
                seq,
                lane,
                name: (*name).into(),
                note: *note,
                dest: None,
            }))?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// A 7-bit value as a 32-bit MIDI 2.0 controller value.
pub fn cc32(v7: u8) -> u32 {
    u32::from(v7 & 0x7F) << 25
}

/// The demo project (2c spec §6): Song → Groove → Beat on the built-in
/// synth, 12 bars at 120 BPM with a ramp to 90 BPM over the last 2 bars.
pub fn demo_document() -> Result<Document> {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let piano = b.dest("Built-in Piano", "builtin:synth", 0)?;
    let bass = b.dest("Built-in Bass", "builtin:synth", 1)?;
    let drums = b.dest_kind(
        "Built-in Drums",
        DestKind::MidiEndpoint {
            endpoint_ref: "builtin:synth".into(),
            group: 0,
            channel: 9,
        },
        Some(Patch {
            program: 0,
            bank: None,
        }),
    )?;

    // Beat: one bar of kick, snare and closed hats on eighths.
    let (beat, kit) = b.sequence("Beat", "Drums", Some(drums))?;
    b.drum_rows(
        beat,
        kit,
        &[("Kick", 36), ("Snare", 38), ("Closed Hat", 42)],
    )?;
    let mut hits: Vec<(i64, u8, u16, i64)> = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..8 {
        let accent = if i % 2 == 0 { 0x9000 } else { 0x6000 };
        hits.push((i * Q / 2, 42, accent, Q / 4));
    }
    hits.extend([
        (0, 36, 0xC000, Q / 4),
        (2 * Q, 36, 0xC000, Q / 4),
        (Q, 38, 0xB000, Q / 4),
        (3 * Q, 38, 0xB000, Q / 4),
    ]);
    b.notes(beat, kit, &hits)?;

    // Groove: 4 bars of Beat plus a bass line (Fingered Bass).
    let (groove, groove_drums) = b.sequence("Groove", "Drums", Some(drums))?;
    b.reference(groove, groove_drums, 0, beat, 4, 0)?;
    let bass_lane = b.lane(groove, "Bass", Some(bass))?;
    b.event(
        groove,
        bass_lane,
        0,
        EventBody::Program {
            program: 33,
            bank: None,
        },
    )?;
    let mut line: Vec<(i64, u8, u16, i64)> = Vec::new();
    for (bar, root) in [36u8, 33, 29, 31].into_iter().enumerate() {
        let start = bar as i64 * BAR;
        for (beat_no, interval) in [0u8, 12, 7, 12].into_iter().enumerate() {
            line.push((
                start + beat_no as i64 * Q,
                root + interval,
                0xA000,
                Q * 9 / 10,
            ));
        }
    }
    b.notes(groove, bass_lane, &line)?;

    // Song: Groove twice (the second use a fourth up), chords over 12 bars,
    // ritardando over the last 2 bars.
    let (song, song_groove) = b.sequence("Song", "Groove", None)?;
    b.reference(song, song_groove, 0, groove, 1, 0)?;
    b.reference(song, song_groove, 4 * BAR, groove, 1, 5)?;
    let chords = b.lane(song, "Chords", Some(piano))?;
    b.event(
        song,
        chords,
        0,
        EventBody::Program {
            program: 0,
            bank: None,
        },
    )?;
    b.event(
        song,
        chords,
        0,
        EventBody::Cc {
            index: 7,
            value: cc32(100),
        },
    )?;
    let shapes: [[u8; 3]; 4] = [[60, 64, 67], [57, 60, 64], [53, 57, 60], [55, 59, 62]];
    let mut voicings: Vec<(i64, u8, u16, i64)> = Vec::new();
    for bar in 0..12i64 {
        let shift: u8 = if (4..8).contains(&bar) { 5 } else { 0 };
        let idx = usize::try_from(bar % 4).unwrap_or(0);
        let Some(shape) = shapes.get(idx) else {
            continue;
        };
        for p in shape {
            voicings.push((bar * BAR, p + shift, 0x7000, BAR - Q / 8));
        }
    }
    b.notes(song, chords, &voicings)?;
    b.event(
        song,
        chords,
        11 * BAR + 2 * Q,
        EventBody::PitchBend { value: 0x9000_0000 },
    )?;
    b.event(
        song,
        chords,
        11 * BAR + 3 * Q,
        EventBody::PitchBend { value: 0x8000_0000 },
    )?;
    b.tempo(
        song,
        &[
            (0, 120, false),
            (10 * BAR, 120, true),
            (12 * BAR, 90, false),
        ],
    )?;
    Ok(b.finish())
}
```

```rust
//! `core-engine`: compiles a core-model sequence into an immutable schedule
//! and plays it sample-accurately through platform backends, with a
//! real-time path that never blocks or allocates.
//!
//! Everything reachable from `render::Renderer::process` is marked
//! `// RT`; such code must not allocate, free, lock, do I/O or log.
#![deny(missing_docs)]
#![cfg_attr(
    not(test),
    deny(
        clippy::unwrap_used,
        clippy::expect_used,
        clippy::indexing_slicing,
        clippy::panic
    )
)]

pub mod backend;
pub mod error;
#[cfg(feature = "fixtures")]
pub mod fixtures;
pub mod midi1;
pub mod schedule;

pub use error::{BackendError, EngineError};
```
