### Task 5: The schedule compiler

**Goal:** `compile()` (spec §4): flatten → route through a resolver → frames from 2a's `tick_to_seconds` relative to the origin → translate → default patches (Plan Ruling 4) → sort by `(frame, class, source order)` → `Schedule` + `CompileReport` (unroutable events with paths, drops, group folds, skipped audio, warnings, compile time).

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/core-engine/src/schedule/compile.rs`
- Modify: `crates/core-engine/src/schedule/mod.rs`
- Test: `crates/core-engine/tests/schedule.rs`
- Test: `crates/core-engine/tests/tempo.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `core_model::query::{flatten, seq_length, PathStep, ResolvedEvent}` (2a Tasks 10–11: `flatten(doc, seq, None, &ViewFilter::default())` yields events sorted by tick with `tick`, `dest: Option<DestId>`, `group_channel: Option<(u8, u8)>`, `body`, `path`), `core_model::selection::ViewFilter`, `Document::{sequence, instruments}`, `Sequence::{tempo, start_offset}`; `midi1::translate` (Task 2); `targets::kinds_for`, `TempoTable::build` (Task 3); `fixtures::Builder` (Task 4, tests).
- Produces:
  - `schedule::compile::CompileOptions { sample_rate: f64, route: BuiltinRoute, ports: Vec<MidiPortInfo> }` with `CompileOptions::new(sample_rate)`.
  - `schedule::compile::Unroutable { path: Vec<PathStep>, reason: String }`.
  - `schedule::compile::CompileReport { events, entries, unroutable: Vec<Unroutable>, dropped_per_note, dropped_note_attributes, dropped_sysex8, group_folded, skipped_audio: usize…, warnings: Vec<String>, compile_time: Duration }` (`Default + Clone + PartialEq`).
  - `schedule::compile::Resolver<'a> = dyn FnMut(&TargetKind) -> Result<Result<TargetIdx, String>, EngineError> + 'a` — `Ok(Ok(i))` plays, `Ok(Err(why))` makes the destination's events unroutable, `Err` aborts.
  - `schedule::compile::compile(doc: &Document, seq: SeqId, opts: &CompileOptions, resolve: &mut Resolver<'_>) -> Result<(Schedule, CompileReport), EngineError>`. All four re-exported from `schedule`.
  - Semantics later tasks rely on: `length_frames = frame(seq_length)`; `loop_range = Some((frame(tick 0), length_frames))`; frame 0 is tick `-start_offset`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `crates/core-engine/tests/schedule.rs`:

```rust
use core_engine::EngineError;
use core_engine::backend::MidiPortInfo;
use core_engine::fixtures::{BAR, Builder, Q};
use core_engine::midi1::Midi1Msg;
use core_engine::schedule::{
    BuiltinRoute, CompileOptions, CompileReport, Entry, Schedule, TargetIdx, TargetKind,
    TargetTable, WireMsg, compile,
};
use core_model::command::{Command, SequenceCmd};
use core_model::document::{DestKind, Document, Patch};
use core_model::event::EventBody;
use core_model::ids::SeqId;
use core_model::time::Tick;
use std::sync::Arc;

fn compile_all(
    doc: &Document,
    seq: SeqId,
    opts: &CompileOptions,
) -> (Schedule, CompileReport, TargetTable) {
    let mut table = TargetTable::new();
    let (s, r) = compile(doc, seq, opts, &mut |k: &TargetKind| {
        table.intern(k.clone()).map(Ok)
    })
    .unwrap();
    (s, r, table)
}

fn bytes(s: &Schedule) -> Vec<(u64, u8, Vec<u8>)> {
    s.entries
        .iter()
        .map(|e| (e.frame, e.target.0, s.bytes(&e.msg).to_vec()))
        .collect()
}

fn one_bar() -> (Document, SeqId) {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("Bar", "Notes", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    b.notes(
        seq,
        lane,
        &[
            (0, 60, 0x8000, Q / 2),
            (Q, 62, 0x8000, Q / 2),
            (2 * Q, 64, 0x8000, Q / 2),
            (3 * Q, 65, 0x8000, Q / 2),
        ],
    )
    .unwrap();
    (b.finish(), seq)
}

#[test]
fn entry_is_24_bytes() {
    assert_eq!(std::mem::size_of::<Entry>(), 24);
}

#[test]
fn quarter_notes_at_120_bpm_land_on_exact_frames() {
    let (doc, seq) = one_bar();
    let (s, r, table) = compile_all(&doc, seq, &CompileOptions::new(48_000.0));
    let ons: Vec<u64> = s
        .entries
        .iter()
        .filter(|e| s.bytes(&e.msg)[0] & 0xF0 == 0x90)
        .map(|e| e.frame)
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(ons, vec![0, 24_000, 48_000, 72_000]);
    assert_eq!(s.length_frames, 96_000);
    assert_eq!(s.loop_range, Some((0, 96_000)));
    assert_eq!(r.events, 4);
    assert_eq!(r.entries, 8);
    assert!(r.unroutable.is_empty() && r.warnings.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(table.get(TargetIdx(0)), Some(&TargetKind::BuiltinSynth));
    assert_eq!(s.used_channels[0], 1);
}

#[test]
fn same_frame_order_is_off_patch_control_on() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("S", "L", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    // A note ending exactly where the same pitch starts again, plus a
    // controller and a program change on that frame, inserted in the
    // "wrong" order.
    b.notes(seq, lane, &[(Q, 60, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    b.event(
        seq,
        lane,
        2 * Q,
        EventBody::Cc {
            index: 7,
            value: 1 << 25,
        },
    )
    .unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, lane, &[(2 * Q, 60, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    b.event(
        seq,
        lane,
        2 * Q,
        EventBody::Program {
            program: 5,
            bank: Some((0, 1)),
        },
    )
    .unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let (s, _, _) = compile_all(&doc, seq, &CompileOptions::new(48_000.0));
    let at: Vec<Vec<u8>> = bytes(&s)
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|(f, _, _)| *f == 48_000)
        .map(|(_, _, b)| b)
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        at,
        vec![
            vec![0x80, 60, 0],
            vec![0xB0, 0, 0],
            vec![0xB0, 32, 1],
            vec![0xC0, 5],
            vec![0xB0, 7, 1],
            vec![0x90, 60, 64],
        ]
    );
}

#[test]
fn default_patch_plays_first_on_frame_zero() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let d = b
        .dest_kind(
            "Strings",
            DestKind::MidiEndpoint {
                endpoint_ref: "builtin:synth".into(),
                group: 0,
                channel: 2,
            },
            Some(Patch {
                program: 48,
                bank: Some((121, 0)),
            }),
        )
        .unwrap();
    let unused = b
        .dest_kind(
            "Unused",
            DestKind::MidiEndpoint {
                endpoint_ref: "builtin:synth".into(),
                group: 0,
                channel: 3,
            },
            Some(Patch {
                program: 1,
                bank: None,
            }),
        )
        .unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("S", "L", Some(d)).unwrap();
    assert_ne!(unused, d);
    b.notes(seq, lane, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    b.event(
        seq,
        lane,
        0,
        EventBody::Program {
            program: 49,
            bank: None,
        },
    )
    .unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let (s, _, _) = compile_all(&doc, seq, &CompileOptions::new(48_000.0));
    let first: Vec<Vec<u8>> = bytes(&s).into_iter().take(5).map(|(_, _, b)| b).collect();
    assert_eq!(
        first,
        vec![
            vec![0xB2, 0, 121],
            vec![0xB2, 32, 0],
            vec![0xC2, 48],
            vec![0xC2, 49],
            vec![0x92, 60, 64]
        ]
    );
}

#[test]
fn unroutable_events_are_reported_not_played() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let plugin = b
        .dest_kind(
            "Pad",
            DestKind::PluginInstrument {
                plugin_ref: "au:pad".into(),
            },
            None,
        )
        .unwrap();
    let missing = b.dest("Rack", "Missing Synth", 0).unwrap();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (seq, pad_lane) = b.sequence("S", "Pad", Some(plugin)).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, pad_lane, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    let rack_lane = b.lane(seq, "Rack", Some(missing)).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, rack_lane, &[(0, 62, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    let none_lane = b.lane(seq, "Nowhere", None).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, none_lane, &[(0, 64, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    let ok_lane = b.lane(seq, "Ok", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, ok_lane, &[(0, 67, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let (s, r, _) = compile_all(&doc, seq, &CompileOptions::new(48_000.0));
    assert_eq!(r.events, 1);
    assert_eq!(s.entries.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(r.unroutable.len(), 3);
    let reasons: Vec<&str> = r.unroutable.iter().map(|u| u.reason.as_str()).collect();
    assert!(
        reasons
            .iter()
            .any(|w| w.contains("Pad") && w.contains("au:pad")),
        "{reasons:?}"
    );
    assert!(
        reasons.iter().any(|w| w.contains("Missing Synth")),
        "{reasons:?}"
    );
    assert!(reasons.contains(&"no destination"), "{reasons:?}");
    assert!(r.unroutable.iter().all(|u| u.path.len() == 1));
    assert_eq!(r.warnings.len(), 3, "{:?}", r.warnings);
}

#[test]
fn named_destination_routes_by_name_or_unique_id() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let by_name = b.dest("A", "IAC Bus 1", 0).unwrap();
    let by_id = b.dest("B", "-12345", 1).unwrap();
    let (seq, l1) = b.sequence("S", "A", Some(by_name)).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, l1, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    let l2 = b.lane(seq, "B", Some(by_id)).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, l2, &[(0, 61, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let ports = vec![
        MidiPortInfo {
            name: "IAC Bus 1".into(),
            unique_id: Some(7),
        },
        MidiPortInfo {
            name: "Other".into(),
            unique_id: Some(-12345),
        },
    ];
    let opts = CompileOptions {
        sample_rate: 48_000.0,
        route: BuiltinRoute::Builtin,
        ports: ports.clone(),
    };
    let (s, r, table) = compile_all(&doc, seq, &opts);
    assert!(r.unroutable.is_empty(), "{:?}", r.unroutable);
    assert_eq!(table.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(
        table.get(TargetIdx(0)),
        Some(&TargetKind::MidiDestination(ports[0].clone()))
    );
    assert_eq!(
        table.get(TargetIdx(1)),
        Some(&TargetKind::MidiDestination(ports[1].clone()))
    );
    assert_eq!(s.entries.len(), 4);
}

#[test]
fn route_both_duplicates_builtin_events() {
    let (doc, seq) = one_bar();
    let opts = CompileOptions {
        sample_rate: 48_000.0,
        route: BuiltinRoute::Both,
        ports: vec![],
    };
    let (s, _, table) = compile_all(&doc, seq, &opts);
    assert_eq!(
        table.get(TargetIdx(1)),
        Some(&TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource)
    );
    assert_eq!(s.entries.len(), 16);
    let virt = CompileOptions {
        route: BuiltinRoute::Virtual,
        ..opts
    };
    let (_, _, table) = compile_all(&doc, seq, &virt);
    assert_eq!(
        table.get(TargetIdx(0)),
        Some(&TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource)
    );
    assert_eq!(table.len(), 1);
}

#[test]
fn groups_fold_with_a_warning_and_sysex_goes_to_the_arena() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let g2 = b
        .dest_kind(
            "G2",
            DestKind::MidiEndpoint {
                endpoint_ref: "builtin:synth".into(),
                group: 2,
                channel: 4,
            },
            None,
        )
        .unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("S", "L", Some(g2)).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, lane, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    b.event(
        seq,
        lane,
        Q,
        EventBody::SysEx {
            data: Arc::from(&[0x7E, 0x7F, 0x09, 0x01][..]),
            eight_bit: false,
        },
    )
    .unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let (s, r, _) = compile_all(&doc, seq, &CompileOptions::new(48_000.0));
    assert_eq!(r.group_folded, 2);
    assert!(r.warnings.iter().any(|w| w.contains("groups other than 0")));
    let sysex: Vec<Vec<u8>> = s
        .entries
        .iter()
        .filter(|e| matches!(e.msg, WireMsg::SysEx { .. }))
        .map(|e| s.bytes(&e.msg).to_vec())
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(sysex, vec![vec![0xF0, 0x7E, 0x7F, 0x09, 0x01, 0xF7]]);
    assert_eq!(
        s.entries[0].msg,
        WireMsg::Midi1(Midi1Msg::three(0x94, 60, 64))
    );
    assert_eq!(s.used_channels[0], 1 << 4);
}

#[test]
fn pickup_puts_frame_zero_at_bar_zero() {
    let (doc, seq) = one_bar();
    let doc = core_model::command::apply(
        &doc,
        &Command::Sequence(SequenceCmd::SetStartOffset {
            seq,
            offset: Tick(Q),
        }),
    )
    .unwrap()
    .doc;
    let (s, _, _) = compile_all(&doc, seq, &CompileOptions::new(48_000.0));
    // One quarter (24000 frames) of pickup comes before tick 0.
    assert_eq!(s.entries[0].frame, 24_000);
    assert_eq!(s.loop_range, Some((24_000, 120_000)));
    assert_eq!(s.tempo.tick_to_frame(Tick(0)), 24_000);
    assert_eq!(s.tempo.frame_to_tick(0), Tick(-Q));
}

#[test]
fn too_many_targets_is_an_error() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let (seq, _) = b.sequence("S", "L", None).unwrap();
    let mut ports = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..17 {
        let name = format!("Port {i}");
        let d = b.dest(&name, &name, 0).unwrap();
        let l = b.lane(seq, &name, Some(d)).unwrap();
        b.notes(seq, l, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
        ports.push(MidiPortInfo {
            name,
            unique_id: None,
        });
    }
    let doc = b.finish();
    let opts = CompileOptions {
        sample_rate: 48_000.0,
        route: BuiltinRoute::Builtin,
        ports,
    };
    let mut table = TargetTable::new();
    let err = compile(&doc, seq, &opts, &mut |k: &TargetKind| {
        table.intern(k.clone()).map(Ok)
    })
    .unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(err, EngineError::TooManyTargets);
}

#[test]
fn unknown_sequence_is_a_model_error() {
    let (doc, _) = one_bar();
    let mut table = TargetTable::new();
    let err = compile(
        &doc,
        SeqId(999),
        &CompileOptions::new(48_000.0),
        &mut |k: &TargetKind| table.intern(k.clone()).map(Ok),
    )
    .unwrap_err();
    assert!(matches!(err, EngineError::Model(_)));
}

#[test]
fn length_covers_whole_bars() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("S", "L", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, lane, &[(BAR + Q, 60, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let (s, _, _) = compile_all(&doc, seq, &CompileOptions::new(44_100.0));
    assert_eq!(s.length_frames, 2 * 88_200);
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/tests/tempo.rs`:

```rust
use core_engine::fixtures::{BAR, Builder, Q};
use core_engine::schedule::{CompileOptions, Schedule, TargetKind, TargetTable, compile};
use core_model::document::Document;
use core_model::ids::SeqId;
use core_model::tempo::{Bpm, TempoMap, TempoPoint};
use core_model::time::{PPQ, Tick};
use proptest::prelude::*;

const SR: f64 = 48_000.0;

fn compile_seq(doc: &Document, seq: SeqId) -> Schedule {
    let mut table = TargetTable::new();
    compile(doc, seq, &CompileOptions::new(SR), &mut |k: &TargetKind| {
        table.intern(k.clone()).map(Ok)
    })
    .unwrap()
    .0
}

/// Note-on frames, in order.
fn on_frames(s: &Schedule) -> Vec<u64> {
    s.entries
        .iter()
        .filter(|e| s.bytes(&e.msg)[0] & 0xF0 == 0x90)
        .map(|e| e.frame)
        .collect()
}

/// Closed form for a ramp linear in BPM per tick from `b0` at tick 0 to
/// `b1` at tick `len`: t(x) = 60 / (PPQ·k) · ln(1 + k·x / b0).
fn ramp_seconds(x: f64, len: f64, b0: f64, b1: f64) -> f64 {
    let k = (b1 - b0) / len;
    60.0 / (PPQ as f64 * k) * (1.0 + k * x / b0).ln()
}

#[test]
fn linear_ramp_matches_2a_and_closed_form() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("Ramp", "L", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    let ticks: Vec<i64> = (0..32).map(|i| i * Q / 2).collect();
    let notes: Vec<(i64, u8, u16, i64)> = ticks.iter().map(|t| (*t, 60, 0x8000, Q / 4)).collect();
    b.notes(seq, lane, &notes).unwrap();
    b.tempo(seq, &[(0, 120, true), (4 * BAR, 60, false)])
        .unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let s = compile_seq(&doc, seq);
    let tempo = &doc.sequence(seq).unwrap().tempo;
    let frames = on_frames(&s);
    assert_eq!(frames.len(), ticks.len());
    for (t, f) in ticks.iter().zip(&frames) {
        let via_2a = (tempo.tick_to_seconds(Tick(*t)) * SR).round() as u64;
        assert_eq!(*f, via_2a, "tick {t}");
        let closed = ramp_seconds(*t as f64, (4 * BAR) as f64, 120.0, 60.0) * SR;
        assert!(
            (*f as f64 - closed).abs() <= 1.0,
            "tick {t}: {f} vs {closed}"
        );
    }
    // The ramp takes ln(2)·16 beats·0.5 s/beat·2 = 16·ln 2 seconds.
    let end = s.tempo.tick_to_frame(Tick(4 * BAR));
    let expected = 16.0 * std::f64::consts::LN_2 * SR;
    assert!((end as f64 - expected).abs() <= 1.0, "{end} vs {expected}");
}

#[test]
fn nested_sequence_uses_the_parents_tempo() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (inner, il) = b.sequence("Inner", "L", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    b.notes(inner, il, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q / 2), (Q, 62, 0x8000, Q / 2)])
        .unwrap();
    b.tempo(inner, &[(0, 60, false)]).unwrap();
    let (outer, ol) = b.sequence("Outer", "Refs", None).unwrap();
    b.reference(outer, ol, BAR, inner, 1, 0).unwrap();
    b.tempo(outer, &[(0, 120, false)]).unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let s = compile_seq(&doc, outer);
    // Bar 2 at 120 BPM is 2 s; the inner quarter is 0.5 s at the parent's
    // tempo, not 1 s at the inner 60 BPM.
    assert_eq!(on_frames(&s), vec![96_000, 120_000]);
}

fn arb_tempo() -> impl Strategy<Value = TempoMap> {
    prop::collection::vec((1i64..4 * BAR, 5_000u32..=999_000, any::<bool>()), 0..6).prop_map(
        |steps| {
            let mut points = vec![];
            let mut tick = 0i64;
            let mut first = true;
            for (gap, bpm, ramp) in steps {
                if !first {
                    tick += gap;
                }
                first = false;
                points.push(TempoPoint {
                    tick: Tick(tick),
                    bpm: Bpm(bpm),
                    ramp,
                });
            }
            if points.is_empty() {
                points.push(TempoPoint {
                    tick: Tick(0),
                    bpm: Bpm(120_000),
                    ramp: false,
                });
            }
            TempoMap::new(points).unwrap()
        },
    )
}

proptest! {
    #![proptest_config(ProptestConfig::with_cases(64))]

    #[test]
    fn compiled_frames_match_2a(tempo in arb_tempo(), ticks in prop::collection::vec(0i64..16 * BAR, 1..40)) {
        let mut b = Builder::new();
        let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
        let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("P", "L", Some(synth)).unwrap();
        let notes: Vec<(i64, u8, u16, i64)> = ticks.iter().map(|t| (*t, 60, 0x8000, 1)).collect();
        b.notes(seq, lane, &notes).unwrap();
        b.run(core_model::command::Command::Sequence(core_model::command::SequenceCmd::SetTempo {
            seq,
            tempo: tempo.clone(),
        }))
        .unwrap();
        let doc = b.finish();
        let s = compile_seq(&doc, seq);
        let mut want: Vec<u64> = ticks.iter().map(|t| (tempo.tick_to_seconds(Tick(*t)) * SR).round() as u64).collect();
        want.sort_unstable();
        let got = on_frames(&s);
        prop_assert_eq!(got.len(), want.len());
        for (g, w) in got.iter().zip(&want) {
            prop_assert!(g.abs_diff(*w) <= 1, "{} vs {}", g, w);
        }
    }
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/schedule/compile.rs`:

```rust
//! The schedule compiler (non-RT): flatten → route → time → translate →
//! sort.

use super::targets::{BuiltinRoute, TargetIdx, TargetKind, kinds_for};
use super::tempo::TempoTable;
use super::{Entry, MAX_TARGETS, Schedule, WireMsg};
use crate::backend::MidiPortInfo;
use crate::error::EngineError;
use crate::midi1::{Class, Midi1Msg, When, translate};
use core_model::document::{DestKind, Document};
use core_model::event::EventBody;
use core_model::ids::{DestId, SeqId};
use core_model::query::{PathStep, flatten, seq_length};
use core_model::selection::ViewFilter;
use core_model::time::Tick;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// Compiler inputs besides the document.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct CompileOptions {
    /// Output sample rate.
    pub sample_rate: f64,
    /// Where `builtin:synth` destinations play.
    pub route: BuiltinRoute,
    /// MIDI destinations that currently exist.
    pub ports: Vec<MidiPortInfo>,
}

impl CompileOptions {
    /// Built-in routing, no MIDI destinations.
    pub fn new(sample_rate: f64) -> CompileOptions {
        CompileOptions {
            sample_rate,
            route: BuiltinRoute::Builtin,
            ports: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}

/// An event that was not played, with where it came from.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Unroutable {
    /// Path from the compiled sequence to the source event.
    pub path: Vec<PathStep>,
    /// Why it cannot play.
    pub reason: String,
}

/// What a compile did; nothing is silently dropped.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct CompileReport {
    /// Flattened MIDI events that play.
    pub events: usize,
    /// Scheduled entries (messages × targets).
    pub entries: usize,
    /// Events that cannot play.
    pub unroutable: Vec<Unroutable>,
    /// Per-note controllers and per-note pitch bends dropped.
    pub dropped_per_note: usize,
    /// Note attributes dropped.
    pub dropped_note_attributes: usize,
    /// 8-bit SysEx events dropped.
    pub dropped_sysex8: usize,
    /// Events from UMP groups other than 0 (sent without their group).
    pub group_folded: usize,
    /// Audio regions skipped (audio playback is not in this engine yet).
    pub skipped_audio: usize,
    /// One line per kind of problem, for display.
    pub warnings: Vec<String>,
    /// Wall-clock compile time, excluding target (sink) creation.
    pub compile_time: Duration,
}

type Routes = Result<Vec<TargetIdx>, String>;

/// Resolves a target kind to an index: `Ok(Ok(idx))` plays, `Ok(Err(why))`
/// makes the events unroutable, `Err` aborts the compile.
pub type Resolver<'a> =
    dyn FnMut(&TargetKind) -> Result<Result<TargetIdx, String>, EngineError> + 'a;

struct Pending {
    frame: u64,
    class: Class,
    group: u8,
    order: u64,
    entry: Entry,
}

/// Compiles sequence `seq` of `doc` into a schedule.
pub fn compile(
    doc: &Document,
    seq: SeqId,
    opts: &CompileOptions,
    resolve: &mut Resolver<'_>,
) -> Result<(Schedule, CompileReport), EngineError> {
    let started = Instant::now();
    let s = doc.sequence(seq)?;
    let len = seq_length(doc, seq)?;
    let origin = -s.start_offset;
    let sr = opts.sample_rate;
    let base = s.tempo.tick_to_seconds(origin);
    let frame = |t: Tick| -> u64 {
        let f = ((s.tempo.tick_to_seconds(t) - base) * sr).round();
        if f <= 0.0 { 0 } else { f as u64 }
    };

    let mut report = CompileReport::default();
    let mut routes: BTreeMap<DestId, Routes> = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut used: BTreeMap<DestId, ()> = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut pending: Vec<Pending> = Vec::new();
    let mut arena: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
    let mut used_channels = [0u16; MAX_TARGETS];
    let mut order = 0u64;
    let mut resolving = Duration::ZERO;

    for r in flatten(doc, seq, None, &ViewFilter::default())? {
        if matches!(r.body, EventBody::AudioRegion { .. }) {
            report.skipped_audio += 1;
            continue;
        }
        if !r.body.is_midi() || matches!(r.body, EventBody::Text { .. }) {
            continue;
        }
        let Some(dest) = r.dest else {
            report.unroutable.push(Unroutable {
                path: r.path,
                reason: "no destination".into(),
            });
            continue;
        };
        let targets = match routes.get(&dest) {
            Some(t) => t.clone(),
            None => {
                let t0 = Instant::now();
                let t = route_dest(doc, dest, opts, resolve)?;
                resolving += t0.elapsed();
                routes.insert(dest, t.clone());
                t
            }
        };
        let targets = match targets {
            Ok(t) => t,
            Err(reason) => {
                report.unroutable.push(Unroutable {
                    path: r.path,
                    reason,
                });
                continue;
            }
        };
        let (group, channel) = r.group_channel.unwrap_or((0, 0));
        if group != 0 {
            report.group_folded += 1;
        }
        let t = translate(&r.body, channel);
        report.dropped_per_note += t.drops.per_note;
        report.dropped_note_attributes += usize::from(t.drops.attribute);
        report.dropped_sysex8 += usize::from(t.drops.sysex8);
        let end = match &r.body {
            EventBody::Note { duration, .. } => r.tick + *duration,
            _ => r.tick,
        };
        let mut played = false;
        for out in t.msgs() {
            let f = frame(if out.when == When::End { end } else { r.tick });
            for target in &targets {
                pending.push(Pending {
                    frame: f,
                    class: out.class,
                    group: 1,
                    order,
                    entry: Entry {
                        frame: f,
                        target: *target,
                        msg: WireMsg::Midi1(out.msg),
                    },
                });
                order += 1;
                mark(&mut used_channels, *target, channel);
            }
            played = true;
        }
        if let Some(data) = t.sysex {
            let start = u32::try_from(arena.len()).unwrap_or(u32::MAX);
            arena.push(0xF0);
            arena.extend_from_slice(data);
            arena.push(0xF7);
            let len = u32::try_from(data.len() + 2).unwrap_or(u32::MAX);
            let f = frame(r.tick);
            for target in &targets {
                pending.push(Pending {
                    frame: f,
                    class: Class::Control,
                    group: 1,
                    order,
                    entry: Entry {
                        frame: f,
                        target: *target,
                        msg: WireMsg::SysEx { start, len },
                    },
                });
                order += 1;
            }
            played = true;
        }
        if played {
            report.events += 1;
            used.insert(dest, ());
        }
    }

    // Default patches of the destinations that play, at frame 0, before
    // every event on that frame of the same class.
    for dest in used.keys() {
        let Some(d) = doc.instruments().get(dest) else {
            continue;
        };
        let (Some(patch), DestKind::MidiEndpoint { channel, .. }) = (d.default_patch, &d.kind)
        else {
            continue;
        };
        let Some(Ok(targets)) = routes.get(dest) else {
            continue;
        };
        let ch = channel & 0x0F;
        let mut msgs: Vec<Midi1Msg> = Vec::new();
        if let Some((msb, lsb)) = patch.bank {
            msgs.push(Midi1Msg::three(0xB0 | ch, 0, msb & 0x7F));
            msgs.push(Midi1Msg::three(0xB0 | ch, 32, lsb & 0x7F));
        }
        msgs.push(Midi1Msg::two(0xC0 | ch, patch.program & 0x7F));
        for msg in msgs {
            for target in targets {
                pending.push(Pending {
                    frame: 0,
                    class: Class::Patch,
                    group: 0,
                    order,
                    entry: Entry {
                        frame: 0,
                        target: *target,
                        msg: WireMsg::Midi1(msg),
                    },
                });
                order += 1;
                mark(&mut used_channels, *target, ch);
            }
        }
    }

    pending.sort_by_key(|p| (p.frame, p.class, p.group, p.order));
    let entries: Box<[Entry]> = pending.iter().map(|p| p.entry).collect();
    report.entries = entries.len();
    let length_frames = frame(len);
    let bar1 = frame(Tick::ZERO);
    let schedule = Schedule {
        sample_rate: sr,
        entries,
        sysex: arena.into_boxed_slice(),
        tempo: TempoTable::build(&s.tempo, origin, sr),
        length_frames,
        loop_range: (bar1 < length_frames).then_some((bar1, length_frames)),
        used_channels,
    };
    report.warnings = warnings(&report);
    report.compile_time = started.elapsed().saturating_sub(resolving);
    Ok((schedule, report))
}

fn mark(used: &mut [u16; MAX_TARGETS], target: TargetIdx, channel: u8) {
    if let Some(m) = used.get_mut(usize::from(target.0)) {
        *m |= 1 << (channel & 0x0F);
    }
}

fn route_dest(
    doc: &Document,
    dest: DestId,
    opts: &CompileOptions,
    resolve: &mut Resolver<'_>,
) -> Result<Routes, EngineError> {
    let Some(d) = doc.instruments().get(&dest) else {
        return Ok(Err(format!("unknown destination {dest}")));
    };
    let kinds = match kinds_for(&d.kind, opts.route, &opts.ports) {
        Ok(k) => k,
        Err(why) => return Ok(Err(format!("{}: {why}", d.name))),
    };
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(kinds.len());
    for k in &kinds {
        match resolve(k)? {
            Ok(i) => out.push(i),
            Err(why) => return Ok(Err(format!("{}: {why}", d.name))),
        }
    }
    Ok(Ok(out))
}

fn warnings(r: &CompileReport) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut w = Vec::new();
    let mut reasons: BTreeMap<&str, usize> = BTreeMap::new();
    for u in &r.unroutable {
        *reasons.entry(u.reason.as_str()).or_default() += 1;
    }
    for (reason, n) in reasons {
        w.push(format!("{n} event(s) not played: {reason}"));
    }
    if r.group_folded > 0 {
        w.push(format!(
            "{} event(s) from UMP groups other than 0 sent without their group",
            r.group_folded
        ));
    }
    if r.dropped_per_note > 0 {
        w.push(format!(
            "{} per-note controller(s) dropped (MIDI 1.0 output)",
            r.dropped_per_note
        ));
    }
    if r.dropped_note_attributes > 0 {
        w.push(format!(
            "{} note attribute(s) dropped (MIDI 1.0 output)",
            r.dropped_note_attributes
        ));
    }
    if r.dropped_sysex8 > 0 {
        w.push(format!(
            "{} 8-bit SysEx event(s) dropped (MIDI 1.0 output)",
            r.dropped_sysex8
        ));
    }
    if r.skipped_audio > 0 {
        w.push(format!(
            "{} audio region(s) skipped (audio playback is not available yet)",
            r.skipped_audio
        ));
    }
    w
}
```

```rust
//! Immutable playback schedules and their compiler.

pub mod compile;
pub mod targets;
pub mod tempo;

pub use compile::{CompileOptions, CompileReport, Unroutable, compile};
pub use targets::{BuiltinRoute, MAX_TARGETS, TargetIdx, TargetKind, TargetTable};
pub use tempo::TempoTable;

use crate::midi1::Midi1Msg;

/// A message on the wire: a MIDI 1.0 channel message or a SysEx stored in
/// the schedule's arena. A `Ump64` variant can be added for MIDI 2.0
/// destinations without touching sorting or scheduling.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum WireMsg {
    /// A MIDI 1.0 channel message.
    Midi1(Midi1Msg),
    /// `F0 … F7` bytes at `sysex[start..start + len]`.
    SysEx {
        /// Arena offset.
        start: u32,
        /// Length including `F0` and `F7`.
        len: u32,
    },
}

/// One scheduled message.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Entry {
    /// Frame from the sequence origin.
    pub frame: u64,
    /// Where it goes.
    pub target: TargetIdx,
    /// What is sent.
    pub msg: WireMsg,
}

/// A compiled sequence: everything the audio thread needs, immutable.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Schedule {
    /// Sample rate the frames were computed at.
    pub sample_rate: f64,
    /// Entries sorted by `(frame, class, source order)`.
    pub entries: Box<[Entry]>,
    /// SysEx arena.
    pub sysex: Box<[u8]>,
    /// Frame ↔ tick map.
    pub tempo: TempoTable,
    /// Frame of the sequence end.
    pub length_frames: u64,
    /// Default loop range: bar 1 (tick 0) to the end.
    pub loop_range: Option<(u64, u64)>,
    /// Per target, a bitmask of the channels the schedule uses.
    pub used_channels: [u16; MAX_TARGETS],
}

impl Schedule {
    /// The bytes of `msg`. // RT
    pub fn bytes<'a>(&'a self, msg: &'a WireMsg) -> &'a [u8] {
        match msg {
            WireMsg::Midi1(m) => m.as_slice(),
            WireMsg::SysEx { start, len } => {
                let s = *start as usize;
                let e = s.saturating_add(*len as usize);
                self.sysex.get(s..e).unwrap_or(&[])
            }
        }
    }

    /// Index of the first entry at or after `frame`. // RT
    pub fn cursor_at(&self, frame: u64) -> usize {
        self.entries.partition_point(|e| e.frame < frame)
    }
}
```

```rust
use core_engine::midi1::Midi1Msg;
use core_engine::notes::ActiveNotes;
use core_engine::schedule::TargetIdx;
use core_engine::transport::{PlayState, Step, Transport};

/// Runs one block through the transport and returns its steps.
fn block(t: &mut Transport, length: u64, frames: u32) -> Vec<Step> {
    let mut steps = vec![];
    let mut offset = 0;
    while offset < frames {
        let s = t.next(length, offset, frames - offset);
        offset += s.frames();
        steps.push(s);
    }
    steps
}

#[test]
fn stopped_is_one_idle_step() {
    let mut t = Transport::new(100);
    assert_eq!(block(&mut t, 1000, 64), vec![Step::Idle { len: 64 }]);
}

#[test]
fn block_splits_exactly_at_loop_end() {
    let mut t = Transport::new(100);
    t.loop_range = Some((10, 50));
    t.locate(40);
    t.play(1000);
    assert_eq!(
        block(&mut t, 1000, 30),
        vec![
            Step::Play {
                from: 40,
                to: 50,
                offset: 0
            },
            Step::Wrap { offset: 10, to: 10 },
            Step::Play {
                from: 10,
                to: 30,
                offset: 10
            },
        ]
    );
    // A loop shorter than the block wraps several times.
    t.locate(10);
    let steps = block(&mut t, 1000, 100);
    assert_eq!(
        steps
            .iter()
            .filter(|s| matches!(s, Step::Wrap { .. }))
            .count(),
        2
    );
    assert_eq!(t.pos, 30);
}

#[test]
fn loop_end_on_a_block_boundary_wraps_in_the_next_block() {
    let mut t = Transport::new(100);
    t.loop_range = Some((0, 64));
    t.play(1000);
    assert_eq!(
        block(&mut t, 1000, 64),
        vec![Step::Play {
            from: 0,
            to: 64,
            offset: 0
        }]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        block(&mut t, 1000, 64),
        vec![
            Step::Wrap { offset: 0, to: 0 },
            Step::Play {
                from: 0,
                to: 64,
                offset: 0
            }
        ]
    );
}

#[test]
fn end_plays_the_tail_then_stops_once() {
    let mut t = Transport::new(100);
    t.play(150);
    assert_eq!(
        block(&mut t, 150, 128),
        vec![Step::Play {
            from: 0,
            to: 128,
            offset: 0
        }]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        block(&mut t, 150, 128),
        vec![
            Step::Play {
                from: 128,
                to: 150,
                offset: 0
            },
            Step::End { offset: 22 },
            Step::Idle { len: 100 },
            Step::Idle { len: 6 },
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(t.state, PlayState::Stopped);
    assert!(t.take_ended());
    assert!(!t.take_ended());
    // Play at the end starts over.
    t.play(150);
    assert_eq!(t.pos, 0);
}

#[test]
fn loop_past_its_end_plays_to_the_sequence_end() {
    let mut t = Transport::new(0);
    t.loop_range = Some((0, 50));
    t.locate(60);
    t.play(100);
    assert_eq!(
        block(&mut t, 100, 64),
        vec![
            Step::Play {
                from: 60,
                to: 100,
                offset: 0
            },
            Step::End { offset: 40 },
            Step::Idle { len: 24 }
        ]
    );
}

#[test]
fn empty_or_inverted_loops_are_ignored() {
    let mut t = Transport::new(0);
    t.loop_range = Some((50, 50));
    t.play(60);
    let steps = block(&mut t, 60, 64);
    assert!(!steps.iter().any(|s| matches!(s, Step::Wrap { .. })));
}

#[test]
fn notes_and_pedals_are_released() {
    let mut n = ActiveNotes::default();
    let t = TargetIdx(2);
    n.observe(t, &[0x90, 60, 100]);
    n.observe(t, &[0x91, 61, 100]);
    n.observe(t, &[0x90, 62, 100]);
    n.observe(t, &[0x80, 62, 0]);
    n.observe(t, &[0x90, 63, 0]); // velocity 0 = off
    n.observe(t, &[0xB1, 64, 127]);
    assert!(n.any());
    let mut sent = vec![];
    n.release(t, |m: Midi1Msg| sent.push(m.as_slice().to_vec()));
    assert_eq!(
        sent,
        vec![vec![0x80, 60, 0], vec![0x81, 61, 0], vec![0xB1, 64, 0]]
    );
    assert!(!n.any());
    let mut again = 0;
    n.release(t, |_| again += 1);
    assert_eq!(again, 0);
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/transport.rs`:

```rust
//! The transport state machine. It splits each block into steps at loop
//! ends and the sequence end; it never allocates. // RT

/// Transport state.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[repr(u8)]
pub enum PlayState {
    /// Not moving.
    Stopped = 0,
    /// Playing the schedule.
    Playing = 1,
    /// Past the sequence end, letting sounds ring out.
    Tail = 2,
}

impl PlayState {
    /// From the published `u8`.
    pub fn from_u8(v: u8) -> PlayState {
        match v {
            1 => PlayState::Playing,
            2 => PlayState::Tail,
            _ => PlayState::Stopped,
        }
    }
}

/// One piece of a block.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Step {
    /// Emit entries with `from <= frame < to`; block offset of `from` is
    /// `offset`; covers `to - from` frames.
    Play {
        /// First schedule frame.
        from: u64,
        /// End schedule frame (exclusive).
        to: u64,
        /// Block offset of `from`.
        offset: u32,
    },
    /// Loop end reached at `offset`: release notes, continue at the loop
    /// start. Covers no frames.
    Wrap {
        /// Block offset.
        offset: u32,
        /// Schedule frame playback continues from.
        to: u64,
    },
    /// Sequence end reached at `offset`: release notes, play the tail.
    /// Covers no frames.
    End {
        /// Block offset.
        offset: u32,
    },
    /// Nothing to emit for `len` frames (stopped or in the tail).
    Idle {
        /// Frames covered.
        len: u32,
    },
}

impl Step {
    /// Frames this step covers.
    pub fn frames(&self) -> u32 {
        match self {
            Step::Play { from, to, .. } => u32::try_from(to - from).unwrap_or(u32::MAX),
            Step::Wrap { .. } | Step::End { .. } => 0,
            Step::Idle { len } => *len,
        }
    }
}

/// Transport position and mode.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Transport {
    /// State.
    pub state: PlayState,
    /// Schedule frame of the next frame to play.
    pub pos: u64,
    /// Loop range `[start, end)`; ignored unless `end > start`.
    pub loop_range: Option<(u64, u64)>,
    /// Tail length in frames.
    pub tail_frames: u64,
    tail_left: u64,
    ended: bool,
}

impl Transport {
    /// A stopped transport at frame 0.
    pub fn new(tail_frames: u64) -> Transport {
        Transport {
            state: PlayState::Stopped,
            pos: 0,
            loop_range: None,
            tail_frames,
            tail_left: 0,
            ended: false,
        }
    }

    fn active_loop(&self) -> Option<(u64, u64)> {
        self.loop_range.filter(|(s, e)| e > s)
    }

    /// Starts playing from `pos` (from 0 when at or past `length`).
    pub fn play(&mut self, length: u64) {
        if self.state == PlayState::Playing {
            return;
        }
        if self.pos >= length {
            self.pos = 0;
        }
        self.state = PlayState::Playing;
    }

    /// Stops; the position is kept.
    pub fn stop(&mut self) {
        self.state = PlayState::Stopped;
    }

    /// Moves to `frame`; a transport in its tail plays again.
    pub fn locate(&mut self, frame: u64) {
        self.pos = frame;
        if self.state == PlayState::Tail {
            self.state = PlayState::Playing;
        }
    }

    /// True once, after the tail finished. // RT
    pub fn take_ended(&mut self) -> bool {
        std::mem::take(&mut self.ended)
    }

    /// The next step of a block at `offset` with `remaining > 0` frames
    /// left, for a schedule `length` frames long. // RT
    pub fn next(&mut self, length: u64, offset: u32, remaining: u32) -> Step {
        match self.state {
            PlayState::Stopped => Step::Idle { len: remaining },
            PlayState::Tail => {
                let n = u64::from(remaining).min(self.tail_left);
                self.tail_left -= n;
                if self.tail_left == 0 {
                    self.state = PlayState::Stopped;
                    self.ended = true;
                }
                Step::Idle {
                    len: u32::try_from(n).unwrap_or(remaining),
                }
            }
            PlayState::Playing => {
                let lp = self.active_loop();
                if let Some((s, e)) = lp {
                    if self.pos == e {
                        self.pos = s;
                        return Step::Wrap { offset, to: s };
                    }
                }
                let end = match lp {
                    Some((_, e)) if self.pos < e => e,
                    _ => length,
                };
                if self.pos >= end {
                    self.state = PlayState::Tail;
                    self.tail_left = self.tail_frames;
                    if self.tail_left == 0 {
                        self.state = PlayState::Stopped;
                        self.ended = true;
                    }
                    return Step::End { offset };
                }
                let n = (end - self.pos).min(u64::from(remaining));
                let from = self.pos;
                self.pos += n;
                Step::Play {
                    from,
                    to: self.pos,
                    offset,
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/notes.rs`:

```rust
//! Active-note tracker: which notes sound per target, channel and pitch,
//! and which channels hold the sustain pedal. // RT

use crate::midi1::Midi1Msg;
use crate::schedule::{MAX_TARGETS, TargetIdx};

/// Fixed-size note and sustain state; no allocation.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ActiveNotes {
    on: [[u128; 16]; MAX_TARGETS],
    sustain: [u16; MAX_TARGETS],
}

impl Default for ActiveNotes {
    fn default() -> Self {
        ActiveNotes {
            on: [[0; 16]; MAX_TARGETS],
            sustain: [0; MAX_TARGETS],
        }
    }
}

impl ActiveNotes {
    /// Updates the state from a message sent to `target`. // RT
    pub fn observe(&mut self, target: TargetIdx, msg: &[u8]) {
        let t = usize::from(target.0);
        let (Some(&status), Some(notes), Some(sus)) =
            (msg.first(), self.on.get_mut(t), self.sustain.get_mut(t))
        else {
            return;
        };
        let ch = usize::from(status & 0x0F);
        let d1 = msg.get(1).copied().unwrap_or(0) & 0x7F;
        let d2 = msg.get(2).copied().unwrap_or(0);
        let Some(bits) = notes.get_mut(ch) else {
            return;
        };
        match status & 0xF0 {
            0x90 if d2 > 0 => *bits |= 1u128 << d1,
            0x90 | 0x80 => *bits &= !(1u128 << d1),
            0xB0 if d1 == 64 => {
                if d2 >= 64 {
                    *sus |= 1 << ch;
                } else {
                    *sus &= !(1 << ch);
                }
            }
            0xB0 if d1 == 123 => *bits = 0,
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    /// Calls `send` with a note-off for every sounding note of `target` and
    /// CC 64 = 0 for every held pedal, then forgets them. // RT
    pub fn release(&mut self, target: TargetIdx, mut send: impl FnMut(Midi1Msg)) {
        let t = usize::from(target.0);
        let (Some(notes), Some(sus)) = (self.on.get_mut(t), self.sustain.get_mut(t)) else {
            return;
        };
        for (ch, bits) in notes.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            let status = 0x80 | (ch as u8 & 0x0F);
            while *bits != 0 {
                let pitch = bits.trailing_zeros() as u8;
                send(Midi1Msg::three(status, pitch, 0));
                *bits &= !(1u128 << pitch);
            }
            if *sus & (1 << ch) != 0 {
                send(Midi1Msg::three(0xB0 | (ch as u8 & 0x0F), 64, 0));
            }
        }
        *sus = 0;
    }

    /// True when any note of any target sounds.
    pub fn any(&self) -> bool {
        self.on.iter().flatten().any(|b| *b != 0) || self.sustain.iter().any(|s| *s != 0)
    }
}
```

```rust
//! `core-engine`: compiles a core-model sequence into an immutable schedule
//! and plays it sample-accurately through platform backends, with a
//! real-time path that never blocks or allocates.
//!
//! Everything reachable from `render::Renderer::process` is marked
//! `// RT`; such code must not allocate, free, lock, do I/O or log.
#![deny(missing_docs)]
#![cfg_attr(
    not(test),
    deny(
        clippy::unwrap_used,
        clippy::expect_used,
        clippy::indexing_slicing,
        clippy::panic
    )
)]

pub mod backend;
pub mod error;
#[cfg(feature = "fixtures")]
pub mod fixtures;
pub mod midi1;
pub mod notes;
pub mod schedule;
pub mod transport;

pub use error::{BackendError, EngineError};
```

### Task 7: Renderer, Engine control handle and the counting allocator

**Goal:** The real-time `Renderer` (spec §8: drain the command ring with deferred swaps, tick-preserving schedule swap, step the transport, emit at offsets, release notes, mix, clamp, publish atomics), the non-RT `Engine` (spec §8 control handle: `load` with a `SinkFactory` (Plan Ruling 6), commands through a 64-slot ring, collector thread), the `AudioBackend` trait, and `rt_check`.

- Modify: `crates/core-engine/src/backend.rs` (adds `AudioBackend`)
- Modify: `crates/core-engine/src/lib.rs`

The test drives `Renderer::process` directly with its own recording sinks (the offline backend comes in Task 8).

Create `crates/core-engine/tests/engine.rs`:

```rust
//! Drives `Renderer::process` directly with test sinks (the offline backend
//! comes later); each sink records `(block, offset or ns, bytes)`.

use core_engine::BackendError;
use core_engine::EngineError;
use core_engine::backend::{BlockTime, InstrumentNode, MidiOut, MidiPortInfo, StereoBuf};
use core_engine::engine::{Engine, EngineConfig, SinkFactory, find_sequence};
use core_engine::fixtures::{Builder, Q};
use core_engine::render::{Faults, Renderer, Sink};
use core_engine::schedule::{BuiltinRoute, TargetKind};
use core_engine::transport::PlayState;
use core_model::document::Document;
use core_model::ids::SeqId;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

type Seen = Arc<Mutex<Vec<(u64, Vec<u8>)>>>;

/// Records `(block_start + offset, bytes)` and renders a constant level.
struct Node {
    seen: Seen,
    rendered: u64,
    level: f32,
}

impl InstrumentNode for Node {
    fn midi(&mut self, offset: u32, msg: &[u8]) {
        self.seen
            .lock()
            .unwrap()
            .push((self.rendered + u64::from(offset), msg.to_vec()));
    }
    fn render(&mut self, frames: u32, out: &mut StereoBuf) {
        out.left[..frames as usize].fill(self.level);
        out.right[..frames as usize].fill(-self.level);
        self.rendered += u64::from(frames);
    }
    fn reset(&mut self) {}
}

/// Records `(host_time_ns, bytes)`.
struct Out {
    seen: Seen,
}

impl MidiOut for Out {
    fn send(&mut self, host_time_ns: u64, msg: &[u8]) -> bool {
        self.seen.lock().unwrap().push((host_time_ns, msg.to_vec()));
        true
    }
}

#[derive(Default, Clone)]
struct Factory {
    synth: Seen,
    midi: Seen,
    level: f32,
}

impl SinkFactory for Factory {
    fn ports(&self) -> Vec<MidiPortInfo> {
        vec![]
    }
    fn make(&mut self, kind: &TargetKind, _sr: f64, _max: u32) -> Result<Sink, BackendError> {
        Ok(match kind {
            TargetKind::BuiltinSynth => Sink::Instrument(Box::new(Node {
                seen: self.synth.clone(),
                rendered: 0,
                level: self.level,
            })),
            _ => Sink::Midi(Box::new(Out {
                seen: self.midi.clone(),
            })),
        })
    }
}

fn doc() -> (Document, SeqId) {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let d = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("S", "L", Some(d)).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, lane, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q), (Q, 62, 0x8000, 2 * Q)])
        .unwrap();
    (b.finish(), seq)
}

/// Renders `n` blocks of `frames`; returns the last block's left channel.
fn blocks(r: &mut Renderer, n: usize, frames: u32, start_ns: &mut u64) -> Vec<f32> {
    let mut l = vec![0.0f32; frames as usize];
    let mut rr = vec![0.0f32; frames as usize];
    for _ in 0..n {
        let mut chans: [&mut [f32]; 2] = [&mut l, &mut rr];
        r.process(
            &mut chans,
            frames,
            BlockTime {
                host_time_ns: *start_ns,
                sample_rate: 48_000.0,
            },
        );
        *start_ns += u64::from(frames) * 1_000_000_000 / 48_000;
    }
    l
}

#[test]
fn events_land_at_their_offsets_and_stop_releases() {
    let (doc, seq) = doc();
    let f = Factory {
        level: 0.25,
        ..Factory::default()
    };
    let (mut engine, mut r) = Engine::new(EngineConfig::new(48_000.0), Box::new(f.clone()));
    let report = engine.load(&doc, seq).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(report.events, 2);
    engine.play().unwrap();
    let mut ns = 0;
    let out = blocks(&mut r, 50, 1000, &mut ns); // 50 000 frames
    assert_eq!(out[0], 0.25, "instrument output is mixed into channel 0");
    assert_eq!(engine.state(), PlayState::Playing);
    assert_eq!(engine.position_frames(), 50_000);
    engine.stop().unwrap();
    blocks(&mut r, 1, 1000, &mut ns);
    assert_eq!(engine.state(), PlayState::Stopped);
    let seen = f.synth.lock().unwrap().clone();
    assert_eq!(
        seen,
        vec![
            (0, vec![0x90, 60, 64]),
            (24_000, vec![0x80, 60, 0]),
            (24_000, vec![0x90, 62, 64]),
            (50_000, vec![0x80, 62, 0]), // cut by stop
        ]
    );
}

#[test]
fn midi_outs_get_host_times() {
    let (doc, seq) = doc();
    let f = Factory::default();
    let mut cfg = EngineConfig::new(48_000.0);
    cfg.route = BuiltinRoute::Virtual;
    cfg.midi_offset_ns = 1_000;
    let (mut engine, mut r) = Engine::new(cfg, Box::new(f.clone()));
    engine.load(&doc, seq).unwrap();
    engine.play().unwrap();
    let mut ns = 5_000_000_000;
    blocks(&mut r, 30, 1000, &mut ns);
    let seen = f.midi.lock().unwrap().clone();
    // Frame 24000 is block 24 offset 0: 5 s + 24 × 1000 frames × 20833 ns.
    assert_eq!(seen[0], (5_000_000_000 + 1_000, vec![0x90, 60, 64]));
    assert_eq!(
        seen[1],
        (5_000_000_000 + 24 * 20_833_333 + 1_000, vec![0x80, 60, 0])
    );
}

#[test]
fn clipping_and_xruns_are_faults_that_clear_on_read() {
    let (doc, seq) = doc();
    let f = Factory {
        level: 2.0,
        ..Factory::default()
    };
    let (mut engine, mut r) = Engine::new(EngineConfig::new(48_000.0), Box::new(f));
    engine.load(&doc, seq).unwrap();
    let mut ns = 0;
    let out = blocks(&mut r, 1, 64, &mut ns);
    assert_eq!(out[0], 1.0, "hard clamp at +1.0");
    assert!(engine.faults().contains(Faults::CLIPPED));
    assert!(engine.faults().is_empty());
    blocks(&mut r, 1, 5000, &mut ns);
    let f = engine.faults();
    assert!(f.contains(Faults::XRUN));
    assert_eq!(f.names(), vec!["xrun"]);
    assert_eq!(engine.counters().xruns, 1);
}

#[test]
fn loop_and_locate_by_tick() {
    let (doc, seq) = doc();
    let f = Factory::default();
    let (mut engine, mut r) = Engine::new(EngineConfig::new(48_000.0), Box::new(f.clone()));
    engine.load(&doc, seq).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(engine.length_frames(), 96_000);
    engine.set_loop(true).unwrap();
    engine.locate_tick(core_model::time::Tick(3 * Q)).unwrap();
    engine.play().unwrap();
    let mut ns = 0;
    blocks(&mut r, 30, 1000, &mut ns); // 72000 → 96000 wraps to 0 → 6000
    assert_eq!(engine.position_frames(), 6_000);
    assert_eq!(engine.position_tick(), core_model::time::Tick(3_840));
    let ons = f
        .synth
        .lock()
        .unwrap()
        .iter()
        .filter(|(_, b)| b[0] == 0x90)
        .count();
    assert_eq!(ons, 1, "note 60 replayed after the wrap");
}

#[test]
fn engine_errors() {
    let (doc, _) = doc();
    assert_eq!(
        find_sequence(&doc, "Nope").unwrap_err(),
        EngineError::UnknownSequence("Nope".into())
    );
    let (mut engine, _r) = Engine::new(EngineConfig::new(48_000.0), Box::new(Factory::default()));
    assert!(matches!(
        engine.load(&doc, SeqId(99)),
        Err(EngineError::Model(_))
    ));
    // The command ring holds 64 commands; nothing drains it here.
    let mut busy = false;
    for _ in 0..100 {
        if engine.play() == Err(EngineError::Busy) {
            busy = true;
            break;
        }
    }
    assert!(busy);
}
```
