Create `crates/core-engine/src/render.rs`:

```rust
//! The real-time renderer and the state it publishes.

use crate::backend::{BlockTime, InstrumentNode, MidiOut, StereoBuf};
use crate::notes::ActiveNotes;
use crate::schedule::{MAX_TARGETS, Schedule, TargetIdx};
use crate::transport::{PlayState, Step, Transport};
use rtrb::{Consumer, Producer};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU8, AtomicU32, AtomicU64, Ordering};

/// Real-time fault bits, cleared when read by [`crate::engine::Engine::faults`].
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Faults(pub u32);

impl Faults {
    /// A block was larger than the prepared size (rendered as silence).
    pub const XRUN: Faults = Faults(1);
    /// The master bus was clamped at ±1.0.
    pub const CLIPPED: Faults = Faults(2);
    /// A MIDI output queue was full; a message was dropped.
    pub const MIDI_DROPPED: Faults = Faults(4);
    /// A schedule swap waited a block because the retire ring was full.
    pub const SWAP_DEFERRED: Faults = Faults(8);
    /// The audio callback panicked; output is silent.
    pub const PANIC: Faults = Faults(16);

    /// True when every bit of `other` is set.
    pub fn contains(self, other: Faults) -> bool {
        self.0 & other.0 == other.0
    }

    /// True when no bit is set.
    pub fn is_empty(self) -> bool {
        self.0 == 0
    }

    /// Names of the set bits.
    pub fn names(self) -> Vec<&'static str> {
        [
            (Faults::XRUN, "xrun"),
            (Faults::CLIPPED, "clipped"),
            (Faults::MIDI_DROPPED, "midi-dropped"),
            (Faults::SWAP_DEFERRED, "swap-deferred"),
            (Faults::PANIC, "panic"),
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|(f, _)| self.contains(*f))
        .map(|(_, n)| n)
        .collect()
    }
}

/// Counters published by the renderer.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Counters {
    /// MIDI messages dropped because an output queue was full.
    pub midi_dropped: u64,
    /// Blocks in which a schedule swap was deferred.
    pub swaps_deferred: u64,
    /// Blocks rendered as silence because they were too large.
    pub xruns: u64,
}

/// State shared between the renderer and the control side (atomics only).
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct Shared {
    position: AtomicU64,
    state: AtomicU8,
    faults: AtomicU32,
    ended: AtomicBool,
    midi_dropped: AtomicU64,
    swaps_deferred: AtomicU64,
    xruns: AtomicU64,
}

impl Shared {
    /// Sets fault bits. // RT
    pub fn raise(&self, f: Faults) {
        self.faults.fetch_or(f.0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Reads and clears the fault bits.
    pub fn take_faults(&self) -> Faults {
        Faults(self.faults.swap(0, Ordering::Relaxed))
    }

    /// Schedule frame of the transport.
    pub fn position(&self) -> u64 {
        self.position.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
    }

    /// Transport state.
    pub fn state(&self) -> PlayState {
        PlayState::from_u8(self.state.load(Ordering::Relaxed))
    }

    /// True once after playback reached the end and the tail finished.
    pub fn take_ended(&self) -> bool {
        self.ended.swap(false, Ordering::Relaxed)
    }

    /// Current counters.
    pub fn counters(&self) -> Counters {
        Counters {
            midi_dropped: self.midi_dropped.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
            swaps_deferred: self.swaps_deferred.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
            xruns: self.xruns.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        }
    }
}

/// Where a target's events go.
pub enum Sink {
    /// An instrument rendered into the mix.
    Instrument(Box<dyn InstrumentNode>),
    /// A timestamped MIDI output.
    Midi(Box<dyn MidiOut>),
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for Sink {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(match self {
            Sink::Instrument(_) => "Sink::Instrument",
            Sink::Midi(_) => "Sink::Midi",
        })
    }
}

/// Commands from the control side, applied at the start of a block.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum EngineCmd {
    /// Replace the schedule, keeping the musical position.
    SetSchedule(Box<Schedule>),
    /// Start playing.
    Play,
    /// Stop (note-offs are sent).
    Stop,
    /// Move to a schedule frame.
    Locate(u64),
    /// Set or clear the loop range.
    SetLoop(Option<(u64, u64)>),
    /// Install the sink of a new target (targets are only appended).
    AddSink(TargetIdx, Sink),
}

/// Capacity of the command ring.
pub const CMD_CAPACITY: usize = 64;
/// Capacity of the retire ring.
pub const RETIRE_CAPACITY: usize = 8;

/// Renderer settings.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct RenderConfig {
    /// Stream sample rate.
    pub sample_rate: f64,
    /// Largest block (≤ [`crate::backend::MAX_FRAMES`] is typical).
    pub max_frames: u32,
    /// Added to every MIDI-out timestamp.
    pub midi_offset_ns: i64,
}

/// The real-time object. Built by [`crate::engine::Engine::new`], moved to
/// the audio thread by a backend and handed back on stop.
pub struct Renderer {
    cfg: RenderConfig,
    cmds: Consumer<EngineCmd>,
    retire: Producer<Box<Schedule>>,
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    schedule: Option<Box<Schedule>>,
    cursor: usize,
    transport: Transport,
    notes: ActiveNotes,
    sinks: [Option<Sink>; MAX_TARGETS],
    bufs: Vec<StereoBuf>,
    block_ns: u64,
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for Renderer {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("Renderer")
            .field("cfg", &self.cfg)
            .field("transport", &self.transport)
            .finish_non_exhaustive()
    }
}

/// Everything `send` needs, borrowed apart from the note tracker.
struct Out<'a> {
    sinks: &'a mut [Option<Sink>; MAX_TARGETS],
    shared: &'a Shared,
    block_ns: u64,
    ns_per_frame: f64,
    midi_offset_ns: i64,
}

impl Out<'_> {
    /// Sends `bytes` to `target` at block offset `offset`. // RT
    fn send(&mut self, target: TargetIdx, offset: u32, bytes: &[u8]) {
        match self.sinks.get_mut(usize::from(target.0)) {
            Some(Some(Sink::Instrument(node))) => node.midi(offset, bytes),
            Some(Some(Sink::Midi(out))) => {
                let at = self.block_ns as i64
                    + (f64::from(offset) * self.ns_per_frame).round() as i64
                    + self.midi_offset_ns;
                if !out.send(at.max(0) as u64, bytes) {
                    self.shared.raise(Faults::MIDI_DROPPED);
                    self.shared.midi_dropped.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }
}

impl Renderer {
    pub(crate) fn new(
        cfg: RenderConfig,
        cmds: Consumer<EngineCmd>,
        retire: Producer<Box<Schedule>>,
        shared: Arc<Shared>,
    ) -> Renderer {
        let mut r = Renderer {
            cfg,
            cmds,
            retire,
            shared,
            schedule: None,
            cursor: 0,
            transport: Transport::new(cfg.sample_rate.max(0.0).round() as u64),
            notes: ActiveNotes::default(),
            sinks: std::array::from_fn(|_| None),
            bufs: Vec::new(),
            block_ns: 0,
        };
        r.prepare(cfg.max_frames);
        r
    }

    /// (Re)allocates scratch buffers for blocks of up to `max_frames`.
    /// Not real-time safe: call only while stopped.
    pub fn prepare(&mut self, max_frames: u32) {
        self.cfg.max_frames = max_frames;
        self.bufs = (0..MAX_TARGETS)
            .map(|_| StereoBuf::new(max_frames))
            .collect();
    }

    /// Settings.
    pub fn config(&self) -> RenderConfig {
        self.cfg
    }

    /// Sets fault bits (used by backends, e.g. after a caught panic). // RT
    pub fn raise(&self, f: Faults) {
        self.shared.raise(f);
    }

    /// Renders one block of `frames` frames into planar `out` channels.
    /// Channels 0–1 get the mix, the rest zeros. // RT
    pub fn process(&mut self, out: &mut [&mut [f32]], frames: u32, time: BlockTime) {
        #[cfg(feature = "rt-check")]
        crate::rt_check::arm();
        self.process_inner(out, frames, time);
        #[cfg(feature = "rt-check")]
        crate::rt_check::disarm();
    }

    fn process_inner(&mut self, out: &mut [&mut [f32]], frames: u32, time: BlockTime) {
        let n = frames as usize;
        if frames > self.cfg.max_frames || out.iter().any(|c| c.len() < n) {
            for ch in out.iter_mut() {
                ch.fill(0.0);
            }
            self.shared.raise(Faults::XRUN);
            self.shared.xruns.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            return;
        }
        self.block_ns = time.host_time_ns;
        self.drain_commands();
        let length = self.schedule.as_ref().map_or(0, |s| s.length_frames);
        let mut o = Out {
            sinks: &mut self.sinks,
            shared: &self.shared,
            block_ns: time.host_time_ns,
            ns_per_frame: 1e9 / self.cfg.sample_rate.max(1.0),
            midi_offset_ns: self.cfg.midi_offset_ns,
        };
        let mut offset = 0u32;
        while offset < frames {
            let step = self.transport.next(length, offset, frames - offset);
            match step {
                Step::Play { from, to, offset } => {
                    if let Some(s) = self.schedule.as_deref() {
                        while let Some(e) = s.entries.get(self.cursor) {
                            if e.frame >= to {
                                break;
                            }
                            let off =
                                offset + u32::try_from(e.frame.saturating_sub(from)).unwrap_or(0);
                            let bytes = s.bytes(&e.msg);
                            o.send(e.target, off, bytes);
                            self.notes.observe(e.target, bytes);
                            self.cursor += 1;
                        }
                    }
                }
                Step::Wrap { offset, to } => {
                    release_all(&mut self.notes, &mut o, offset);
                    self.cursor = self.schedule.as_ref().map_or(0, |s| s.cursor_at(to));
                }
                Step::End { offset } => release_all(&mut self.notes, &mut o, offset),
                Step::Idle { .. } => {}
            }
            offset += step.frames();
        }
        self.mix(out, frames);
        self.publish();
    }

    /// Applies queued commands. A `SetSchedule` waits when the retire ring
    /// is full (and everything after it waits too, to keep order). // RT
    fn drain_commands(&mut self) {
        loop {
            let deferred = match self.cmds.peek() {
                Ok(EngineCmd::SetSchedule(_)) => {
                    self.schedule.is_some() && self.retire.slots() == 0
                }
                Ok(_) => false,
                Err(_) => break,
            };
            if deferred {
                self.shared.raise(Faults::SWAP_DEFERRED);
                self.shared.swaps_deferred.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                break;
            }
            let Ok(cmd) = self.cmds.pop() else {
                break;
            };
            self.apply(cmd);
        }
    }

    fn release_now(&mut self) {
        let mut o = Out {
            sinks: &mut self.sinks,
            shared: &self.shared,
            block_ns: self.block_ns,
            ns_per_frame: 0.0,
            midi_offset_ns: self.cfg.midi_offset_ns,
        };
        release_all(&mut self.notes, &mut o, 0);
    }

    fn apply(&mut self, cmd: EngineCmd) {
        match cmd {
            EngineCmd::SetSchedule(new) => {
                self.release_now();
                if let Some(old) = self.schedule.take() {
                    let tick = old.tempo.frame_to_tick(self.transport.pos);
                    self.transport.pos = new.tempo.tick_to_frame(tick);
                    self.transport.loop_range = self.transport.loop_range.map(|(s, e)| {
                        (
                            new.tempo.tick_to_frame(old.tempo.frame_to_tick(s)),
                            new.tempo.tick_to_frame(old.tempo.frame_to_tick(e)),
                        )
                    });
                    if let Err(rtrb::PushError::Full(old)) = self.retire.push(old) {
                        // Unreachable: room was checked in `drain_commands`.
                        // Leaking is the only RT-safe choice.
                        std::mem::forget(old);
                    }
                }
                self.cursor = new.cursor_at(self.transport.pos);
                self.schedule = Some(new);
            }
            EngineCmd::Play => {
                let length = self.schedule.as_ref().map_or(0, |s| s.length_frames);
                self.transport.play(length);
                self.seek();
            }
            EngineCmd::Stop => {
                self.release_now();
                self.transport.stop();
            }
            EngineCmd::Locate(frame) => {
                self.release_now();
                self.transport.locate(frame);
                self.seek();
            }
            EngineCmd::SetLoop(range) => self.transport.loop_range = range,
            EngineCmd::AddSink(idx, sink) => {
                if let Some(slot) = self.sinks.get_mut(usize::from(idx.0)) {
                    if let Some(old) = slot.replace(sink) {
                        // Unreachable: the engine only fills empty slots.
                        std::mem::forget(old);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fn seek(&mut self) {
        self.cursor = self
            .schedule
            .as_ref()
            .map_or(0, |s| s.cursor_at(self.transport.pos));
    }

    /// Renders instrument sinks and sums them into channels 0–1. // RT
    fn mix(&mut self, out: &mut [&mut [f32]], frames: u32) {
        let n = frames as usize;
        for ch in out.iter_mut() {
            if let Some(c) = ch.get_mut(..n) {
                c.fill(0.0);
            }
        }
        let mut clipped = false;
        for (sink, buf) in self.sinks.iter_mut().zip(self.bufs.iter_mut()) {
            let Some(Sink::Instrument(node)) = sink else {
                continue;
            };
            node.render(frames, buf);
            for (i, src) in [&buf.left, &buf.right].into_iter().enumerate() {
                let (Some(dst), Some(src)) =
                    (out.get_mut(i).and_then(|c| c.get_mut(..n)), src.get(..n))
                else {
                    continue;
                };
                for (d, s) in dst.iter_mut().zip(src) {
                    *d += *s;
                }
            }
        }
        for ch in out.iter_mut().take(2) {
            if let Some(c) = ch.get_mut(..n) {
                for x in c {
                    if *x > 1.0 || *x < -1.0 {
                        clipped = true;
                        *x = x.clamp(-1.0, 1.0);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if clipped {
            self.shared.raise(Faults::CLIPPED);
        }
    }

    fn publish(&mut self) {
        let s = &self.shared;
        s.position.store(self.transport.pos, Ordering::Relaxed);
        s.state.store(self.transport.state as u8, Ordering::Relaxed);
        if self.transport.take_ended() {
            s.ended.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
        }
    }

    /// Resets every instrument (all notes off). Call while stopped.
    pub fn reset_instruments(&mut self) {
        for sink in self.sinks.iter_mut().flatten() {
            if let Sink::Instrument(node) = sink {
                node.reset();
            }
        }
    }

    /// Mutable access to a target's sink (for backends and tests, not RT).
    pub fn sink_mut(&mut self, idx: TargetIdx) -> Option<&mut Sink> {
        self.sinks
            .get_mut(usize::from(idx.0))
            .and_then(Option::as_mut)
    }
}

/// Note-offs (and pedal-ups) for every target at block offset `offset`. // RT
fn release_all(notes: &mut ActiveNotes, o: &mut Out<'_>, offset: u32) {
    for t in 0..MAX_TARGETS {
        let target = TargetIdx(t as u8);
        notes.release(target, |m| o.send(target, offset, m.as_slice()));
    }
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/engine.rs`:

```rust
//! The control handle (non-RT): compiles documents, sends commands to the
//! renderer and reads what it publishes.

use crate::backend::MidiPortInfo;
use crate::error::{BackendError, EngineError};
use crate::render::{
    CMD_CAPACITY, Counters, EngineCmd, Faults, RETIRE_CAPACITY, RenderConfig, Renderer, Shared,
    Sink,
};
use crate::schedule::{
    BuiltinRoute, CompileOptions, CompileReport, MAX_TARGETS, Schedule, TargetIdx, TargetKind,
    TargetTable, TempoTable, compile,
};
use crate::transport::PlayState;
use core_model::document::Document;
use core_model::ids::SeqId;
use core_model::time::Tick;
use rtrb::{Producer, RingBuffer};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::thread::JoinHandle;
use std::time::Duration;

/// Creates sinks for new targets (control thread).
pub trait SinkFactory: Send {
    /// MIDI destinations that exist now.
    fn ports(&self) -> Vec<MidiPortInfo>;
    /// Creates the sink of a target at the stream's rate and block size.
    fn make(
        &mut self,
        kind: &TargetKind,
        sample_rate: f64,
        max_frames: u32,
    ) -> Result<Sink, BackendError>;
}

/// Engine settings.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct EngineConfig {
    /// Stream sample rate.
    pub sample_rate: f64,
    /// Largest block the renderer accepts.
    pub max_frames: u32,
    /// Added to MIDI-out timestamps (CLI `--midi-offset-ms`).
    pub midi_offset_ns: i64,
    /// Where `builtin:synth` destinations play.
    pub route: BuiltinRoute,
}

impl EngineConfig {
    /// Defaults: 4096-frame blocks, no MIDI offset, built-in routing.
    pub fn new(sample_rate: f64) -> EngineConfig {
        EngineConfig {
            sample_rate,
            max_frames: crate::backend::MAX_FRAMES,
            midi_offset_ns: 0,
            route: BuiltinRoute::Builtin,
        }
    }
}

/// The control handle.
pub struct Engine {
    cfg: EngineConfig,
    cmds: Producer<EngineCmd>,
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    targets: TargetTable,
    factory: Box<dyn SinkFactory>,
    tempo: Option<TempoTable>,
    length_frames: u64,
    loop_range: Option<(u64, u64)>,
    collector_stop: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    collector_paused: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    collector: Option<JoinHandle<()>>,
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for Engine {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("Engine")
            .field("cfg", &self.cfg)
            .field("targets", &self.targets)
            .finish_non_exhaustive()
    }
}

impl Engine {
    /// Creates the control handle and its renderer, and starts the collector
    /// thread that frees retired schedules every 10 ms.
    pub fn new(cfg: EngineConfig, factory: Box<dyn SinkFactory>) -> (Engine, Renderer) {
        let (cmd_tx, cmd_rx) = RingBuffer::<EngineCmd>::new(CMD_CAPACITY);
        let (retire_tx, mut retire_rx) = RingBuffer::<Box<Schedule>>::new(RETIRE_CAPACITY);
        let shared = Arc::new(Shared::default());
        let collector_stop = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
        let collector_paused = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
        let stop = Arc::clone(&collector_stop);
        let paused = Arc::clone(&collector_paused);
        let collector = std::thread::Builder::new()
            .name("vizdaw-collector".into())
            .spawn(move || {
                loop {
                    while !paused.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                        let Ok(old) = retire_rx.pop() else {
                            break;
                        };
                        drop(old);
                    }
                    if stop.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                        break;
                    }
                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
                }
            })
            .ok();
        let renderer = Renderer::new(
            RenderConfig {
                sample_rate: cfg.sample_rate,
                max_frames: cfg.max_frames,
                midi_offset_ns: cfg.midi_offset_ns,
            },
            cmd_rx,
            retire_tx,
            Arc::clone(&shared),
        );
        let engine = Engine {
            cfg,
            cmds: cmd_tx,
            shared,
            targets: TargetTable::new(),
            factory,
            tempo: None,
            length_frames: 0,
            loop_range: None,
            collector_stop,
            collector_paused,
            collector,
        };
        (engine, renderer)
    }

    /// Pauses or resumes freeing retired schedules (a diagnostic hook:
    /// tests use it to fill the retire ring).
    pub fn pause_collector(&self, paused: bool) {
        self.collector_paused.store(paused, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Settings.
    pub fn config(&self) -> EngineConfig {
        self.cfg
    }

    /// Targets created so far.
    pub fn targets(&self) -> &TargetTable {
        &self.targets
    }

    /// Compiles `seq` and sends the schedule (and sinks for new targets) to
    /// the renderer. Call again after every committed edit.
    pub fn load(&mut self, doc: &Document, seq: SeqId) -> Result<CompileReport, EngineError> {
        // Room for every possible new sink plus the schedule, so no push
        // below can fail after targets were interned.
        if self.cmds.slots() < MAX_TARGETS + 1 {
            return Err(EngineError::Busy);
        }
        let opts = CompileOptions {
            sample_rate: self.cfg.sample_rate,
            route: self.cfg.route,
            ports: self.factory.ports(),
        };
        let (sr, max) = (self.cfg.sample_rate, self.cfg.max_frames);
        let mut new_sinks: Vec<(TargetIdx, Sink)> = Vec::new();
        let targets = &mut self.targets;
        let factory = &mut self.factory;
        let mut resolve = |kind: &TargetKind| -> Result<Result<TargetIdx, String>, EngineError> {
            if let Some(i) = targets.find(kind) {
                return Ok(Ok(i));
            }
            if targets.len() >= MAX_TARGETS {
                return Err(EngineError::TooManyTargets);
            }
            match factory.make(kind, sr, max) {
                Ok(sink) => {
                    let i = targets.intern(kind.clone())?;
                    new_sinks.push((i, sink));
                    Ok(Ok(i))
                }
                Err(e) => Ok(Err(e.to_string())),
            }
        };
        let (schedule, report) = compile(doc, seq, &opts, &mut resolve)?;
        for (i, sink) in new_sinks {
            self.push(EngineCmd::AddSink(i, sink))?;
        }
        self.tempo = Some(schedule.tempo.clone());
        self.length_frames = schedule.length_frames;
        self.loop_range = schedule.loop_range;
        self.push(EngineCmd::SetSchedule(Box::new(schedule)))?;
        Ok(report)
    }

    fn push(&mut self, cmd: EngineCmd) -> Result<(), EngineError> {
        self.cmds.push(cmd).map_err(|_| EngineError::Busy)
    }

    /// Starts playback (from the start when at or past the end).
    pub fn play(&mut self) -> Result<(), EngineError> {
        self.push(EngineCmd::Play)
    }

    /// Stops playback; sounding notes get note-offs.
    pub fn stop(&mut self) -> Result<(), EngineError> {
        self.push(EngineCmd::Stop)
    }

    /// Moves to a schedule frame.
    pub fn locate_frame(&mut self, frame: u64) -> Result<(), EngineError> {
        self.push(EngineCmd::Locate(frame))
    }

    /// Moves to a tick of the loaded sequence.
    pub fn locate_tick(&mut self, tick: Tick) -> Result<(), EngineError> {
        let frame = self.tempo.as_ref().map_or(0, |t| t.tick_to_frame(tick));
        self.locate_frame(frame)
    }

    /// Loops bar 1 to the end of the loaded sequence, or stops looping.
    pub fn set_loop(&mut self, on: bool) -> Result<(), EngineError> {
        let range = if on { self.loop_range } else { None };
        self.push(EngineCmd::SetLoop(range))
    }

    /// Loops an explicit frame range, or stops looping.
    pub fn set_loop_range(&mut self, range: Option<(u64, u64)>) -> Result<(), EngineError> {
        self.push(EngineCmd::SetLoop(range))
    }

    /// Schedule frame of the transport.
    pub fn position_frames(&self) -> u64 {
        self.shared.position()
    }

    /// Tick of the transport, through the last loaded schedule's tempo table.
    pub fn position_tick(&self) -> Tick {
        let f = self.shared.position();
        self.tempo
            .as_ref()
            .map_or(Tick::ZERO, |t| t.frame_to_tick(f))
    }

    /// Frame of the loaded sequence's end.
    pub fn length_frames(&self) -> u64 {
        self.length_frames
    }

    /// Transport state.
    pub fn state(&self) -> PlayState {
        self.shared.state()
    }

    /// Reads and clears the fault bits.
    pub fn faults(&self) -> Faults {
        self.shared.take_faults()
    }

    /// Current counters.
    pub fn counters(&self) -> Counters {
        self.shared.counters()
    }

    /// True once after playback reached the end and the tail finished.
    pub fn take_ended(&self) -> bool {
        self.shared.take_ended()
    }
}

impl Drop for Engine {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.collector_stop.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
        if let Some(h) = self.collector.take() {
            let _ = h.join();
        }
    }
}

/// Finds a sequence by name.
pub fn find_sequence(doc: &Document, name: &str) -> Result<SeqId, EngineError> {
    doc.sequences()
        .values()
        .find(|s| s.name == name)
        .map(|s| s.id)
        .ok_or_else(|| EngineError::UnknownSequence(name.to_string()))
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/rt_check.rs`:

```rust
//! A counting global allocator for real-time checks. Install it with
//! `#[global_allocator] static A: CountingAlloc = CountingAlloc;` in a test
//! or binary; allocations are counted only on threads that are armed.

use std::alloc::{GlobalAlloc, Layout, System};
use std::cell::Cell;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};

static COUNT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);

thread_local! {
    static ARMED: Cell<bool> = const { Cell::new(false) };
}

/// Forwards to the system allocator and counts `alloc`, `dealloc` and
/// `realloc` made while the calling thread is armed.
#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct CountingAlloc;

fn note() {
    if ARMED.try_with(Cell::get).unwrap_or(false) {
        COUNT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }
}

// SAFETY: every method forwards to `System` with the caller's arguments.
unsafe impl GlobalAlloc for CountingAlloc {
    unsafe fn alloc(&self, layout: Layout) -> *mut u8 {
        note();
        // SAFETY: forwarded contract.
        unsafe { System.alloc(layout) }
    }

    unsafe fn dealloc(&self, ptr: *mut u8, layout: Layout) {
        note();
        // SAFETY: forwarded contract.
        unsafe { System.dealloc(ptr, layout) }
    }

    unsafe fn alloc_zeroed(&self, layout: Layout) -> *mut u8 {
        note();
        // SAFETY: forwarded contract.
        unsafe { System.alloc_zeroed(layout) }
    }

    unsafe fn realloc(&self, ptr: *mut u8, layout: Layout, new_size: usize) -> *mut u8 {
        note();
        // SAFETY: forwarded contract.
        unsafe { System.realloc(ptr, layout, new_size) }
    }
}

/// Starts counting on this thread.
pub fn arm() {
    let _ = ARMED.try_with(|a| a.set(true));
}

/// Stops counting on this thread.
pub fn disarm() {
    let _ = ARMED.try_with(|a| a.set(false));
}

/// Allocations counted so far (all threads).
pub fn count() -> u64 {
    COUNT.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
}
```

```rust
//! Platform-neutral backend traits. `platform-macos` implements them with
//! CoreAudio/CoreMIDI; the `offline` module implements them for tests.

use crate::error::BackendError;
use crate::render::Renderer;

/// Largest block any node or renderer is prepared for.
pub const MAX_FRAMES: u32 = 4096;

/// Timing of one block: its first frame reaches the output at
/// `host_time_ns`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct BlockTime {
    /// Host time (nanoseconds) at which the block's first frame is heard.
    pub host_time_ns: u64,
    /// Stream sample rate.
    pub sample_rate: f64,
}

/// What the caller wants from an audio device.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct AudioConfig {
    /// Device name; `None` follows the system default output.
    pub device: Option<String>,
    /// Requested buffer size in frames (32–2048); `None` keeps the device's.
    pub buffer_frames: Option<u32>,
}

/// What a backend opened.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct StreamInfo {
    /// Device display name.
    pub device_name: String,
    /// Nominal sample rate.
    pub sample_rate: f64,
    /// IO buffer size in frames.
    pub buffer_frames: u32,
    /// Output channel count.
    pub channels: u32,
}

/// Something that happened to the device, reported by
/// [`AudioBackend::take_events`].
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum BackendEvent {
    /// The device disappeared.
    DeviceLost,
    /// The system default output changed (only when following it).
    DefaultDeviceChanged,
    /// The nominal sample rate changed.
    SampleRateChanged(f64),
    /// The IO buffer size changed.
    BufferSizeChanged(u32),
}

/// An audio output device driving a [`Renderer`].
pub trait AudioBackend {
    /// Opens the device (not yet running).
    fn open(&mut self, cfg: &AudioConfig) -> Result<StreamInfo, BackendError>;
    /// Moves the renderer to the real-time thread and starts it.
    fn start(&mut self, renderer: Renderer) -> Result<(), BackendError>;
    /// Stops the device and hands the renderer back (never dropped on the
    /// real-time thread).
    fn stop(&mut self) -> Result<Renderer, BackendError>;
    /// Device events since the last call.
    fn take_events(&mut self) -> Vec<BackendEvent>;
}

/// Planar stereo scratch buffers, allocated once.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct StereoBuf {
    /// Left channel.
    pub left: Box<[f32]>,
    /// Right channel.
    pub right: Box<[f32]>,
}

impl StereoBuf {
    /// Zeroed buffers of `frames` samples per channel.
    pub fn new(frames: u32) -> StereoBuf {
        let n = frames as usize;
        StereoBuf {
            left: vec![0.0; n].into_boxed_slice(),
            right: vec![0.0; n].into_boxed_slice(),
        }
    }

    /// Capacity in frames.
    pub fn capacity(&self) -> u32 {
        u32::try_from(self.left.len().min(self.right.len())).unwrap_or(u32::MAX)
    }

    /// Zeroes the first `frames` samples of both channels. // RT
    pub fn clear(&mut self, frames: u32) {
        let n = (frames as usize).min(self.left.len()).min(self.right.len());
        if let (Some(l), Some(r)) = (self.left.get_mut(..n), self.right.get_mut(..n)) {
            l.fill(0.0);
            r.fill(0.0);
        }
    }
}

/// An instrument hosted inside the engine (the built-in synth, later
/// plugins). Every method is called on the real-time thread.
pub trait InstrumentNode: Send {
    /// Queues one MIDI 1.0 message (or a whole `F0 … F7` SysEx) at a frame
    /// offset within the next `render` call. // RT
    fn midi(&mut self, offset: u32, msg: &[u8]);
    /// Renders `frames` frames into `out`, replacing its contents. // RT
    fn render(&mut self, frames: u32, out: &mut StereoBuf);
    /// All notes off and controllers reset. // RT
    fn reset(&mut self);
}

/// A timestamped MIDI output. `send` must be wait-free.
pub trait MidiOut: Send {
    /// Queues `msg` for delivery at `host_time_ns`; `false` means it was
    /// dropped because the queue is full. // RT
    fn send(&mut self, host_time_ns: u64, msg: &[u8]) -> bool;
}

/// A MIDI destination visible to the system.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub struct MidiPortInfo {
    /// Display name.
    pub name: String,
    /// CoreMIDI unique ID, when known.
    pub unique_id: Option<i32>,
}

/// The system MIDI service.
pub trait MidiBackend {
    /// Destinations that can be opened.
    fn destinations(&self) -> Vec<MidiPortInfo>;
    /// Creates VizDAW's own virtual source.
    fn open_virtual_source(&mut self, name: &str) -> Result<Box<dyn MidiOut>, BackendError>;
    /// Opens an existing destination.
    fn open_destination(&mut self, port: &MidiPortInfo) -> Result<Box<dyn MidiOut>, BackendError>;
}
```

```rust
//! `core-engine`: compiles a core-model sequence into an immutable schedule
//! and plays it sample-accurately through platform backends, with a
//! real-time path that never blocks or allocates.
//!
//! Everything reachable from `render::Renderer::process` is marked
//! `// RT`; such code must not allocate, free, lock, do I/O or log.
#![deny(missing_docs)]
#![cfg_attr(
    not(test),
    deny(
        clippy::unwrap_used,
        clippy::expect_used,
        clippy::indexing_slicing,
        clippy::panic
    )
)]

pub mod backend;
pub mod engine;
pub mod error;
#[cfg(feature = "fixtures")]
pub mod fixtures;
pub mod midi1;
pub mod notes;
pub mod render;
pub mod rt_check;
pub mod schedule;
pub mod transport;

pub use error::{BackendError, EngineError};
```

### Task 8: Offline backend, event log and offline render tests

**Goal:** `OfflineBackend` (an `AudioBackend` driving the renderer on the caller's thread with any block sizes), recording sinks feeding an `EventLog` (Plan Ruling 13), and the spec §12 offline tests: snapshots of the fixtures, block-size independence (proptest), schedule swap mid-play, stop/locate, full MIDI queue, xrun and deferred swap.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/core-engine/src/offline.rs`
- Modify: `crates/core-engine/src/lib.rs`
- Test: `crates/core-engine/tests/offline.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Engine`, `SinkFactory`, `Renderer::process`, `Sink` (Task 7); `fixtures::{Builder, demo_document}` (Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `offline::LogRecord { frame: u64, target: TargetIdx, len: u16, data: [u8; 16] }` with `bytes()` and `line() -> String` (`"24000 t0 90 3c 40"`, `…` after 16 bytes); `offline::LOG_BYTES = 16`.
  - `offline::EventLog` (`Clone`): `drain() -> Vec<LogRecord>` sorted by `(frame, target)` (stable), `drain_text() -> String` (one line per record, `\n`-terminated).
  - `offline::OfflineSinks` (`SinkFactory`): `new(capacity_per_sink: usize)`, `with_ports(Vec<MidiPortInfo>)`, `log() -> EventLog`, `pub midi_accepts: bool`. `BuiltinSynth` → `RecordingNode` (silent; frame = frames rendered + offset); MIDI targets → `RecordingMidiOut` (frame = round(ns × rate / 1e9)).
  - `offline::OfflineBackend` (`AudioBackend`): `new(sample_rate, block)`, `frames() -> u64`, `reserve(max)`, `block(frames) -> f32` (peak), `run_frames(total)`, `run_blocks(sizes)`, `renderer_mut()`. Block n starts at host time `round(frames_so_far × 1e9 / rate)` ns.

```rust
use core_engine::backend::AudioBackend;
use core_engine::engine::{Engine, EngineConfig, find_sequence};
use core_engine::fixtures::{BAR, Builder, Q, demo_document};
use core_engine::offline::{EventLog, LogRecord, OfflineBackend, OfflineSinks};
use core_engine::render::Faults;
use core_engine::schedule::{BuiltinRoute, CompileReport};
use core_engine::transport::PlayState;
use core_model::document::{DestKind, Document};
use core_model::event::EventBody;
use core_model::ids::SeqId;
use proptest::prelude::*;

const SR: f64 = 48_000.0;

struct Run {
    engine: Engine,
    backend: OfflineBackend,
    log: EventLog,
    report: CompileReport,
}

fn start_with(
    doc: &Document,
    seq: SeqId,
    block: u32,
    cfg: EngineConfig,
    sinks: OfflineSinks,
) -> Run {
    let log = sinks.log();
    let (mut engine, renderer) = Engine::new(cfg, Box::new(sinks));
    let report = engine.load(doc, seq).unwrap();
    let mut backend = OfflineBackend::new(SR, block);
    backend.start(renderer).unwrap();
    Run {
        engine,
        backend,
        log,
        report,
    }
}

fn start(doc: &Document, seq: SeqId, block: u32) -> Run {
    start_with(
        doc,
        seq,
        block,
        EngineConfig::new(SR),
        OfflineSinks::new(100_000),
    )
}

impl Run {
    /// Plays until the end bit is set (tail included); returns the log.
    fn play_to_end(&mut self) -> String {
        self.engine.play().unwrap();
        for _ in 0..100_000 {
            self.backend.run_frames(512);
            if self.engine.take_ended() {
                return self.log.drain_text();
            }
        }
        panic!("never ended");
    }
}

fn one_bar() -> (Document, SeqId) {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("Bar", "Notes", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    b.notes(
        seq,
        lane,
        &[
            (0, 60, 0x8000, Q / 2),
            (Q, 62, 0x8000, Q / 2),
            (2 * Q, 64, 0x8000, Q / 2),
            (3 * Q, 65, 0x8000, Q / 2),
        ],
    )
    .unwrap();
    (b.finish(), seq)
}

/// Short, stable summary of a long log: counts per status and channel,
/// first and last lines, and an FNV-1a hash of the whole text.
fn digest(log: &str) -> String {
    let mut counts = std::collections::BTreeMap::<String, usize>::new();
    let mut hash: u64 = 0xcbf2_9ce4_8422_2325;
    for b in log.bytes() {
        hash ^= u64::from(b);
        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x0100_0000_01b3);
    }
    for line in log.lines() {
        let status = line.split(' ').nth(2).unwrap_or("?");
        *counts.entry(status.to_string()).or_default() += 1;
    }
    let lines: Vec<&str> = log.lines().collect();
    let mut s = format!("lines {} hash {hash:016x}\n", lines.len());
    for (k, v) in counts {
        s.push_str(&format!("{k}: {v}\n"));
    }
    s.push_str("first:\n");
    for l in lines.iter().take(8) {
        s.push_str(&format!("  {l}\n"));
    }
    s.push_str("last:\n");
    for l in lines.iter().rev().take(4).rev() {
        s.push_str(&format!("  {l}\n"));
    }
    s
}

#[test]
fn demo_plays_to_the_end() {
    let doc = demo_document().unwrap();
    let song = find_sequence(&doc, "Song").unwrap();
    let mut run = start(&doc, song, 512);
    assert!(run.report.unroutable.is_empty());
    assert!(run.report.warnings.is_empty(), "{:?}", run.report.warnings);
    let log = run.play_to_end();
    assert_eq!(run.engine.state(), PlayState::Stopped);
    assert!(run.engine.faults().is_empty());
    insta::assert_snapshot!(digest(&log), @r"
lines 335 hash 91aebc6c2ca3a33a
80: 36
81: 32
89: 96
90: 36
91: 32
99: 96
b0: 1
c0: 1
c1: 2
c9: 1
e0: 2
first:
  0 t0 c9 00
  0 t0 c1 21
  0 t0 c0 00
  0 t0 b0 07 64
  0 t0 99 2a 48
  0 t0 99 24 60
  0 t0 91 24 50
  0 t0 90 3c 38
last:
  1149589 t0 e0 00 40
  1176950 t0 80 37 00
  1176950 t0 80 3b 00
  1176950 t0 80 3e 00
");
}

#[test]
fn one_bar_at_120_bpm() {
    let (doc, seq) = one_bar();
    let mut run = start(&doc, seq, 512);
    insta::assert_snapshot!(run.play_to_end(), @r"
0 t0 90 3c 40
12000 t0 80 3c 00
24000 t0 90 3e 40
36000 t0 80 3e 00
48000 t0 90 40 40
60000 t0 80 40 00
72000 t0 90 41 40
84000 t0 80 41 00
");
}

#[test]
fn nested_reference_with_transpose_and_loop_count() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (inner, il) = b.sequence("Inner", "L", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    b.notes(inner, il, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q), (2 * Q, 64, 0x8000, Q)])
        .unwrap();
    let (mid, ml) = b.sequence("Mid", "Refs", None).unwrap();
    b.reference(mid, ml, 0, inner, 2, 2).unwrap();
    let (top, tl) = b.sequence("Top", "Refs", None).unwrap();
    b.reference(top, tl, 0, mid, 1, 0).unwrap();
    b.reference(top, tl, 2 * BAR, inner, 1, -12).unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let mut run = start(&doc, top, 512);
    insta::assert_snapshot!(run.play_to_end(), @r"
0 t0 90 3e 40
24000 t0 80 3e 00
48000 t0 90 42 40
72000 t0 80 42 00
96000 t0 90 3e 40
120000 t0 80 3e 00
144000 t0 90 42 40
168000 t0 80 42 00
192000 t0 90 30 40
216000 t0 80 30 00
240000 t0 90 34 40
264000 t0 80 34 00
");
}

#[test]
fn loop_wraps_three_times_with_note_offs() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("Loop", "L", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    // The second note lasts to the bar end, so it is still on at the wrap.
    b.notes(seq, lane, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q), (2 * Q, 67, 0x8000, 2 * Q)])
        .unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let mut run = start(&doc, seq, 500);
    run.engine.set_loop(true).unwrap();
    run.engine.play().unwrap();
    // Three wraps at 96000, 192000 and 288000, then stop at 300000.
    run.backend.run_frames(300_000);
    run.engine.stop().unwrap();
    run.backend.run_frames(500);
    assert_eq!(run.engine.state(), PlayState::Stopped);
    insta::assert_snapshot!(run.log.drain_text(), @r"
0 t0 90 3c 40
24000 t0 80 3c 00
48000 t0 90 43 40
96000 t0 80 43 00
96000 t0 90 3c 40
120000 t0 80 3c 00
144000 t0 90 43 40
192000 t0 80 43 00
192000 t0 90 3c 40
216000 t0 80 3c 00
240000 t0 90 43 40
288000 t0 80 43 00
288000 t0 90 3c 40
300000 t0 80 3c 00
");
}

#[test]
fn program_and_bank_change() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 5).unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("Patch", "L", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    b.event(
        seq,
        lane,
        0,
        EventBody::Program {
            program: 48,
            bank: Some((121, 1)),
        },
    )
    .unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, lane, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    b.event(
        seq,
        lane,
        2 * Q,
        EventBody::Program {
            program: 0,
            bank: None,
        },
    )
    .unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, lane, &[(2 * Q, 62, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let mut run = start(&doc, seq, 512);
    insta::assert_snapshot!(run.play_to_end(), @r"
0 t0 b5 00 79
0 t0 b5 20 01
0 t0 c5 30
0 t0 95 3c 40
24000 t0 85 3c 00
48000 t0 c5 00
48000 t0 95 3e 40
72000 t0 85 3e 00
");
}

#[test]
fn unroutable_plugin_is_reported_and_absent() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let pad = b
        .dest_kind(
            "Pad",
            DestKind::PluginInstrument {
                plugin_ref: "clap:pad".into(),
            },
            None,
        )
        .unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("S", "Synth", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, lane, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    let pl = b.lane(seq, "Pad", Some(pad)).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, pl, &[(0, 72, 0x8000, Q), (Q, 74, 0x8000, Q)])
        .unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let mut run = start(&doc, seq, 512);
    assert_eq!(run.report.unroutable.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(run.report.warnings, vec![
        "2 event(s) not played: Pad: plugin instrument \"clap:pad\" cannot play until plugin hosting exists".to_string()
    ]);
    insta::assert_snapshot!(run.play_to_end(), @r"
0 t0 90 3c 40
24000 t0 80 3c 00
");
}

#[test]
fn stop_and_locate_send_note_offs() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("S", "L", Some(synth)).unwrap();
    b.notes(seq, lane, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, 4 * Q)]).unwrap();
    b.event(
        seq,
        lane,
        0,
        EventBody::Cc {
            index: 64,
            value: 0xFFFF_FFFF,
        },
    )
    .unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let mut run = start(&doc, seq, 100);
    run.engine.play().unwrap();
    run.backend.run_frames(1_000);
    run.engine.stop().unwrap();
    run.backend.run_frames(100);
    run.engine.locate_frame(0).unwrap();
    run.engine.play().unwrap();
    run.backend.run_frames(1_000);
    run.engine
        .locate_tick(core_model::time::Tick(2 * Q))
        .unwrap();
    run.backend.run_frames(100);
    assert_eq!(run.engine.position_frames(), 48_100);
    assert_eq!(
        run.engine.position_tick(),
        core_model::time::Tick(2 * Q + 64)
    );
    insta::assert_snapshot!(run.log.drain_text(), @r"
0 t0 b0 40 7f
0 t0 90 3c 40
1000 t0 80 3c 00
1000 t0 b0 40 00
1100 t0 b0 40 7f
1100 t0 90 3c 40
2100 t0 80 3c 00
2100 t0 b0 40 00
");
}

#[test]
fn schedule_swap_mid_play_keeps_position_and_cuts_notes() {
    let build = |bpm: u32, second: u8| {
        let mut b = Builder::new();
        let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
        let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("S", "L", Some(synth)).unwrap();
        b.notes(
            seq,
            lane,
            &[(0, 60, 0x8000, BAR), (2 * Q, second, 0x8000, Q)],
        )
        .unwrap();
        b.tempo(seq, &[(0, bpm, false)]).unwrap();
        (b.finish(), seq)
    };
    let (old, seq) = build(120, 62);
    let mut run = start(&old, seq, 512);
    run.engine.play().unwrap();
    run.backend.run_frames(512 * 59);
    // Swap at device frame 30208: tick 30208 × 15360 / 24000 ≈ 19333 at
    // 120 BPM, which is frame 60416 at 60 BPM.
    assert_eq!(run.backend.frames(), 30_208);
    assert_eq!(run.engine.position_tick().0, 19_333);
    let (new, seq2) = build(60, 67);
    run.engine.load(&new, seq2).unwrap();
    run.backend.run_frames(512 * 100);
    assert_eq!(run.engine.position_frames(), 60_416 + 51_200);
    let log: Vec<String> = run.log.drain().iter().map(LogRecord::line).collect();
    assert_eq!(
        log,
        vec![
            // Old schedule only before the swap.
            "0 t0 90 3c 40".to_string(),
            // The sounding note is cut at the swap.
            "30208 t0 80 3c 00".to_string(),
            // New schedule from the same tick: beat 3 (tick 30720) is frame
            // 96000 at 60 BPM, i.e. 96000 − 60416 frames after the swap.
            "65792 t0 90 43 40".to_string(),
        ]
    );
}

#[test]
fn full_midi_queue_raises_a_fault() {
    let (doc, seq) = one_bar();
    let mut sinks = OfflineSinks::new(16);
    sinks.midi_accepts = false;
    let mut cfg = EngineConfig::new(SR);
    cfg.route = BuiltinRoute::Virtual;
    let mut run = start_with(&doc, seq, 512, cfg, sinks);
    run.engine.play().unwrap();
    run.backend.run_frames(48_000);
    assert!(run.engine.faults().contains(Faults::MIDI_DROPPED));
    assert_eq!(run.engine.counters().midi_dropped, 4);
    assert!(run.engine.faults().is_empty(), "faults clear on read");
}

#[test]
fn oversized_block_is_silence_and_an_xrun() {
    let (doc, seq) = one_bar();
    let mut cfg = EngineConfig::new(SR);
    cfg.max_frames = 256;
    let mut run = start_with(&doc, seq, 512, cfg, OfflineSinks::new(100));
    run.engine.play().unwrap();
    run.backend.run_blocks([512]);
    assert!(run.engine.faults().contains(Faults::XRUN));
    assert_eq!(run.engine.position_frames(), 0);
    run.backend.run_blocks([256]);
    assert_eq!(run.engine.position_frames(), 256);
}

#[test]
fn swap_waits_while_the_retire_ring_is_full() {
    let (doc, seq) = one_bar();
    let mut run = start(&doc, seq, 64);
    run.engine.pause_collector(true);
    run.backend.run_blocks([64]);
    for _ in 0..8 {
        run.engine.load(&doc, seq).unwrap();
        run.backend.run_blocks([64]);
    }
    assert!(run.engine.faults().is_empty());
    run.engine.load(&doc, seq).unwrap();
    run.backend.run_blocks([64]);
    assert!(run.engine.faults().contains(Faults::SWAP_DEFERRED));
    assert_eq!(run.engine.counters().swaps_deferred, 1);
    run.engine.pause_collector(false);
    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(50));
    run.backend.run_blocks([64]);
    assert!(run.engine.faults().is_empty());
}

fn arb_doc() -> impl Strategy<Value = Vec<(i64, u8, i64, bool)>> {
    prop::collection::vec((0i64..BAR, 30u8..90, 1i64..BAR, any::<bool>()), 1..24)
}

fn render_with_blocks(doc: &Document, seq: SeqId, sizes: &[u32], looped: bool) -> String {
    let mut run = start(doc, seq, 512);
    run.engine.set_loop(looped).unwrap();
    run.engine.play().unwrap();
    let total = 2 * 96_000 + 48_000 + 7;
    let mut done = 0u64;
    let mut i = 0usize;
    while done < total {
        let n = sizes[i % sizes.len()].min((total - done) as u32);
        run.backend.run_blocks([n]);
        done += u64::from(n);
        i += 1;
    }
    run.log.drain_text()
}

proptest! {
    #![proptest_config(ProptestConfig::with_cases(12))]

    #[test]
    fn logs_do_not_depend_on_block_size(
        notes in arb_doc(),
        base in prop::sample::select(vec![1u32, 7, 64, 512, 4096]),
        jitter in prop::collection::vec(0u32..4096, 1..16),
        looped in any::<bool>(),
    ) {
        let mut b = Builder::new();
        let synth = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
        let (seq, lane) = b.sequence("P", "L", Some(synth)).unwrap();
        for (t, p, d, cc) in &notes {
            b.notes(seq, lane, &[(*t, *p, 0x8000, *d)]).unwrap();
            if *cc {
                b.event(seq, lane, *t, EventBody::Cc { index: 1, value: u32::from(*p) << 25 }).unwrap();
            }
        }
        let doc = b.finish();
        let reference = render_with_blocks(&doc, seq, &[512], looped);
        let sizes: Vec<u32> = jitter.iter().map(|j| 1 + j % base).collect();
        prop_assert_eq!(render_with_blocks(&doc, seq, &sizes, looped), reference);
    }
}
```

Create `crates/core-engine/src/offline.rs`:

```rust
//! The offline backend: drives a [`Renderer`] on the calling thread with
//! chosen block sizes, and records every event its sinks receive.

use crate::backend::{
    AudioBackend, AudioConfig, BackendEvent, BlockTime, InstrumentNode, MidiOut, MidiPortInfo,
    StereoBuf, StreamInfo,
};
use crate::engine::SinkFactory;
use crate::error::BackendError;
use crate::render::{Renderer, Sink};
use crate::schedule::{TargetIdx, TargetKind};
use rtrb::{Consumer, Producer, RingBuffer};
use std::fmt::Write as _;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

/// Bytes kept per logged message (longer SysEx is truncated in the log).
pub const LOG_BYTES: usize = 16;

/// One logged message.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct LogRecord {
    /// Absolute output frame.
    pub frame: u64,
    /// Target.
    pub target: TargetIdx,
    /// Full message length.
    pub len: u16,
    /// The first [`LOG_BYTES`] bytes.
    pub data: [u8; LOG_BYTES],
}

impl LogRecord {
    fn new(frame: u64, target: TargetIdx, msg: &[u8]) -> LogRecord {
        let mut data = [0u8; LOG_BYTES];
        for (d, s) in data.iter_mut().zip(msg) {
            *d = *s;
        }
        LogRecord {
            frame,
            target,
            len: u16::try_from(msg.len()).unwrap_or(u16::MAX),
            data,
        }
    }

    /// The kept bytes.
    pub fn bytes(&self) -> &[u8] {
        let n = usize::from(self.len).min(LOG_BYTES);
        self.data.get(..n).unwrap_or(&[])
    }

    /// `frame tN hex bytes`, e.g. `24000 t0 90 3c 64`.
    pub fn line(&self) -> String {
        let mut s = format!("{} t{}", self.frame, self.target.0);
        for b in self.bytes() {
            let _ = write!(s, " {b:02x}");
        }
        if usize::from(self.len) > LOG_BYTES {
            s.push_str(" …");
        }
        s
    }
}

type Readers = Vec<(TargetIdx, Consumer<LogRecord>)>;

/// Collects the records of every recording sink (control side).
#[derive(Clone, Default)]
pub struct EventLog {
    readers: Arc<Mutex<Readers>>,
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for EventLog {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str("EventLog")
    }
}

impl EventLog {
    /// Removes and returns everything recorded so far, ordered by
    /// `(frame, target, arrival)`.
    pub fn drain(&self) -> Vec<LogRecord> {
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if let Ok(mut readers) = self.readers.lock() {
            for (_, rx) in readers.iter_mut() {
                while let Ok(r) = rx.pop() {
                    out.push(r);
                }
            }
        }
        out.sort_by_key(|r| (r.frame, r.target));
        out
    }

    /// [`EventLog::drain`] as text, one [`LogRecord::line`] per line.
    pub fn drain_text(&self) -> String {
        let mut s = String::new();
        for r in self.drain() {
            s.push_str(&r.line());
            s.push('\n');
        }
        s
    }

    fn add(&self, target: TargetIdx, capacity: usize) -> Producer<LogRecord> {
        let (tx, rx) = RingBuffer::new(capacity);
        if let Ok(mut readers) = self.readers.lock() {
            readers.push((target, rx));
        }
        tx
    }
}

/// An instrument that records what it receives and renders silence.
pub struct RecordingNode {
    tx: Producer<LogRecord>,
    target: TargetIdx,
    rendered: u64,
}

impl InstrumentNode for RecordingNode {
    fn midi(&mut self, offset: u32, msg: &[u8]) {
        let _ = self.tx.push(LogRecord::new(
            self.rendered + u64::from(offset),
            self.target,
            msg,
        ));
    }

    fn render(&mut self, frames: u32, out: &mut StereoBuf) {
        out.clear(frames);
        self.rendered += u64::from(frames);
    }

    fn reset(&mut self) {}
}

/// A MIDI output that records what it receives, converting host time back
/// to frames.
pub struct RecordingMidiOut {
    tx: Producer<LogRecord>,
    target: TargetIdx,
    sample_rate: f64,
    accept: bool,
}

impl MidiOut for RecordingMidiOut {
    fn send(&mut self, host_time_ns: u64, msg: &[u8]) -> bool {
        if !self.accept {
            return false;
        }
        let frame = (host_time_ns as f64 * self.sample_rate / 1e9).round() as u64;
        self.tx
            .push(LogRecord::new(frame, self.target, msg))
            .is_ok()
    }
}

/// Makes recording sinks that feed one [`EventLog`].
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct OfflineSinks {
    log: EventLog,
    capacity: usize,
    ports: Vec<MidiPortInfo>,
    /// When false, every MIDI output refuses messages (tests the
    /// midi-dropped fault).
    pub midi_accepts: bool,
}

impl OfflineSinks {
    /// Sinks whose logs hold `capacity` records each.
    pub fn new(capacity: usize) -> OfflineSinks {
        OfflineSinks {
            log: EventLog::default(),
            capacity,
            ports: Vec::new(),
            midi_accepts: true,
        }
    }

    /// Pretends these MIDI destinations exist.
    pub fn with_ports(mut self, ports: Vec<MidiPortInfo>) -> OfflineSinks {
        self.ports = ports;
        self
    }

    /// The shared log.
    pub fn log(&self) -> EventLog {
        self.log.clone()
    }
}

impl SinkFactory for OfflineSinks {
    fn ports(&self) -> Vec<MidiPortInfo> {
        self.ports.clone()
    }

    fn make(
        &mut self,
        kind: &TargetKind,
        sample_rate: f64,
        _max_frames: u32,
    ) -> Result<Sink, BackendError> {
        // The index this target will get is the number of sinks made so far.
        let target = TargetIdx(
            self.log
                .readers
                .lock()
                .map(|r| u8::try_from(r.len()).unwrap_or(u8::MAX))
                .unwrap_or(0),
        );
        let tx = self.log.add(target, self.capacity);
        Ok(match kind {
            TargetKind::BuiltinSynth => Sink::Instrument(Box::new(RecordingNode {
                tx,
                target,
                rendered: 0,
            })),
            TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource | TargetKind::MidiDestination(_) => {
                Sink::Midi(Box::new(RecordingMidiOut {
                    tx,
                    target,
                    sample_rate,
                    accept: self.midi_accepts,
                }))
            }
        })
    }
}

/// Drives a renderer without hardware. Block `n` starts at host time
/// `frames_so_far × 1e9 / sample_rate` ns.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct OfflineBackend {
    sample_rate: f64,
    block: u32,
    channels: usize,
    renderer: Option<Renderer>,
    frames: u64,
    left: Vec<f32>,
    right: Vec<f32>,
}

impl OfflineBackend {
    /// A backend at `sample_rate` whose default block is `block` frames.
    pub fn new(sample_rate: f64, block: u32) -> OfflineBackend {
        OfflineBackend {
            sample_rate,
            block,
            channels: 2,
            renderer: None,
            frames: 0,
            left: Vec::new(),
            right: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    /// Frames rendered so far.
    pub fn frames(&self) -> u64 {
        self.frames
    }

    /// Prepares output buffers for blocks up to `max` frames (non-RT).
    pub fn reserve(&mut self, max: u32) {
        let n = max as usize;
        if self.left.len() < n {
            self.left.resize(n, 0.0);
            self.right.resize(n, 0.0);
        }
    }

    /// Renders one block of `frames` frames; returns the peak absolute
    /// sample. Call [`OfflineBackend::reserve`] first for blocks larger than
    /// any seen before.
    pub fn block(&mut self, frames: u32) -> f32 {
        let Some(r) = self.renderer.as_mut() else {
            return 0.0;
        };
        let n = (frames as usize).min(self.left.len());
        let time = BlockTime {
            host_time_ns: (self.frames as f64 * 1e9 / self.sample_rate).round() as u64,
            sample_rate: self.sample_rate,
        };
        let (Some(l), Some(rt)) = (self.left.get_mut(..n), self.right.get_mut(..n)) else {
            return 0.0;
        };
        let mut chans: [&mut [f32]; 2] = [l, rt];
        r.process(&mut chans, frames, time);
        self.frames += u64::from(frames);
        chans
            .iter()
            .flat_map(|c| c.iter())
            .fold(0.0f32, |m, x| m.max(x.abs()))
    }

    /// Renders `total` frames in blocks of the default size.
    pub fn run_frames(&mut self, total: u64) {
        self.reserve(self.block);
        let mut left = total;
        while left > 0 {
            let n = u32::try_from(left.min(u64::from(self.block))).unwrap_or(self.block);
            self.block(n);
            left -= u64::from(n);
        }
    }

    /// Renders blocks of the given sizes.
    pub fn run_blocks(&mut self, sizes: impl IntoIterator<Item = u32>) {
        for n in sizes {
            self.reserve(n);
            self.block(n);
        }
    }

    /// Mutable access to the running renderer (not RT; for tests).
    pub fn renderer_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut Renderer> {
        self.renderer.as_mut()
    }
}

impl AudioBackend for OfflineBackend {
    fn open(&mut self, _cfg: &AudioConfig) -> Result<StreamInfo, BackendError> {
        Ok(StreamInfo {
            device_name: "offline".into(),
            sample_rate: self.sample_rate,
            buffer_frames: self.block,
            channels: u32::try_from(self.channels).unwrap_or(2),
        })
    }

    fn start(&mut self, renderer: Renderer) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
        self.reserve(self.block.max(renderer.config().max_frames));
        self.renderer = Some(renderer);
        Ok(())
    }

    fn stop(&mut self) -> Result<Renderer, BackendError> {
        self.renderer.take().ok_or(BackendError::NoDevice)
    }

    fn take_events(&mut self) -> Vec<BackendEvent> {
        Vec::new()
    }
}
```

### Task 9: Real-time allocation test

**Goal:** Spec §12 `tests/rt_alloc.rs`: with `rt_check::CountingAlloc` as the global allocator, armed only around render blocks, 10k blocks of play/stop/locate/loop, 100 schedule swaps including deferred ones, blocks larger than `max_frames` and a full MIDI queue record zero allocations (Plan Ruling 26).

**Files:**
- Test: `crates/core-engine/tests/rt_alloc.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `rt_check::{CountingAlloc, arm, disarm, count}`, `Engine::{pause_collector, counters, faults}` (Task 7); `OfflineBackend::{block, reserve}`, `OfflineSinks` (Task 8); `fixtures::demo_document` (Task 4).
- Produces: nothing new (a test).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

Create `crates/core-engine/tests/rt_alloc.rs`:

```rust
//! Counts allocations made on the render path: the counting allocator is
//! armed only around `OfflineBackend::block`, which only calls
//! `Renderer::process`.

use core_engine::backend::AudioBackend;
use core_engine::engine::{Engine, EngineConfig, find_sequence};
use core_engine::fixtures::demo_document;
use core_engine::offline::{OfflineBackend, OfflineSinks};
use core_engine::render::Faults;
use core_engine::rt_check::{self, CountingAlloc};
use core_engine::schedule::BuiltinRoute;
use core_model::time::Tick;

#[global_allocator]
static ALLOC: CountingAlloc = CountingAlloc;

fn armed_block(b: &mut OfflineBackend, frames: u32) {
    rt_check::arm();
    b.block(frames);
    rt_check::disarm();
}

/// One test, so no other test thread allocates while the global counter
/// is compared.
#[test]
fn render_path_never_allocates() {
    // The counter works: one allocation and one free.
    let before = rt_check::count();
    rt_check::arm();
    let v: Vec<u8> = Vec::with_capacity(10);
    drop(v);
    rt_check::disarm();
    assert_eq!(rt_check::count() - before, 2);

    let doc = demo_document().unwrap();
    let song = find_sequence(&doc, "Song").unwrap();
    let before = rt_check::count();

    // 10k blocks of play, stop, locate and loop wraps on the built-in
    // synth plus a MIDI output (route both).
    let mut cfg = EngineConfig::new(48_000.0);
    cfg.route = BuiltinRoute::Both;
    let sinks = OfflineSinks::new(1 << 20);
    let log = sinks.log();
    let (mut engine, renderer) = Engine::new(cfg, Box::new(sinks));
    engine.load(&doc, song).unwrap();
    let mut b = OfflineBackend::new(48_000.0, 512);
    b.start(renderer).unwrap();
    b.reserve(4096);
    engine.set_loop(true).unwrap();
    engine.play().unwrap();
    for i in 0..10_000u32 {
        match i % 1000 {
            300 => engine.stop().unwrap(),
            310 => engine.play().unwrap(),
            500 => engine.locate_tick(Tick(15_360 * 40)).unwrap(),
            700 => engine.set_loop(false).unwrap(),
            900 => engine.set_loop(true).unwrap(),
            _ => {}
        }
        armed_block(&mut b, 1 + (i * 37) % 1024);
        if i % 100 == 0 {
            log.drain();
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(rt_check::count() - before, 0, "allocations while playing");

    // 100 schedule swaps; with the collector paused the retire ring
    // (capacity 8) fills and the ninth swap is deferred.
    engine.pause_collector(true);
    for i in 0..100 {
        if i == 12 {
            engine.pause_collector(false);
            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(50));
        }
        engine.load(&doc, song).unwrap();
        armed_block(&mut b, 256);
    }
    assert!(engine.counters().swaps_deferred >= 1);
    assert_eq!(rt_check::count() - before, 0, "allocations during swaps");

    // Blocks larger than the prepared size.
    armed_block(&mut b, 8192);
    assert!(engine.faults().contains(Faults::XRUN));
    assert_eq!(rt_check::count() - before, 0, "allocations on xrun");
    drop(b.stop().unwrap());
    drop(engine);

    // A full MIDI queue drops instead of allocating.
    let mut cfg = EngineConfig::new(48_000.0);
    cfg.route = BuiltinRoute::Virtual;
    let (mut engine, renderer) = Engine::new(cfg, Box::new(OfflineSinks::new(8)));
    engine.load(&doc, song).unwrap();
    let mut b = OfflineBackend::new(48_000.0, 512);
    b.start(renderer).unwrap();
    engine.play().unwrap();
    for _ in 0..2_000 {
        armed_block(&mut b, 512);
    }
    assert!(engine.counters().midi_dropped > 0);
    assert_eq!(
        rt_check::count() - before,
        0,
        "allocations with a full MIDI queue"
    );
}
```

Create `crates/platform-macos/tests/dls.rs`:

```rust
#![cfg(target_os = "macos")]

use core_engine::backend::{InstrumentNode, StereoBuf};
use platform_macos::dls::DlsSynth;

fn rms_dbfs(buf: &StereoBuf, frames: usize) -> f64 {
    let sum: f64 = buf.left[..frames]
        .iter()
        .chain(&buf.right[..frames])
        .map(|x| f64::from(*x) * f64::from(*x))
        .sum();
    let rms = (sum / (2 * frames) as f64).sqrt();
    20.0 * rms.max(1e-12).log10()
}

#[test]
fn dls_renders_sound() {
    let mut synth = DlsSynth::new(48_000.0, 4096).unwrap();
    let mut buf = StereoBuf::new(512);
    synth.midi(0, &[0xC0, 0]); // Acoustic Grand Piano
    synth.midi(10, &[0x90, 60, 100]);
    let mut loudest = f64::NEG_INFINITY;
    for _ in 0..20 {
        synth.render(512, &mut buf);
        loudest = loudest.max(rms_dbfs(&buf, 512));
    }
    assert!(loudest > -40.0, "note too quiet: {loudest:.1} dBFS");

    // The DLS synth's built-in reverb tail is still about -78 dBFS after
    // 2 s, so the decay check waits 3 s.
    synth.midi(0, &[0x80, 60, 0]);
    let mut last = 0.0;
    for _ in 0..(3 * 48_000u32).div_ceil(512) {
        synth.render(512, &mut buf);
        last = rms_dbfs(&buf, 512);
    }
    assert!(last < -80.0, "note did not decay: {last:.1} dBFS");
}

#[test]
fn reset_silences_held_notes() {
    let mut synth = DlsSynth::new(44_100.0, 4096).unwrap();
    let mut buf = StereoBuf::new(1024);
    synth.midi(0, &[0x90, 64, 110]);
    synth.render(1024, &mut buf);
    synth.reset();
    for _ in 0..(3 * 44_100 / 1024) {
        synth.render(1024, &mut buf);
    }
    assert!(rms_dbfs(&buf, 1024) < -80.0);
}
```

```rust
//! `platform-macos`: CoreAudio HAL output, CoreMIDI output and Apple's DLS
//! synth as the built-in synth, implementing `core-engine`'s backend traits.
//!
//! All `unsafe` lives in `hal.rs`, `dls.rs` and `midi.rs`, with a
//! `// SAFETY:` comment per block.
#![cfg(target_os = "macos")]
#![deny(missing_docs)]
#![cfg_attr(
    not(test),
    deny(
        clippy::unwrap_used,
        clippy::expect_used,

)]

pub mod dls;
```

Create `crates/platform-macos/src/dls.rs`:

```rust
//! Apple's `DLSMusicDevice` (the system GM bank) as an
//! [`InstrumentNode`].

use core_engine::BackendError;
use core_engine::backend::{InstrumentNode, StereoBuf};
use objc2_audio_toolbox::{
    AudioComponentDescription, AudioComponentFindNext, AudioComponentInstanceDispose,
    AudioComponentInstanceNew, AudioUnit, AudioUnitInitialize, AudioUnitRender,
    AudioUnitRenderActionFlags, AudioUnitSetProperty, AudioUnitUninitialize, MusicDeviceMIDIEvent,
    MusicDeviceSysEx, kAudioUnitManufacturer_Apple, kAudioUnitProperty_MaximumFramesPerSlice,
    kAudioUnitProperty_StreamFormat, kAudioUnitScope_Global, kAudioUnitScope_Output,
    kAudioUnitSubType_DLSSynth, kAudioUnitType_MusicDevice,
};
use objc2_core_audio_types::{
    AudioBuffer, AudioBufferList, AudioStreamBasicDescription, AudioTimeStamp, AudioTimeStampFlags,
    SMPTETime, SMPTETimeFlags, SMPTETimeType, kAudioFormatFlagIsFloat,
    kAudioFormatFlagIsNonInterleaved, kAudioFormatFlagIsPacked, kAudioFormatLinearPCM,
};
use std::ffi::c_void;
use std::ptr::{self, NonNull};

/// An `AudioBufferList` with room for two buffers (the C type declares a
/// one-element trailing array).
#[repr(C)]
struct StereoBufferList {
    count: u32,
    buffers: [AudioBuffer; 2],
}

fn os(call: &'static str, status: i32) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
    if status == 0 {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(BackendError::Os { call, status })
    }
}

/// The built-in synth: one `DLSMusicDevice` serving all 16 channels.
pub struct DlsSynth {
    unit: AudioUnit,
    sample_time: f64,
    max_frames: u32,
}

// SAFETY: the AudioUnit is owned by this value and used by one thread at a
// time (created on the control thread, then moved to the render thread).
unsafe impl Send for DlsSynth {}

impl DlsSynth {
    /// Instantiates and initializes the synth for stereo non-interleaved f32
    /// output at `sample_rate`, rendering at most `max_frames` per call.
    pub fn new(sample_rate: f64, max_frames: u32) -> Result<DlsSynth, BackendError> {
        let mut desc = AudioComponentDescription {
            componentType: kAudioUnitType_MusicDevice,
            componentSubType: kAudioUnitSubType_DLSSynth,
            componentManufacturer: kAudioUnitManufacturer_Apple,
            componentFlags: 0,
            componentFlagsMask: 0,
        };
        // SAFETY: `desc` is a valid description; a null component starts the
        // search from the beginning.
        let comp = unsafe { AudioComponentFindNext(ptr::null_mut(), NonNull::from(&mut desc)) };
        if comp.is_null() {
            return Err(BackendError::ComponentMissing);
        }
        let mut unit: AudioUnit = ptr::null_mut();
        // SAFETY: `comp` is a valid component and `unit` a valid out-pointer.
        os("AudioComponentInstanceNew", unsafe {
            AudioComponentInstanceNew(comp, NonNull::from(&mut unit))
        })?;
        let synth = DlsSynth {
            unit,
            sample_time: 0.0,
            max_frames,
        };
        let fmt = AudioStreamBasicDescription {
            mSampleRate: sample_rate,
            mFormatID: kAudioFormatLinearPCM,
            mFormatFlags: kAudioFormatFlagIsFloat
                | kAudioFormatFlagIsPacked
                | kAudioFormatFlagIsNonInterleaved,
            mBytesPerPacket: 4,
            mFramesPerPacket: 1,
            mBytesPerFrame: 4,
            mChannelsPerFrame: 2,
            mBitsPerChannel: 32,
            mReserved: 0,
        };
        // SAFETY: `fmt` outlives the call and the size matches its type.
        os("AudioUnitSetProperty(StreamFormat)", unsafe {
            AudioUnitSetProperty(
                synth.unit,
                kAudioUnitProperty_StreamFormat,
                kAudioUnitScope_Output,
                0,
                (&raw const fmt).cast::<c_void>(),
                size_of::<AudioStreamBasicDescription>() as u32,
            )
        })?;
        // SAFETY: as above, for a u32 property.
        os("AudioUnitSetProperty(MaximumFramesPerSlice)", unsafe {
            AudioUnitSetProperty(
                synth.unit,
                kAudioUnitProperty_MaximumFramesPerSlice,
                kAudioUnitScope_Global,
                0,
                (&raw const max_frames).cast::<c_void>(),
                size_of::<u32>() as u32,
            )
        })?;
        // SAFETY: the unit is configured and not yet initialized.
        os("AudioUnitInitialize", unsafe {
            AudioUnitInitialize(synth.unit)
        })?;
        Ok(synth)
    }
}

impl Drop for DlsSynth {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        // SAFETY: the unit was created by `AudioComponentInstanceNew` and is
        // not used after this.
        unsafe {
            AudioUnitUninitialize(self.unit);
            AudioComponentInstanceDispose(self.unit);
        }
    }
}

impl InstrumentNode for DlsSynth {
    fn midi(&mut self, offset: u32, msg: &[u8]) {
        match msg {
            [0xF0, ..] => {
                let Ok(len) = u32::try_from(msg.len()) else {
                    return;
                };
                // SAFETY: `msg` is valid for `len` bytes during the call.
                // SysEx has no frame offset and lands at the block start.
                unsafe {
                    MusicDeviceSysEx(self.unit, NonNull::from(msg).cast::<u8>(), len);
                }
            }
            [status, rest @ ..] => {
                let d1 = rest.first().copied().unwrap_or(0);
                let d2 = rest.get(1).copied().unwrap_or(0);
                // SAFETY: plain values; the unit is initialized. // RT
                unsafe {
                    MusicDeviceMIDIEvent(
                        self.unit,
                        u32::from(*status),
                        u32::from(d1),
                        u32::from(d2),
                        offset,
                    );
                }
            }
            [] => {}
        }
    }

    fn render(&mut self, frames: u32, out: &mut StereoBuf) {
        let n = frames.min(self.max_frames).min(out.capacity());
        let bytes = n * 4;
        let mut list = StereoBufferList {
            count: 2,
            buffers: [
                AudioBuffer {
                    mNumberChannels: 1,
                    mDataByteSize: bytes,
                    mData: out.left.as_mut_ptr().cast::<c_void>(),
                },
                AudioBuffer {
                    mNumberChannels: 1,
                    mDataByteSize: bytes,
                    mData: out.right.as_mut_ptr().cast::<c_void>(),
                },
            ],
        };
        let mut stamp = AudioTimeStamp {
            mSampleTime: self.sample_time,
            mHostTime: 0,
            mRateScalar: 0.0,
            mWordClockTime: 0,
            mSMPTETime: SMPTETime {
                mSubframes: 0,
                mSubframeDivisor: 0,
                mCounter: 0,
                mType: SMPTETimeType(0),
                mFlags: SMPTETimeFlags(0),
                mHours: 0,
                mMinutes: 0,
                mSeconds: 0,
                mFrames: 0,
            },
            mFlags: AudioTimeStampFlags::SampleTimeValid,
            mReserved: 0,
        };
        let mut flags = AudioUnitRenderActionFlags(0);
        // SAFETY: `list` describes two buffers of `n` f32 samples owned by
        // `out`, valid for the call; `stamp` and `flags` are valid. // RT
        let status = unsafe {
            AudioUnitRender(
                self.unit,
                &mut flags,
                NonNull::from(&mut stamp),
                0,
                n,
                NonNull::from(&mut list).cast::<AudioBufferList>(),
            )
        };
        self.sample_time += f64::from(n);
        if status != 0 {
            out.clear(frames);
            return;
        }
        // If the unit substituted its own buffers, copy from them.
        for (buf, dst) in list.buffers.iter().zip([&mut out.left, &mut out.right]) {
            let ours = dst.as_mut_ptr().cast::<c_void>();
            if buf.mData != ours && !buf.mData.is_null() {
                // SAFETY: the unit guarantees `mData` holds `n` samples until
                // the next render call. // RT
                let src =
                    unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(buf.mData.cast::<f32>(), n as usize) };
                if let Some(d) = dst.get_mut(..n as usize) {
                    d.copy_from_slice(src);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fn reset(&mut self) {
        for ch in 0..16u8 {
            self.midi(0, &[0xB0 | ch, 123, 0]);
            self.midi(0, &[0xB0 | ch, 121, 0]);
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 11: CoreMIDI output with a sender thread

**Goal:** Spec §9 `midi.rs`: one `MIDIClient`, the "VizDAW Out" virtual source (`MIDISourceCreateWithProtocol(1.0)` + `MIDIReceivedEventList`) and named destinations (`MIDIOutputPortCreate` + `MIDISendEventList`); `MidiOut::send` is a wait-free push of UMP-sized packets into a 4096-slot `rtrb` ring, drained every 1 ms by a sender thread that packs MIDI 1.0 channel voice (UMP type 2) and SysEx7 (type 3) into `MIDIEventList`s with host-time timestamps (Plan Rulings 19–20). `MidiMonitor` listens to our own virtual source for the round-trip test.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/platform-macos/src/midi.rs`
- Modify: `crates/platform-macos/src/lib.rs`
- Test: `crates/platform-macos/tests/midi.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `core_engine::backend::{MidiBackend, MidiOut, MidiPortInfo}` (Task 1), `core_engine::schedule::targets::find_port` (Task 3); `objc2_core_midi` client/port/endpoint/event-list API; `objc2_core_audio::{AudioConvertNanosToHostTime, AudioConvertHostTimeToNanos, AudioGetCurrentHostTime}`; `block2::RcBlock`.
- Produces:
  - `midi::MIDI_RING_CAPACITY = 4096`; `midi::RingMidiOut` (`MidiOut`; `send` returns `false` when the ring lacks room for the whole message).
  - `midi::MidiSystem` (`MidiBackend`): `MidiSystem::new(client_name: &str) -> Result<MidiSystem, BackendError>`; `open_virtual_source(name)` reuses an existing source of the same name; `Drop` stops the sender and disposes everything.
  - `midi::list_destinations() -> Vec<MidiPortInfo>` (no client needed).
  - `midi::MidiMonitor::watch(&MidiSystem, source_name: &str) -> Result<MidiMonitor, BackendError>`, `take(&self) -> Vec<Received>`; `midi::Received { host_time: u64, host_ns: u64, bytes: Vec<u8> }`.
  - `midi::ns_to_host(ns: u64) -> u64`, `midi::now_ns() -> u64`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `crates/platform-macos/tests/midi.rs`:

```rust
#![cfg(target_os = "macos")]

use core_engine::backend::MidiBackend;
use platform_macos::midi::{MidiMonitor, MidiSystem, now_ns, ns_to_host};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

#[test]
fn virtual_source_roundtrip() {
    let mut sys = MidiSystem::new("VizDAW Test").unwrap();
    let mut out = sys.open_virtual_source("VizDAW Test Out").unwrap();
    let monitor = MidiMonitor::watch(&sys, "VizDAW Test Out").unwrap();
    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50));

    let t0 = now_ns() + 20_000_000; // 20 ms ahead, like a block's output time
    let mut sent = vec![];
    for i in 0..100u64 {
        let at = t0 + i * 1_000_000;
        let msg: Vec<u8> = match i % 4 {
            0 => vec![0x90, (40 + i % 40) as u8, 100],
            1 => vec![0x80, (40 + i % 40) as u8, 0],
            2 => vec![0xC3, (i % 128) as u8],
            _ => vec![0xB1, 7, (i % 128) as u8],
        };
        assert!(out.send(at, &msg));
        sent.push((at, msg));
    }
    let sysex = vec![0xF0, 0x7E, 0x7F, 0x09, 0x01, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 0xF7];
    let at = t0 + 200_000_000;
    assert!(out.send(at, &sysex));
    sent.push((at, sysex));

    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(3);
    let mut got = vec![];
    while got.len() < sent.len() && Instant::now() < deadline {
        got.extend(monitor.take());
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5));
    }
    let got: Vec<(u64, Vec<u8>)> = got.into_iter().map(|r| (r.host_time, r.bytes)).collect();
    // Same bytes, same order, and exactly the requested host times.
    let want: Vec<(u64, Vec<u8>)> = sent
        .into_iter()
        .map(|(ns, b)| (ns_to_host(ns), b))
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(got, want);
}

#[test]
fn destinations_can_be_listed() {
    let sys = MidiSystem::new("VizDAW List").unwrap();
    for d in sys.destinations() {
        assert!(!d.name.is_empty());
    }
}
```
