- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `crates/platform-macos/src/midi.rs`:

```rust
//! CoreMIDI output: VizDAW's virtual source and existing destinations.
//!
//! `MidiOut::send` is wait-free: it pushes MIDI 1.0 UMP-sized packets into
//! an `rtrb` ring. A sender thread drains every ring each millisecond and
//! hands the packets to CoreMIDI (`MIDIReceivedEventList` for the virtual
//! source, `MIDISendEventList` for destinations) with their host-time
//! timestamps, so the IPC never happens on the audio thread.

use block2::RcBlock;
use core_engine::BackendError;
use core_engine::backend::{MidiBackend, MidiOut, MidiPortInfo};
use core_engine::schedule::targets::find_port;
use objc2_core_audio::{AudioConvertHostTimeToNanos, AudioConvertNanosToHostTime};
use objc2_core_foundation::{CFRetained, CFString};
use objc2_core_midi::{
    MIDIClientCreate, MIDIClientDispose, MIDIClientRef, MIDIEndpointDispose, MIDIEndpointRef,
    MIDIEventList, MIDIEventPacket, MIDIGetDestination, MIDIGetNumberOfDestinations,
    MIDIInputPortCreateWithProtocol, MIDIObjectGetIntegerProperty, MIDIObjectGetStringProperty,
    MIDIObjectRef, MIDIOutputPortCreate, MIDIPortConnectSource, MIDIPortDispose, MIDIPortRef,
    MIDIProtocolID, MIDIReceivedEventList, MIDISendEventList, MIDISourceCreateWithProtocol,
    kMIDIPropertyName, kMIDIPropertyUniqueID,
};
use rtrb::{Consumer, Producer, RingBuffer};
use std::ffi::c_void;
use std::ptr::{self, NonNull};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::thread::JoinHandle;
use std::time::Duration;

/// Packets per output ring.
pub const MIDI_RING_CAPACITY: usize = 4096;

/// Packet kinds: a MIDI 1.0 channel-voice message (UMP type 2) or one
/// 6-byte SysEx7 chunk (UMP type 3) with its status.
const KIND_CHANNEL: u8 = 0x20;
const KIND_SYSEX: u8 = 0x30;
const SYSEX_COMPLETE: u8 = 0;
const SYSEX_START: u8 = 1;
const SYSEX_CONTINUE: u8 = 2;
const SYSEX_END: u8 = 3;

/// One ring entry: at most one UMP's worth of MIDI 1.0 data.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
struct Packet {
    host_ns: u64,
    kind: u8,
    len: u8,
    data: [u8; 6],
}

impl Packet {
    /// The UMP words of this packet on group 0.
    fn words(&self) -> ([u32; 2], u32) {
        let d = self.data;
        if self.kind == KIND_CHANNEL {
            let w =
                (0x2 << 28) | (u32::from(d[0]) << 16) | (u32::from(d[1]) << 8) | u32::from(d[2]);
            return ([w, 0], 1);
        }
        let status = u32::from(self.kind & 0x0F);
        let w0 = (0x3 << 28)
            | (status << 20)
            | (u32::from(self.len) << 16)
            | (u32::from(d[0]) << 8)
            | u32::from(d[1]);
        let w1 = (u32::from(d[2]) << 24)
            | (u32::from(d[3]) << 16)
            | (u32::from(d[4]) << 8)
            | u32::from(d[5]);
        ([w0, w1], 2)
    }
}

/// The wait-free side of an output.
pub struct RingMidiOut {
    tx: Producer<Packet>,
}

impl MidiOut for RingMidiOut {
    fn send(&mut self, host_time_ns: u64, msg: &[u8]) -> bool {
        match msg {
            [0xF0, rest @ ..] => {
                let payload = match rest {
                    [body @ .., 0xF7] => body,
                    body => body,
                };
                let n = payload.len().div_ceil(6).max(1);
                if self.tx.slots() < n {
                    return false;
                }
                let mut chunks = payload.chunks(6);
                for i in 0..n {
                    let chunk = chunks.next().unwrap_or(&[]);
                    let status = match (n, i) {
                        (1, _) => SYSEX_COMPLETE,
                        (_, 0) => SYSEX_START,
                        (_, i) if i + 1 == n => SYSEX_END,
                        _ => SYSEX_CONTINUE,
                    };
                    let mut data = [0u8; 6];
                    for (d, s) in data.iter_mut().zip(chunk) {
                        *d = *s & 0x7F;
                    }
                    let p = Packet {
                        host_ns: host_time_ns,
                        kind: KIND_SYSEX | status,
                        len: chunk.len() as u8,
                        data,
                    };
                    if self.tx.push(p).is_err() {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                true
            }
            [status, rest @ ..] if *status >= 0x80 => {
                let mut data = [0u8; 6];
                data[0] = *status;
                for (d, s) in data.iter_mut().skip(1).zip(rest.iter().take(2)) {
                    *d = *s;
                }
                let p = Packet {
                    host_ns: host_time_ns,
                    kind: KIND_CHANNEL,
                    len: msg.len().min(3) as u8,
                    data,
                };
                self.tx.push(p).is_ok()
            }
            _ => true,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
enum Dest {
    /// Our virtual source: `MIDIReceivedEventList`.
    Source(MIDIEndpointRef),
    /// A destination through our output port: `MIDISendEventList`.
    Port(MIDIPortRef, MIDIEndpointRef),
}

struct Lane {
    rx: Consumer<Packet>,
    dest: Dest,
}

#[derive(Default)]
struct SenderState {
    lanes: Mutex<Vec<Lane>>,
    stop: AtomicBool,
}

fn os(call: &'static str, status: i32) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
    if status == 0 {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(BackendError::Os { call, status })
    }
}

/// Sends one packet as a one-packet `MIDIEventList`.
fn deliver(dest: Dest, p: &Packet) {
    let (w, count) = p.words();
    let mut words = [0u32; 64];
    words[0] = w[0];
    words[1] = w[1];
    let mut list = MIDIEventList {
        protocol: MIDIProtocolID::Protocol_1_0,
        numPackets: 1,
        packet: [MIDIEventPacket {
            timeStamp: ns_to_host(p.host_ns),
            wordCount: count,
            words,
        }],
    };
    let list_ptr = NonNull::from(&mut list);
    // SAFETY: `list` is a valid one-packet event list for the duration of
    // the call; the endpoint and port belong to our live client.
    unsafe {
        match dest {
            Dest::Source(src) => {
                MIDIReceivedEventList(src, list_ptr);
            }
            Dest::Port(port, endpoint) => {
                MIDISendEventList(port, endpoint, list_ptr);
            }
        }
    }
}

fn sender_loop(state: Arc<SenderState>) {
    while !state.stop.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
        if let Ok(mut lanes) = state.lanes.lock() {
            for lane in lanes.iter_mut() {
                while let Ok(p) = lane.rx.pop() {
                    deliver(lane.dest, &p);
                }
            }
        }
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(1));
    }
}

fn cfstring_property(obj: MIDIObjectRef, key: &CFString) -> Option<String> {
    let mut out: *const CFString = ptr::null();
    // SAFETY: `out` is a valid out-pointer; on success CoreMIDI returns a
    // +1 retained string, which `CFRetained::from_raw` takes over.
    let status = unsafe { MIDIObjectGetStringProperty(obj, key, NonNull::from(&mut out)) };
    let p = NonNull::new(out.cast_mut())?;
    if status != 0 {
        return None;
    }
    // SAFETY: see above.
    let s: CFRetained<CFString> = unsafe { CFRetained::from_raw(p) };
    Some(s.to_string())
}

fn int_property(obj: MIDIObjectRef, key: &CFString) -> Option<i32> {
    let mut v = 0i32;
    // SAFETY: `v` is a valid out-pointer.
    let status = unsafe { MIDIObjectGetIntegerProperty(obj, key, NonNull::from(&mut v)) };
    (status == 0).then_some(v)
}

fn destination_endpoints() -> Vec<(MIDIEndpointRef, MidiPortInfo)> {
    // SAFETY: plain queries of the MIDI server.
    let n = unsafe { MIDIGetNumberOfDestinations() };
    (0..n)
        .filter_map(|i| {
            // SAFETY: `i` is below the count just read.
            let e = unsafe { MIDIGetDestination(i) };
            if e == 0 {
                return None;
            }
            // SAFETY: the property keys are static CFStrings.
            let (name_key, id_key) = unsafe { (kMIDIPropertyName, kMIDIPropertyUniqueID) };
            let name = cfstring_property(e, name_key)?;
            Some((
                e,
                MidiPortInfo {
                    name,
                    unique_id: int_property(e, id_key),
                },
            ))
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Destinations visible to the system (no client needed).
pub fn list_destinations() -> Vec<MidiPortInfo> {
    destination_endpoints()
        .into_iter()
        .map(|(_, p)| p)
        .collect()
}

/// One CoreMIDI client plus the sender thread for every output it opened.
pub struct MidiSystem {
    client: MIDIClientRef,
    out_port: Option<MIDIPortRef>,
    sources: Vec<(String, MIDIEndpointRef)>,
    state: Arc<SenderState>,
    thread: Option<JoinHandle<()>>,
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for MidiSystem {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("MidiSystem")
            .field("client", &self.client)
            .finish_non_exhaustive()
    }
}

impl MidiSystem {
    /// Creates the process's MIDI client (control thread) and starts the
    /// sender thread.
    pub fn new(name: &str) -> Result<MidiSystem, BackendError> {
        let mut client: MIDIClientRef = 0;
        let cf = CFString::from_str(name);
        // SAFETY: `client` is a valid out-pointer; no notification callback.
        os("MIDIClientCreate", unsafe {
            MIDIClientCreate(&cf, None, ptr::null_mut(), NonNull::from(&mut client))
        })?;
        let state = Arc::new(SenderState::default());
        let s = Arc::clone(&state);
        let thread = std::thread::Builder::new()
            .name("vizdaw-midi-out".into())
            .spawn(move || sender_loop(s))
            .map_err(|_| BackendError::Os {
                call: "spawn MIDI sender",
                status: -1,
            })?;
        Ok(MidiSystem {
            client,
            out_port: None,
            sources: Vec::new(),
            state,
            thread: Some(thread),
        })
    }

    fn add_lane(&mut self, dest: Dest) -> Box<dyn MidiOut> {
        let (tx, rx) = RingBuffer::new(MIDI_RING_CAPACITY);
        if let Ok(mut lanes) = self.state.lanes.lock() {
            lanes.push(Lane { rx, dest });
        }
        Box::new(RingMidiOut { tx })
    }

    /// The endpoint of a virtual source this system created.
    fn source(&self, name: &str) -> Option<MIDIEndpointRef> {
        self.sources
            .iter()
            .find(|(n, _)| n == name)
            .map(|(_, e)| *e)
    }
}

impl MidiBackend for MidiSystem {
    fn destinations(&self) -> Vec<MidiPortInfo> {
        list_destinations()
    }

    fn open_virtual_source(&mut self, name: &str) -> Result<Box<dyn MidiOut>, BackendError> {
        let endpoint = match self.source(name) {
            Some(e) => e,
            None => {
                let mut e: MIDIEndpointRef = 0;
                let cf = CFString::from_str(name);
                // SAFETY: valid client and out-pointer.
                os("MIDISourceCreateWithProtocol", unsafe {
                    MIDISourceCreateWithProtocol(
                        self.client,
                        &cf,
                        MIDIProtocolID::Protocol_1_0,
                        NonNull::from(&mut e),
                    )
                })?;
                self.sources.push((name.to_string(), e));
                e
            }
        };
        Ok(self.add_lane(Dest::Source(endpoint)))
    }

    fn open_destination(&mut self, port: &MidiPortInfo) -> Result<Box<dyn MidiOut>, BackendError> {
        let all = destination_endpoints();
        let infos: Vec<MidiPortInfo> = all.iter().map(|(_, p)| p.clone()).collect();
        let found = find_port(&port.name, &infos)
            .or_else(|| {
                infos
                    .iter()
                    .find(|p| p.unique_id.is_some() && p.unique_id == port.unique_id)
            })
            .and_then(|p| all.iter().find(|(_, q)| q == p))
            .map(|(e, _)| *e)
            .ok_or(BackendError::NoDevice)?;
        let out_port = match self.out_port {
            Some(p) => p,
            None => {
                let mut p: MIDIPortRef = 0;
                let cf = CFString::from_str("VizDAW Output");
                // SAFETY: valid client and out-pointer.
                os("MIDIOutputPortCreate", unsafe {
                    MIDIOutputPortCreate(self.client, &cf, NonNull::from(&mut p))
                })?;
                self.out_port = Some(p);
                p
            }
        };
        Ok(self.add_lane(Dest::Port(out_port, found)))
    }
}

impl Drop for MidiSystem {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.state.stop.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
        if let Some(t) = self.thread.take() {
            let _ = t.join();
        }
        // SAFETY: the objects belong to this client and are not used after
        // this point (the sender thread has stopped).
        unsafe {
            for (_, e) in &self.sources {
                MIDIEndpointDispose(*e);
            }
            if let Some(p) = self.out_port {
                MIDIPortDispose(p);
            }
            MIDIClientDispose(self.client);
        }
    }
}

/// A received MIDI 1.0 message with its timestamp in host nanoseconds.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Received {
    /// Timestamp in host-clock ticks, as delivered.
    pub host_time: u64,
    /// Timestamp, converted to nanoseconds.
    pub host_ns: u64,
    /// The message bytes (`F0 … F7` for SysEx).
    pub bytes: Vec<u8>,
}

/// Listens to one of the system's own virtual sources (tests and
/// diagnostics; MIDI input proper is a later sub-project).
pub struct MidiMonitor {
    port: MIDIPortRef,
    received: Arc<Mutex<Vec<Received>>>,
    _block: RcBlock<dyn Fn(NonNull<MIDIEventList>, *mut c_void)>,
}

fn channel_len(status: u8) -> usize {
    match status & 0xF0 {
        0xC0 | 0xD0 => 2,
        _ => 3,
    }
}

/// Size in words of a UMP of message type `t`.
fn ump_words(t: u32) -> usize {
    match t {
        0x0 | 0x1 | 0x2 | 0x6 | 0x7 => 1,
        0x3 | 0x4 | 0x8 | 0x9 | 0xA => 2,
        0xB | 0xC => 3,
        _ => 4,
    }
}

/// Decodes the type-2 and type-3 UMPs of an event list.
fn decode(list: &MIDIEventList, out: &mut Vec<Received>, sysex: &mut Vec<u8>) {
    let mut p: *const MIDIEventPacket = list.packet.as_ptr();
    for _ in 0..list.numPackets {
        // SAFETY: CoreMIDI guarantees `numPackets` consecutive packets, each
        // followed by the next at `words[wordCount]` (MIDIEventPacketNext);
        // packets are 4-byte aligned, so they are read unaligned-safely.
        let pkt = unsafe { ptr::read_unaligned(p) };
        // SAFETY: pure conversion.
        let host_ns = unsafe { AudioConvertHostTimeToNanos(pkt.timeStamp) };
        let words = pkt.words;
        let n = (pkt.wordCount as usize).min(64);
        let mut i = 0;
        while i < n {
            let w0 = words.get(i).copied().unwrap_or(0);
            match w0 >> 28 {
                0x2 => {
                    let b = [(w0 >> 16) as u8, (w0 >> 8) as u8 & 0x7F, w0 as u8 & 0x7F];
                    let len = channel_len(b[0]);
                    out.push(Received {
                        host_time: pkt.timeStamp,
                        host_ns,
                        bytes: b.get(..len).unwrap_or(&b).to_vec(),
                    });
                    i += 1;
                }
                0x3 => {
                    let w1 = words.get(i + 1).copied().unwrap_or(0);
                    let status = (w0 >> 20) & 0xF;
                    let count = ((w0 >> 16) & 0xF) as usize;
                    let bytes = [
                        (w0 >> 8) as u8,
                        w0 as u8,
                        (w1 >> 24) as u8,
                        (w1 >> 16) as u8,
                        (w1 >> 8) as u8,
                        w1 as u8,
                    ];
                    if u32::from(SYSEX_COMPLETE) == status || u32::from(SYSEX_START) == status {
                        sysex.clear();
                        sysex.push(0xF0);
                    }
                    sysex.extend_from_slice(bytes.get(..count.min(6)).unwrap_or(&[]));
                    if u32::from(SYSEX_COMPLETE) == status || u32::from(SYSEX_END) == status {
                        sysex.push(0xF7);
                        out.push(Received {
                            host_time: pkt.timeStamp,
                            host_ns,
                            bytes: std::mem::take(sysex),
                        });
                    }
                    i += 2;
                }
                t => i += ump_words(t),
            }
        }
        // SAFETY: see above; stays within the list CoreMIDI handed us.
        p = unsafe {
            (p.cast::<u8>())
                .add(std::mem::offset_of!(MIDIEventPacket, words) + n * 4)
                .cast::<MIDIEventPacket>()
        };
    }
}

impl MidiMonitor {
    /// Connects an input port of `system`'s client to its virtual source
    /// `name` (opened earlier with `open_virtual_source`).
    pub fn watch(system: &MidiSystem, name: &str) -> Result<MidiMonitor, BackendError> {
        let source = system.source(name).ok_or(BackendError::NoDevice)?;
        let received = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
        let sink = Arc::clone(&received);
        let pending = Mutex::new(Vec::new());
        let block = RcBlock::new(move |list: NonNull<MIDIEventList>, _src: *mut c_void| {
            // SAFETY: CoreMIDI passes a valid event list for the call.
            let list = unsafe { list.as_ref() };
            if let (Ok(mut out), Ok(mut sx)) = (sink.lock(), pending.lock()) {
                decode(list, &mut out, &mut sx);
            }
        });
        let mut port: MIDIPortRef = 0;
        let cf = CFString::from_str("VizDAW Monitor");
        // SAFETY: valid client, out-pointer and block; CoreMIDI copies the
        // block, and we keep ours alive for the port's lifetime anyway.
        os("MIDIInputPortCreateWithProtocol", unsafe {
            MIDIInputPortCreateWithProtocol(
                system.client,
                &cf,
                MIDIProtocolID::Protocol_1_0,
                NonNull::from(&mut port),
                (&*block as *const block2::DynBlock<_>).cast_mut(),
            )
        })?;
        // SAFETY: both objects are live and belong to our client.
        os("MIDIPortConnectSource", unsafe {
            MIDIPortConnectSource(port, source, ptr::null_mut())
        })?;
        Ok(MidiMonitor {
            port,
            received,
            _block: block,
        })
    }

    /// Everything received so far.
    pub fn take(&self) -> Vec<Received> {
        self.received
            .lock()
            .map(|mut v| std::mem::take(&mut *v))
            .unwrap_or_default()
    }
}

impl Drop for MidiMonitor {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        // SAFETY: the port belongs to this monitor and is not used again.
        unsafe {
            MIDIPortDispose(self.port);
        }
    }
}

/// Nanoseconds to host-clock ticks (the timestamps CoreMIDI carries).
pub fn ns_to_host(ns: u64) -> u64 {
    // SAFETY: pure conversion.
    unsafe { AudioConvertNanosToHostTime(ns) }
}

/// Current host time in nanoseconds.
pub fn now_ns() -> u64 {
    // SAFETY: pure queries.
    unsafe { AudioConvertHostTimeToNanos(objc2_core_audio::AudioGetCurrentHostTime()) }
}
```

### Task 12: HAL output backend and the macOS sink factory

**Goal:** Spec §9 `hal.rs`: `CoreAudioBackend` opens the default (or named) output device, checks for f32 linear PCM, reads/sets the buffer size, runs a `catch_unwind`-wrapped `extern "C-unwind"` IOProc that renders planar stereo through the `Renderer` and writes channels 1–2 (zeros elsewhere), converts `mHostTime` to `BlockTime`, and turns device listeners into `BackendEvent`s. `MacSinks` builds DLS synths and MIDI outs for the engine.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/platform-macos/src/hal.rs`
- Create: `crates/platform-macos/src/sinks.rs`
- Modify: `crates/platform-macos/src/lib.rs`
- Test: `crates/platform-macos/tests/hal.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AudioBackend`, `Renderer::{process, raise, config}`, `Faults::PANIC`, `SinkFactory`, `Sink`, `TargetKind` (Task 7); `DlsSynth` (Task 10); `MidiSystem`, `list_destinations` (Task 11); `OfflineSinks`, `fixtures::Builder` (tests).
- Produces:
  - `hal::CoreAudioBackend` (`AudioBackend + Send + Default`): `new()`, `info() -> Option<&StreamInfo>`, `close() -> Option<Renderer>`; `open` fails with `NoDevice` (no/unknown device or no output channels) or `Format { detail }` (non-f32 stream); a panic inside the IOProc sets `Faults::PANIC` and silences every later block.
  - `hal::output_devices() -> Vec<String>` (names of devices with output channels).
  - `sinks::MacSinks` (`SinkFactory + Default`): `MacSinks::new()`; `BuiltinSynth` → `DlsSynth`, `MidiVirtualSource` → `"VizDAW Out"` (`sinks::VIRTUAL_SOURCE_NAME`), `MidiDestination(port)` → `open_destination`; the MIDI client is created on first use.

```rust
#![cfg(target_os = "macos")]

use core_engine::backend::{AudioBackend, AudioConfig};
use core_engine::engine::{Engine, EngineConfig};
use core_engine::fixtures::Builder;
use core_engine::offline::OfflineSinks;
use platform_macos::hal::{CoreAudioBackend, output_devices};
use std::time::Duration;

#[test]
#[ignore = "needs an audio output device"]
fn hal_default_device_opens() {
    let mut backend = CoreAudioBackend::new();
    let info = backend.open(&AudioConfig::default()).unwrap();
    assert!(info.sample_rate > 0.0 && info.channels > 0 && info.buffer_frames > 0);
    assert!(output_devices().contains(&info.device_name));

    // An empty one-bar sequence: 2 s of silence at 120 BPM.
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let (seq, _) = b.sequence("Silence", "L", None).unwrap();
    let doc = b.finish();
    let (mut engine, renderer) = Engine::new(
        EngineConfig::new(info.sample_rate),
        Box::new(OfflineSinks::new(16)),
    );
    engine.load(&doc, seq).unwrap();
    engine.play().unwrap();
    backend.start(renderer).unwrap();
    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(1));
    let pos = engine.position_frames() as f64;
    let renderer = backend.stop().unwrap();
    drop(renderer);
    assert!(
        (pos - info.sample_rate).abs() < info.sample_rate * 0.25,
        "advanced {pos} frames in 1 s at {}",
        info.sample_rate
    );
    assert!(engine.faults().is_empty());
}
```

Create `crates/platform-macos/src/hal.rs`:

```rust
//! CoreAudio HAL output through an IOProc on the device's own real-time
//! thread (no AUHAL).

use core_engine::BackendError;
use core_engine::backend::{
    AudioBackend, AudioConfig, BackendEvent, BlockTime, MAX_FRAMES, StreamInfo,
};
use core_engine::render::{Faults, Renderer};
use objc2_core_audio::{
    AudioConvertHostTimeToNanos, AudioDeviceCreateIOProcID, AudioDeviceDestroyIOProcID,
    AudioDeviceIOProcID, AudioDeviceStart, AudioDeviceStop, AudioGetCurrentHostTime,
    AudioObjectAddPropertyListener, AudioObjectGetPropertyData, AudioObjectGetPropertyDataSize,
    AudioObjectID, AudioObjectPropertyAddress, AudioObjectPropertyScope,
    AudioObjectPropertySelector, AudioObjectRemovePropertyListener, AudioObjectSetPropertyData,
    kAudioDevicePropertyBufferFrameSize, kAudioDevicePropertyDeviceIsAlive,
    kAudioDevicePropertyNominalSampleRate, kAudioDevicePropertyStreamConfiguration,
    kAudioDevicePropertyStreams, kAudioHardwarePropertyDefaultOutputDevice,
    kAudioHardwarePropertyDevices, kAudioObjectPropertyElementMain, kAudioObjectPropertyName,
    kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal, kAudioObjectPropertyScopeOutput, kAudioObjectSystemObject,
    kAudioStreamPropertyVirtualFormat,
};
use objc2_core_audio_types::{
    AudioBuffer, AudioBufferList, AudioStreamBasicDescription, AudioTimeStamp, AudioTimeStampFlags,
    kAudioFormatFlagIsFloat, kAudioFormatLinearPCM,
};
use objc2_core_foundation::{CFRetained, CFString};
use std::ffi::c_void;
use std::panic::{AssertUnwindSafe, catch_unwind};
use std::ptr::{self, NonNull};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};

const SYSTEM: AudioObjectID = kAudioObjectSystemObject as AudioObjectID;

fn os(call: &'static str, status: i32) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
    if status == 0 {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(BackendError::Os { call, status })
    }
}

fn addr(
    selector: AudioObjectPropertySelector,
    scope: AudioObjectPropertyScope,
) -> AudioObjectPropertyAddress {
    AudioObjectPropertyAddress {
        mSelector: selector,
        mScope: scope,
        mElement: kAudioObjectPropertyElementMain,
    }
}

/// Reads a fixed-size property.
fn get<T: Copy + Default>(
    obj: AudioObjectID,
    selector: AudioObjectPropertySelector,
    scope: AudioObjectPropertyScope,
) -> Result<T, BackendError> {
    let mut a = addr(selector, scope);
    let mut value = T::default();
    let mut size = size_of::<T>() as u32;
    // SAFETY: `value` is a valid buffer of `size` bytes for a property of
    // type `T`; `a` and `size` are valid pointers.
    os("AudioObjectGetPropertyData", unsafe {
        AudioObjectGetPropertyData(
            obj,
            NonNull::from(&mut a),
            0,
            ptr::null(),
            NonNull::from(&mut size),
            NonNull::from(&mut value).cast::<c_void>(),
        )
    })?;
    Ok(value)
}

/// Writes a fixed-size property.
fn set<T: Copy>(
    obj: AudioObjectID,
    selector: AudioObjectPropertySelector,
    scope: AudioObjectPropertyScope,
    value: T,
) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
    let mut a = addr(selector, scope);
    let mut v = value;
    // SAFETY: `v` is a valid `T` of the stated size.
    os("AudioObjectSetPropertyData", unsafe {
        AudioObjectSetPropertyData(
            obj,
            NonNull::from(&mut a),
            0,
            ptr::null(),
            size_of::<T>() as u32,
            NonNull::from(&mut v).cast::<c_void>(),
        )
    })
}

/// Reads a variable-size property as 8-byte-aligned words.
fn get_bytes(
    obj: AudioObjectID,
    selector: AudioObjectPropertySelector,
    scope: AudioObjectPropertyScope,
) -> Result<Vec<u64>, BackendError> {
    let mut a = addr(selector, scope);
    let mut size = 0u32;
    // SAFETY: valid pointers.
    os("AudioObjectGetPropertyDataSize", unsafe {
        AudioObjectGetPropertyDataSize(
            obj,
            NonNull::from(&mut a),
            0,
            ptr::null(),
            NonNull::from(&mut size),
        )
    })?;
    let mut buf = vec![0u64; (size as usize).div_ceil(8).max(1)];
    // SAFETY: `buf` holds at least `size` bytes.
    os("AudioObjectGetPropertyData", unsafe {
        AudioObjectGetPropertyData(
            obj,
            NonNull::from(&mut a),
            0,
            ptr::null(),
            NonNull::from(&mut size),
            NonNull::new(buf.as_mut_ptr().cast::<c_void>()).unwrap_or(NonNull::dangling()),
        )
    })?;
    buf.truncate((size as usize).div_ceil(8));
    Ok(buf)
}

/// Reads an array-of-`u32` property (object IDs).
fn get_ids(
    obj: AudioObjectID,
    selector: AudioObjectPropertySelector,
    scope: AudioObjectPropertyScope,
) -> Result<Vec<AudioObjectID>, BackendError> {
    let words = get_bytes(obj, selector, scope)?;
    Ok(words
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|w| [*w as u32, (*w >> 32) as u32])
        .filter(|id| *id != 0)
        .collect())
}

fn name_of(obj: AudioObjectID) -> Option<String> {
    let mut a = addr(kAudioObjectPropertyName, kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal);
    let mut s: *const CFString = ptr::null();
    let mut size = size_of::<*const CFString>() as u32;
    // SAFETY: the name property is a +1 retained CFStringRef.
    let status = unsafe {
        AudioObjectGetPropertyData(
            obj,
            NonNull::from(&mut a),
            0,
            ptr::null(),
            NonNull::from(&mut size),
            NonNull::from(&mut s).cast::<c_void>(),
        )
    };
    let p = NonNull::new(s.cast_mut())?;
    if status != 0 {
        return None;
    }
    // SAFETY: see above; we take over the reference.
    let s: CFRetained<CFString> = unsafe { CFRetained::from_raw(p) };
    Some(s.to_string())
}

/// Output channel counts of the device's output buffers, in order.
fn output_layout(dev: AudioObjectID) -> Result<Vec<u32>, BackendError> {
    let words = get_bytes(
        dev,
        kAudioDevicePropertyStreamConfiguration,
        kAudioObjectPropertyScopeOutput,
    )?;
    let list = words.as_ptr().cast::<AudioBufferList>();
    // SAFETY: the property is an AudioBufferList with `mNumberBuffers`
    // trailing buffers, stored in `words` (8-byte aligned).
    let n = unsafe { (*list).mNumberBuffers } as usize;
    let max = (words.len() * 8).saturating_sub(8) / size_of::<AudioBuffer>();
    // SAFETY: as above; `n` is clamped to what the buffer holds.
    let bufs = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts((*list).mBuffers.as_ptr(), n.min(max)) };
    Ok(bufs.iter().map(|b| b.mNumberChannels).collect())
}

/// Every output stream must be 32-bit float linear PCM.
fn check_formats(dev: AudioObjectID) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
    for stream in get_ids(
        dev,
        kAudioDevicePropertyStreams,
        kAudioObjectPropertyScopeOutput,
    )? {
        let f: AudioStreamBasicDescription = get_asbd(stream)?;
        if f.mFormatID != kAudioFormatLinearPCM
            || f.mFormatFlags & kAudioFormatFlagIsFloat == 0
            || f.mBitsPerChannel != 32
        {
            return Err(BackendError::Format {
                detail: format!(
                    "stream {stream}: format {:#x}, flags {:#x}, {} bits",
                    f.mFormatID, f.mFormatFlags, f.mBitsPerChannel
                ),
            });
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn get_asbd(stream: AudioObjectID) -> Result<AudioStreamBasicDescription, BackendError> {
    let mut a = addr(
        kAudioStreamPropertyVirtualFormat,
        kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal,
    );
    let mut f = AudioStreamBasicDescription {
        mSampleRate: 0.0,
        mFormatID: 0,
        mFormatFlags: 0,
        mBytesPerPacket: 0,
        mFramesPerPacket: 0,
        mBytesPerFrame: 0,
        mChannelsPerFrame: 0,
        mBitsPerChannel: 0,
        mReserved: 0,
    };
    let mut size = size_of::<AudioStreamBasicDescription>() as u32;
    // SAFETY: `f` is a valid ASBD buffer.
    os("AudioObjectGetPropertyData(VirtualFormat)", unsafe {
        AudioObjectGetPropertyData(
            stream,
            NonNull::from(&mut a),
            0,
            ptr::null(),
            NonNull::from(&mut size),
            NonNull::from(&mut f).cast::<c_void>(),
        )
    })?;
    Ok(f)
}

/// Names of every device with output channels.
pub fn output_devices() -> Vec<String> {
    get_ids(
        SYSTEM,
        kAudioHardwarePropertyDevices,
        kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal,
    )
    .unwrap_or_default()
    .into_iter()
    .filter(|d| output_layout(*d).is_ok_and(|l| l.iter().sum::<u32>() > 0))
    .filter_map(name_of)
    .collect()
}

/// Flags set by property listeners (non-RT CoreAudio notification thread)
/// and read by `take_events`.
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct Flags {
    dead: AtomicBool,
    default_changed: AtomicBool,
    rate_changed: AtomicBool,
    buffer_changed: AtomicBool,
}

unsafe extern "C-unwind" fn listener(
    _obj: AudioObjectID,
    count: u32,
    addresses: NonNull<AudioObjectPropertyAddress>,
    client: *mut c_void,
) -> i32 {
    // SAFETY: `client` is the `Arc<Flags>` pointer registered in `listen`,
    // kept alive until the listener is removed; `addresses` holds `count`
    // entries.
    let (flags, addrs) = unsafe {
        (
            &*client.cast::<Flags>(),
            std::slice::from_raw_parts(addresses.as_ptr(), count as usize),
        )
    };
    for a in addrs {
        match a.mSelector {
            s if s == kAudioDevicePropertyDeviceIsAlive => {
                flags.dead.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed)
            }
            s if s == kAudioHardwarePropertyDefaultOutputDevice => {
                flags.default_changed.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed)
            }
            s if s == kAudioDevicePropertyNominalSampleRate => {
                flags.rate_changed.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed)
            }
            s if s == kAudioDevicePropertyBufferFrameSize => {
                flags.buffer_changed.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed)
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    0
}

/// State owned by the IOProc while running.
struct RtContext {
    renderer: Renderer,
    left: Box<[f32]>,
    right: Box<[f32]>,
    poisoned: bool,
}

/// Zeroes every output buffer. // RT
fn silence(out: &mut AudioBufferList) {
    let n = out.mNumberBuffers as usize;
    // SAFETY: CoreAudio hands us `mNumberBuffers` trailing buffers.
    let bufs = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(out.mBuffers.as_mut_ptr(), n) };
    for b in bufs {
        if !b.mData.is_null() {
            // SAFETY: `mData` holds `mDataByteSize` bytes.
            unsafe { ptr::write_bytes(b.mData.cast::<u8>(), 0, b.mDataByteSize as usize) };
        }
    }
}

/// Renders planar stereo and writes it to the device's buffers:
/// global channel 0 = left, 1 = right, others zero. // RT
fn render(ctx: &mut RtContext, out: &mut AudioBufferList, time: &AudioTimeStamp) {
    let n = out.mNumberBuffers as usize;
    // SAFETY: CoreAudio hands us `mNumberBuffers` trailing buffers.
    let bufs = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(out.mBuffers.as_mut_ptr(), n) };
    let Some(first) = bufs.first() else {
        return;
    };
    let ch0 = first.mNumberChannels.max(1);
    let frames = first.mDataByteSize / (4 * ch0);
    let host_ns = if time.mFlags.contains(AudioTimeStampFlags::HostTimeValid) {
        // SAFETY: pure conversion.
        unsafe { AudioConvertHostTimeToNanos(time.mHostTime) }
    } else {
        // SAFETY: pure queries.
        unsafe { AudioConvertHostTimeToNanos(AudioGetCurrentHostTime()) }
    };
    let sr = ctx.renderer.config().sample_rate;
    let len = (frames as usize).min(ctx.left.len());
    let (Some(l), Some(r)) = (ctx.left.get_mut(..len), ctx.right.get_mut(..len)) else {
        return;
    };
    let mut chans: [&mut [f32]; 2] = [l, r];
    ctx.renderer.process(
        &mut chans,
        frames,
        BlockTime {
            host_time_ns: host_ns,
            sample_rate: sr,
        },
    );
    let mut global = 0usize;
    for b in bufs.iter_mut() {
        let nch = b.mNumberChannels.max(1) as usize;
        let samples = b.mDataByteSize as usize / 4;
        if b.mData.is_null() {
            global += nch;
            continue;
        }
        // SAFETY: `mData` holds `mDataByteSize` bytes of f32 samples.
        let data = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(b.mData.cast::<f32>(), samples) };
        for (i, x) in data.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            let frame = i / nch;
            let ch = global + i % nch;
            *x = match ch {
                0 => chans[0].get(frame).copied().unwrap_or(0.0),
                1 => chans[1].get(frame).copied().unwrap_or(0.0),
                _ => 0.0,
            };
        }
        global += nch;
    }
}

unsafe extern "C-unwind" fn io_proc(
    _device: AudioObjectID,
    _now: NonNull<AudioTimeStamp>,
    _input: NonNull<AudioBufferList>,
    _input_time: NonNull<AudioTimeStamp>,
    mut output: NonNull<AudioBufferList>,
    output_time: NonNull<AudioTimeStamp>,
    client: *mut c_void,
) -> i32 {
    // SAFETY: `client` is the `RtContext` boxed in `start`, owned by this
    // IOProc until `stop` has returned from `AudioDeviceStop`; the buffer
    // list and timestamp are valid for this call. // RT
    let (ctx, out, time) = unsafe {
        (
            &mut *client.cast::<RtContext>(),
            output.as_mut(),
            output_time.as_ref(),
        )
    };
    if ctx.poisoned {
        silence(out);
        return 0;
    }
    let ok = catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| render(ctx, out, time))).is_ok();
    if !ok {
        // Never unwind into CoreAudio: go silent for good and report.
        ctx.poisoned = true;
        ctx.renderer.raise(Faults::PANIC);
        silence(out);
    }
    0
}

struct Running {
    proc_id: AudioDeviceIOProcID,
    ctx: *mut RtContext,
}

struct Listening {
    flags: *const Flags,
    device: AudioObjectID,
    follow_default: bool,
}

/// The HAL output backend.
pub struct CoreAudioBackend {
    device: Option<AudioObjectID>,
    info: Option<StreamInfo>,
    running: Option<Running>,
    listening: Option<Listening>,
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for CoreAudioBackend {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("CoreAudioBackend")
            .field("device", &self.device)
            .field("info", &self.info)
            .finish_non_exhaustive()
    }
}

// SAFETY: the raw pointers are owned by the backend and only touched by the
// control thread (the IOProc owns `ctx` between start and stop).
unsafe impl Send for CoreAudioBackend {}

impl Default for CoreAudioBackend {
    fn default() -> Self {
        CoreAudioBackend::new()
    }
}

const LISTENED: [(AudioObjectPropertySelector, bool); 4] = [
    (kAudioDevicePropertyDeviceIsAlive, false),
    (kAudioDevicePropertyNominalSampleRate, false),
    (kAudioDevicePropertyBufferFrameSize, false),
    (kAudioHardwarePropertyDefaultOutputDevice, true),
];

impl CoreAudioBackend {
    /// A closed backend.
    pub fn new() -> CoreAudioBackend {
        CoreAudioBackend {
            device: None,
            info: None,
            running: None,
            listening: None,
        }
    }

    /// What `open` found.
    pub fn info(&self) -> Option<&StreamInfo> {
        self.info.as_ref()
    }

    fn listen(&mut self, device: AudioObjectID, follow_default: bool) {
        let flags = Arc::into_raw(Arc::new(Flags::default()));
        for (selector, system) in LISTENED {
            if system && !follow_default {
                continue;
            }
            let obj = if system { SYSTEM } else { device };
            let mut a = addr(selector, kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal);
            // SAFETY: `flags` stays alive until `unlisten` removes this
            // listener with the same arguments.
            unsafe {
                AudioObjectAddPropertyListener(
                    obj,
                    NonNull::from(&mut a),
                    Some(listener),
                    flags.cast_mut().cast::<c_void>(),
                );
            }
        }
        self.listening = Some(Listening {
            flags,
            device,
            follow_default,
        });
    }

    fn unlisten(&mut self) {
        let Some(l) = self.listening.take() else {
            return;
        };
        for (selector, system) in LISTENED {
            if system && !l.follow_default {
                continue;
            }
            let obj = if system { SYSTEM } else { l.device };
            let mut a = addr(selector, kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal);
            // SAFETY: removes exactly the listener added in `listen`.
            unsafe {
                AudioObjectRemovePropertyListener(
                    obj,
                    NonNull::from(&mut a),
                    Some(listener),
                    l.flags.cast_mut().cast::<c_void>(),
                );
            }
        }
        // SAFETY: no listener refers to `flags` any more.
        drop(unsafe { Arc::from_raw(l.flags) });
    }

    /// Stops (if running) and forgets the device.
    pub fn close(&mut self) -> Option<Renderer> {
        let r = self.stop().ok();
        self.unlisten();
        self.device = None;
        self.info = None;
        r
    }
}

impl AudioBackend for CoreAudioBackend {
    fn open(&mut self, cfg: &AudioConfig) -> Result<StreamInfo, BackendError> {
        self.close();
        let device = match &cfg.device {
            None => get::<AudioObjectID>(
                SYSTEM,
                kAudioHardwarePropertyDefaultOutputDevice,
                kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal,
            )?,
            Some(name) => get_ids(
                SYSTEM,
                kAudioHardwarePropertyDevices,
                kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal,
            )?
            .into_iter()
            .find(|d| name_of(*d).as_deref() == Some(name.as_str()))
            .ok_or(BackendError::NoDevice)?,
        };
        if device == 0 {
            return Err(BackendError::NoDevice);
        }
        let layout = output_layout(device)?;
        let channels: u32 = layout.iter().sum();
        if channels == 0 {
            return Err(BackendError::NoDevice);
        }
        check_formats(device)?;
        if let Some(n) = cfg.buffer_frames {
            set(
                device,
                kAudioDevicePropertyBufferFrameSize,
                kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal,
                n,
            )?;
        }
        let sample_rate: f64 = get(
            device,
            kAudioDevicePropertyNominalSampleRate,
            kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal,
        )?;
        let buffer_frames: u32 = get(
            device,
            kAudioDevicePropertyBufferFrameSize,
            kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal,
        )?;
        let info = StreamInfo {
            device_name: name_of(device).unwrap_or_default(),
            sample_rate,
            buffer_frames,
            channels,
        };
        self.device = Some(device);
        self.info = Some(info.clone());
        self.listen(device, cfg.device.is_none());
        Ok(info)
    }

    fn start(&mut self, renderer: Renderer) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
        let device = self.device.ok_or(BackendError::NoDevice)?;
        if self.running.is_some() {
            return Err(BackendError::Os {
                call: "start (already running)",
                status: -1,
            });
        }
        let max = renderer.config().max_frames.max(MAX_FRAMES) as usize;
        let ctx = Box::into_raw(Box::new(RtContext {
            renderer,
            left: vec![0.0; max].into_boxed_slice(),
            right: vec![0.0; max].into_boxed_slice(),
            poisoned: false,
        }));
        let mut proc_id: AudioDeviceIOProcID = None;
        // SAFETY: `io_proc` matches the IOProc signature; `ctx` stays valid
        // until `stop` destroys the IOProc and reclaims it.
        let created = unsafe {
            AudioDeviceCreateIOProcID(
                device,
                Some(io_proc),
                ctx.cast::<c_void>(),
                NonNull::from(&mut proc_id),
            )
        };
        if let Err(e) = os("AudioDeviceCreateIOProcID", created) {
            // SAFETY: the IOProc was not created, so we still own `ctx`.
            drop(unsafe { Box::from_raw(ctx) });
            return Err(e);
        }
        // SAFETY: `proc_id` was just created on `device`.
        let started = unsafe { AudioDeviceStart(device, proc_id) };
        if let Err(e) = os("AudioDeviceStart", started) {
            // SAFETY: the device never ran the IOProc; destroy it and take
            // `ctx` back.
            unsafe {
                AudioDeviceDestroyIOProcID(device, proc_id);
                drop(Box::from_raw(ctx));
            }
            return Err(e);
        }
        self.running = Some(Running { proc_id, ctx });
        Ok(())
    }

    fn stop(&mut self) -> Result<Renderer, BackendError> {
        let device = self.device.ok_or(BackendError::NoDevice)?;
        let run = self.running.take().ok_or(BackendError::NoDevice)?;
        // SAFETY: after `AudioDeviceStop` returns the IOProc no longer runs,
        // so after destroying it we own `ctx` again.
        let ctx = unsafe {
            AudioDeviceStop(device, run.proc_id);
            AudioDeviceDestroyIOProcID(device, run.proc_id);
            Box::from_raw(run.ctx)
        };
        Ok(ctx.renderer)
    }

    fn take_events(&mut self) -> Vec<BackendEvent> {
        let mut ev = Vec::new();
        let (Some(l), Some(device)) = (self.listening.as_ref(), self.device) else {
            return ev;
        };
        // SAFETY: `flags` lives until `unlisten`.
        let flags = unsafe { &*l.flags };
        if flags.dead.swap(false, Ordering::Relaxed) {
            let alive: u32 = get(
                device,
                kAudioDevicePropertyDeviceIsAlive,
                kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal,
            )
            .unwrap_or(0);
            if alive == 0 {
                ev.push(BackendEvent::DeviceLost);
            }
        }
        if flags.default_changed.swap(false, Ordering::Relaxed) {
            let now: AudioObjectID = get(
                SYSTEM,
                kAudioHardwarePropertyDefaultOutputDevice,
                kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal,
            )
            .unwrap_or(0);
            if now != device {
                ev.push(BackendEvent::DefaultDeviceChanged);
            }
        }
        if flags.rate_changed.swap(false, Ordering::Relaxed) {
            if let Ok(rate) = get::<f64>(
                device,
                kAudioDevicePropertyNominalSampleRate,
                kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal,
            ) {
                if self.info.as_ref().is_some_and(|i| i.sample_rate != rate) {
                    if let Some(i) = self.info.as_mut() {
                        i.sample_rate = rate;
                    }
                    ev.push(BackendEvent::SampleRateChanged(rate));
                }
            }
        }
        if flags.buffer_changed.swap(false, Ordering::Relaxed) {
            if let Ok(n) = get::<u32>(
                device,
                kAudioDevicePropertyBufferFrameSize,
                kAudioObjectPropertyScopeGlobal,
            ) {
                if self.info.as_ref().is_some_and(|i| i.buffer_frames != n) {
                    if let Some(i) = self.info.as_mut() {
                        i.buffer_frames = n;
                    }
                    ev.push(BackendEvent::BufferSizeChanged(n));
                }
            }
        }
        ev
    }
}

impl Drop for CoreAudioBackend {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        drop(self.close());
    }
}
```

Create `crates/platform-macos/src/sinks.rs`:

```rust
//! The macOS [`SinkFactory`]: the DLS synth for `BuiltinSynth`, CoreMIDI
//! for the virtual source and named destinations.

use crate::dls::DlsSynth;
use crate::midi::{MidiSystem, list_destinations};
use core_engine::BackendError;
use core_engine::backend::{MidiBackend, MidiPortInfo};
use core_engine::engine::SinkFactory;
use core_engine::render::Sink;
use core_engine::schedule::TargetKind;

/// Name of VizDAW's virtual source.
pub const VIRTUAL_SOURCE_NAME: &str = "VizDAW Out";

/// Creates platform sinks; the MIDI client is created on first use.
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct MacSinks {
    midi: Option<MidiSystem>,
}

impl MacSinks {
    /// No MIDI client yet.
    pub fn new() -> MacSinks {
        MacSinks::default()
    }

    fn midi(&mut self) -> Result<&mut MidiSystem, BackendError> {
        if self.midi.is_none() {
            self.midi = Some(MidiSystem::new("VizDAW")?);
        }
        self.midi.as_mut().ok_or(BackendError::NoDevice)
    }
}

impl SinkFactory for MacSinks {
    fn ports(&self) -> Vec<MidiPortInfo> {
        list_destinations()
    }

    fn make(
        &mut self,
        kind: &TargetKind,
        sample_rate: f64,
        max_frames: u32,
    ) -> Result<Sink, BackendError> {
        Ok(match kind {
            TargetKind::BuiltinSynth => {
                Sink::Instrument(Box::new(DlsSynth::new(sample_rate, max_frames)?))
            }
            TargetKind::MidiVirtualSource => {
                Sink::Midi(self.midi()?.open_virtual_source(VIRTUAL_SOURCE_NAME)?)
            }
            TargetKind::MidiDestination(port) => Sink::Midi(self.midi()?.open_destination(port)?),
        })
    }
}
```

**Goal:** Spec §10: `vizdaw-play <project.vizdaw> [--seq NAME] [--loop] [--device NAME] [--buffer N] [--to builtin|virtual|both] [--midi-offset-ms N] [--offline OUT.txt] [--list]` and `vizdaw-play --write-demo <dir/demo.vizdaw>`; live playback with a position display, fault reporting, device-change handling (Plan Ruling 25), Ctrl-C via a polled `SIGINT` flag (Plan Ruling 23) and exit 0 at the end; `--offline` at 48 kHz/512 (Plan Ruling 24); the checked-in `fixtures/demo.vizdaw` and its offline log.

```rust
use core_engine::fixtures::{Builder, Q, demo_document};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::Command;

fn repo() -> PathBuf {
    Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../..")
}

fn bin() -> Command {
    Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_vizdaw-play"))
}

fn temp(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("vizdaw-play-{}-{name}", std::process::id()));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
    dir
}

#[test]
fn checked_in_demo_matches_a_fresh_build() {
    let checked_in =
        std::fs::read_to_string(repo().join("fixtures/demo.vizdaw/project.json")).unwrap();
    let fresh = core_model::file::to_json(&demo_document().unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        checked_in, fresh,
        "run: cargo run -p vizdaw-play -- --write-demo fixtures/demo.vizdaw"
    );
}

#[test]
fn write_demo_writes_a_loadable_package() {
    let dir = temp("write");
    let pkg = dir.join("demo.vizdaw");
    let out = bin().arg("--write-demo").arg(&pkg).output().unwrap();
    assert!(
        out.status.success(),
        "{}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)
    );
    let doc = core_model::file::load(&pkg).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(doc, demo_document().unwrap());
}

#[test]
fn offline_render_of_the_demo_matches_the_checked_in_log() {
    let dir = temp("offline");
    let log = dir.join("demo.txt");
    let out = bin()
        .arg(repo().join("fixtures/demo.vizdaw"))
        .args(["--seq", "Song", "--offline"])
        .arg(&log)
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(
        out.status.success(),
        "{}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)
    );
    let got = std::fs::read_to_string(&log).unwrap();
    let want = std::fs::read_to_string(repo().join("fixtures/demo.offline.txt")).unwrap();
    assert!(
        got == want,
        "offline log differs from fixtures/demo.offline.txt"
    );
}

#[test]
fn offline_loop_renders_three_passes() {
    let dir = temp("loop");
    let log = dir.join("loop.txt");
    let out = bin()
        .arg(repo().join("fixtures/demo.vizdaw"))
        .args(["--loop", "--to", "virtual", "--offline"])
        .arg(&log)
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(
        out.status.success(),
        "{}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)
    );
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&log).unwrap();
    let program_33 = text.lines().filter(|l| l.ends_with(" c1 21")).count();
    assert_eq!(program_33, 6, "two Groove uses per pass, three passes");
}

#[test]
fn several_top_level_sequences_list_and_exit_2() {
    let mut b = Builder::new();
    let d = b.dest("Synth", "builtin:synth", 0).unwrap();
    let (a, al) = b.sequence("Verse", "L", Some(d)).unwrap();
    b.notes(a, al, &[(0, 60, 0x8000, Q)]).unwrap();
    b.sequence("Chorus", "L", Some(d)).unwrap();
    let dir = temp("ambiguous");
    let pkg = dir.join("two.vizdaw");
    core_model::file::save(&b.finish(), &pkg).unwrap();

    let out = bin()
        .arg(&pkg)
        .args(["--offline"])
        .arg(dir.join("x.txt"))
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status.code(), Some(2));
    let err = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
    assert!(err.contains("Verse") && err.contains("Chorus"), "{err}");

    let out = bin()
        .arg(&pkg)
        .args(["--seq", "Verse", "--offline"])
        .arg(dir.join("v.txt"))
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(out.status.success());
    assert_eq!(
        std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("v.txt")).unwrap(),
        "0 t0 90 3c 40\n24000 t0 80 3c 00\n"
    );

    let out = bin()
        .arg(&pkg)
        .args(["--seq", "Bridge", "--offline"])
        .arg(dir.join("b.txt"))
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status.code(), Some(1));
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("Bridge"));
}

#[test]
fn list_shows_sequences() {
    let out = bin()
        .arg(repo().join("fixtures/demo.vizdaw"))
        .arg("--list")
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(out.status.success());
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
    assert!(text.contains("Song  12 bar(s)  (top level)"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("Beat  1 bar(s)"), "{text}");
}

#[test]
fn bad_arguments_are_refused() {
    let out = bin()
        .arg("x.vizdaw")
        .args(["--buffer", "16"])
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status.code(), Some(2));
    let out = bin()
        .arg("missing.vizdaw")
        .args(["--offline", "x.txt"])
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status.code(), Some(1));
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("cannot load"));
}
```

```rust
//! Command-line arguments.

use clap::{Parser, ValueEnum};
use core_engine::schedule::BuiltinRoute;
use std::path::PathBuf;

/// Where `builtin:synth` destinations play.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, ValueEnum)]
pub enum To {
    /// Apple's built-in DLS synth.
    Builtin,
    /// The "VizDAW Out" virtual MIDI source.
    Virtual,
    /// Both.
    Both,
}

impl From<To> for BuiltinRoute {
    fn from(t: To) -> BuiltinRoute {
        match t {
            To::Builtin => BuiltinRoute::Builtin,
            To::Virtual => BuiltinRoute::Virtual,
            To::Both => BuiltinRoute::Both,
        }
    }
}

/// Plays a VizDAW project sequence through the built-in synth.
#[derive(Debug, Parser)]
#[command(name = "vizdaw-play", version)]
pub struct Args {
    /// The `.vizdaw` package to play.
    #[arg(required_unless_present = "write_demo")]
    pub project: Option<PathBuf>,
    /// Sequence to play (default: the only top-level sequence).
    #[arg(long = "seq", value_name = "NAME")]
    pub seq: Option<String>,
    /// Loop from bar 1 to the end until Ctrl-C.
    #[arg(long = "loop")]
    pub looped: bool,
    /// Output device name (default: follow the system default output).
    #[arg(long, value_name = "NAME")]
    pub device: Option<String>,
    /// IO buffer size in frames.
    #[arg(long, value_name = "N", value_parser = clap::value_parser!(u32).range(32..=2048))]
    pub buffer: Option<u32>,
    /// Where `builtin:synth` destinations play.
    #[arg(long, value_enum, default_value_t = To::Builtin)]
    pub to: To,
    /// Shift MIDI output timestamps (milliseconds, may be negative).
    #[arg(
        long = "midi-offset-ms",
        value_name = "N",
        default_value_t = 0.0,
        allow_negative_numbers = true
    )]
    pub midi_offset_ms: f64,
    /// Render offline (48 kHz, 512-frame blocks) and write the event log.
    #[arg(long, value_name = "OUT.txt")]
    pub offline: Option<PathBuf>,
    /// List sequences, output devices and MIDI destinations, then exit.
    #[arg(long)]
    pub list: bool,
    /// Write the demo project to this package path, then exit.
    #[arg(long = "write-demo", value_name = "DIR/demo.vizdaw", conflicts_with_all = ["project", "offline", "list"])]
    pub write_demo: Option<PathBuf>,
}
```

Create `crates/vizdaw-play/src/project.rs`:

```rust
//! Loading a project and choosing the sequence to play.

use core_engine::engine::find_sequence;
use core_model::document::Document;
use core_model::ids::SeqId;
use core_model::query::{seq_length, unused_sequences};
use std::path::Path;

/// Why no sequence could be chosen.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Choice {
    /// Play this one.
    Play(SeqId),
    /// `--seq` named no sequence.
    Unknown(String),
    /// Several (or no) top-level sequences; these are the candidates.
    Ambiguous(Vec<String>),
}

/// Loads `project.json` from a package.
pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Result<Document, String> {
    core_model::file::load(path).map_err(|e| format!("cannot load {}: {e}", path.display()))
}

/// `--seq NAME`, else the only top-level sequence.
pub fn choose(doc: &Document, name: Option<&str>) -> Choice {
    if let Some(n) = name {
        return match find_sequence(doc, n) {
            Ok(id) => Choice::Play(id),
            Err(_) => Choice::Unknown(n.to_string()),
        };
    }
    let top = unused_sequences(doc);
    if let [only] = top.as_slice() {
        return Choice::Play(*only);
    }
    let candidates: Vec<SeqId> = if top.is_empty() {
        doc.sequences().keys().copied().collect()
    } else {
        top
    };
    Choice::Ambiguous(
        candidates
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|s| doc.sequence(*s).ok())
            .map(|s| s.name.clone())
            .collect(),
    )
}

/// One line per sequence: name, length in bars, top-level marker.
pub fn describe_sequences(doc: &Document) -> Vec<String> {
    let top = unused_sequences(doc);
    doc.sequences()
        .values()
        .map(|s| {
            let bar = s
                .meter
                .entry_at(core_model::time::Tick::ZERO)
                .meter
                .bar_ticks()
                .max(1);
            let len = seq_length(doc, s.id).map_or(0, |t| t.0 / bar);
            let mark = if top.contains(&s.id) {
                "  (top level)"
            } else {
                ""
            };
            format!("{}  {} bar(s){mark}", s.name, len)
        })
        .collect()
}
```

Create `crates/vizdaw-play/src/offline.rs`:

```rust
//! `--offline`: render without audio hardware and write the event log.

use core_engine::backend::AudioBackend;
use core_engine::engine::{Engine, EngineConfig};
use core_engine::offline::{OfflineBackend, OfflineSinks};
use core_engine::schedule::{BuiltinRoute, CompileReport};
use core_model::document::Document;
use core_model::ids::SeqId;

/// Offline sample rate.
pub const RATE: f64 = 48_000.0;
/// Offline block size.
pub const BLOCK: u32 = 512;

/// Plays `seq` to its end and returns the compile report and the log text.
/// When `looped`, the loop is switched off during the third pass, so three
/// passes play followed by the tail.
pub fn render(
    doc: &Document,
    seq: SeqId,
    route: BuiltinRoute,
    looped: bool,
) -> Result<(CompileReport, String), String> {
    let sinks = OfflineSinks::new(1 << 16);
    let log = sinks.log();
    let mut cfg = EngineConfig::new(RATE);
    cfg.route = route;
    let (mut engine, renderer) = Engine::new(cfg, Box::new(sinks));
    let report = engine.load(doc, seq).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut backend = OfflineBackend::new(RATE, BLOCK);
    backend.start(renderer).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    engine.set_loop(looped).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    engine.play().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut text = String::new();
    let length = engine.length_frames();
    let mut looping = looped;
    // Bounded: three passes plus the tail, with margin.
    let limit = 4 * length + 10 * RATE as u64;
    while backend.frames() < limit {
        backend.run_frames(u64::from(BLOCK));
        text.push_str(&log.drain_text());
        if looping && backend.frames() >= 2 * length {
            engine.set_loop(false).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            looping = false;
        }
        if engine.take_ended() {
            break;
        }
    }
    let _ = backend.stop();
    Ok((report, text))
}
```

Create `crates/vizdaw-play/src/signal.rs`:

```rust
//! Ctrl-C: a `SIGINT` handler that only sets a flag, polled by the main loop.

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};

static INTERRUPTED: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);

extern "C" fn on_sigint(_: i32) {
    INTERRUPTED.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
}

unsafe extern "C" {
    fn signal(sig: i32, handler: extern "C" fn(i32)) -> usize;
}

const SIGINT: i32 = 2;

/// Installs the handler.
pub fn install() {
    // SAFETY: `on_sigint` is async-signal-safe (one relaxed atomic store);
    // `signal(2)` installs it with BSD (sigaction) semantics on macOS.
    unsafe {
        signal(SIGINT, on_sigint);
    }
}

/// True once Ctrl-C was pressed.
pub fn interrupted() -> bool {
    INTERRUPTED.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
}
```

Create `crates/vizdaw-play/src/live.rs`:

```rust
//! Live playback through CoreAudio, the DLS synth and CoreMIDI (macOS).

use crate::args::Args;
use crate::signal;
use core_engine::backend::{AudioBackend, AudioConfig, BackendEvent, StreamInfo};
use core_engine::engine::{Engine, EngineConfig};
use core_engine::render::Renderer;
use core_engine::transport::PlayState;
use core_model::document::Document;
use core_model::ids::SeqId;
use core_model::time::Tick;
use platform_macos::hal::CoreAudioBackend;
use platform_macos::sinks::MacSinks;
use std::io::Write;
use std::time::Duration;

struct Session {
    backend: CoreAudioBackend,
    engine: Engine,
    info: StreamInfo,
}

fn engine_config(args: &Args, rate: f64) -> EngineConfig {
    let mut cfg = EngineConfig::new(rate);
    cfg.route = args.to.into();
    cfg.midi_offset_ns = (args.midi_offset_ms * 1e6).round() as i64;
    cfg
}

/// Opens the device, builds the engine at its rate, loads and starts.
fn open(
    args: &Args,
    doc: &Document,
    seq: SeqId,
    at: Tick,
    playing: bool,
) -> Result<Session, String> {
    let mut backend = CoreAudioBackend::new();
    let cfg = AudioConfig {
        device: args.device.clone(),
        buffer_frames: args.buffer,
    };
    let info = backend
        .open(&cfg)
        .map_err(|e| format!("audio device: {e}"))?;
    let (mut engine, renderer) = Engine::new(
        engine_config(args, info.sample_rate),
        Box::new(MacSinks::new()),
    );
    let report = engine.load(doc, seq).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    for w in &report.warnings {
        eprintln!("warning: {w}");
    }
    engine.set_loop(args.looped).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if at != Tick::ZERO {
        engine.locate_tick(at).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    if playing {
        engine.play().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    backend
        .start(renderer)
        .map_err(|e| format!("audio start: {e}"))?;
    eprintln!(
        "playing on {} at {} Hz, {} frames, {} event(s), compiled in {:.1} ms",
        info.device_name,
        info.sample_rate,
        info.buffer_frames,
        report.events,
        report.compile_time.as_secs_f64() * 1e3
    );
    Ok(Session {
        backend,
        engine,
        info,
    })
}

/// Stops the device and frees the renderer on this thread.
fn close(s: &mut Session) {
    let r: Option<Renderer> = s.backend.stop().ok();
    drop(r);
}

/// Plays until the end (exit 0) or Ctrl-C.
pub fn run(args: &Args, doc: &Document, seq: SeqId) -> Result<(), String> {
    signal::install();
    let meter = doc.sequence(seq).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.meter.clone();
    let mut s = open(args, doc, seq, Tick::ZERO, true)?;
    loop {
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
        let pos = meter.tick_to_bbu(s.engine.position_tick(), 4);
        print!("\r{:>4}.{}.{}  ", pos.bar, pos.beat, pos.unit + 1);
        let _ = std::io::stdout().flush();
        let faults = s.engine.faults();
        if !faults.is_empty() {
            let c = s.engine.counters();
            eprintln!(
                "\nfault: {} (midi dropped {}, swaps deferred {}, xruns {})",
                faults.names().join(", "),
                c.midi_dropped,
                c.swaps_deferred,
                c.xruns
            );
        }
        if s.engine.take_ended() {
            println!();
            break;
        }
        if signal::interrupted() {
            // Stop sends note-offs; give the device a few blocks to play
            // them and the release tails.
            let _ = s.engine.stop();
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300));
            println!();
            break;
        }
        for ev in s.backend.take_events() {
            match ev {
                BackendEvent::BufferSizeChanged(n) => {
                    eprintln!("\nbuffer size changed to {n}");
                    let mut r = s.backend.stop().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                    if n > r.config().max_frames {
                        r.prepare(n);
                    }
                    s.backend.start(r).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                }
                BackendEvent::DeviceLost
                | BackendEvent::DefaultDeviceChanged
                | BackendEvent::SampleRateChanged(_) => {
                    eprintln!("\n{ev:?}: reopening the output");
                    let at = s.engine.position_tick();
                    let playing = s.engine.state() == PlayState::Playing;
                    close(&mut s);
                    let old_rate = s.info.sample_rate;
                    s = open(args, doc, seq, at, playing)?;
                    if s.info.sample_rate != old_rate {
                        eprintln!(
                            "sample rate {old_rate} -> {}: recompiled",
                            s.info.sample_rate
                        );
                    }
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    close(&mut s);
    Ok(())
}
```

Create `crates/vizdaw-play/src/main.rs`:

```rust
//! `vizdaw-play`: play a VizDAW project sequence through the built-in synth.

mod args;
#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
mod live;
mod offline;
mod project;
#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
mod signal;

use args::Args;
use clap::Parser;
use project::Choice;
use std::process::ExitCode;

#[cfg(feature = "rt-check")]
#[global_allocator]
static ALLOC: core_engine::rt_check::CountingAlloc = core_engine::rt_check::CountingAlloc;

fn main() -> ExitCode {
    let args = Args::parse();
    match run(&args) {
        Ok(code) => code,
        Err(e) => {
            eprintln!("error: {e}");
            ExitCode::from(1)
        }
    }
}

fn run(args: &Args) -> Result<ExitCode, String> {
    if let Some(path) = &args.write_demo {
        let doc = core_engine::fixtures::demo_document().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        core_model::file::save(&doc, path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        println!("wrote {}", path.display());
        return Ok(ExitCode::SUCCESS);
    }
    let Some(path) = &args.project else {
        return Err("no project given".into());
    };
    let doc = project::load(path)?;
    if args.list {
        list(&doc);
        return Ok(ExitCode::SUCCESS);
    }
    let seq = match project::choose(&doc, args.seq.as_deref()) {
        Choice::Play(s) => s,
        Choice::Unknown(name) => return Err(format!("no sequence named {name:?}")),
        Choice::Ambiguous(names) => {
            eprintln!("choose a sequence with --seq:");
            for n in names {
                eprintln!("  {n}");
            }
            return Ok(ExitCode::from(2));
        }
    };
    if let Some(out) = &args.offline {
        let (report, text) = offline::render(&doc, seq, args.to.into(), args.looped)?;
        for w in &report.warnings {
            eprintln!("warning: {w}");
        }
        std::fs::write(out, text).map_err(|e| format!("cannot write {}: {e}", out.display()))?;
        eprintln!("wrote {} ({} event(s))", out.display(), report.events);
        return Ok(ExitCode::SUCCESS);
    }
    play(args, &doc, seq)?;
    Ok(ExitCode::SUCCESS)
}

#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
fn play(
    args: &Args,
    doc: &core_model::document::Document,
    seq: core_model::ids::SeqId,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    live::run(args, doc, seq)?;
    #[cfg(feature = "rt-check")]
    eprintln!(
        "rt-check: {} allocation(s) on the render path",
        core_engine::rt_check::count()
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
fn play(
    _: &Args,
    _: &core_model::document::Document,
    _: core_model::ids::SeqId,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    Err("live playback needs macOS; use --offline".into())
}

fn list(doc: &core_model::document::Document) {
    println!("sequences:");
    for s in project::describe_sequences(doc) {
        println!("  {s}");
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
    {
        println!("audio outputs:");
        for d in platform_macos::hal::output_devices() {
            println!("  {d}");
        }
        println!("MIDI destinations:");
        for p in platform_macos::midi::list_destinations() {
            println!("  {}", p.name);
        }
    }
}
```

**Goal:** The manual smoke test documented in `crates/vizdaw-play/README.md` (spec §12), CI steps for the rt-check build, the HAL device test and the offline fixture diff (spec §12 CI, Plan Ruling 28), and the final verification including `--features rt-check`.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/vizdaw-play/README.md`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`

- Consumes: everything above.
- Produces: documentation and CI only.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the README**

Create `crates/vizdaw-play/README.md`:

````markdown
# vizdaw-play

Plays a sequence of a VizDAW project through Apple's built-in DLS synth
(or the "VizDAW Out" virtual MIDI source).

```
vizdaw-play <project.vizdaw> [--seq NAME] [--loop] [--device NAME] [--buffer N]
            [--to builtin|virtual|both] [--midi-offset-ms N] [--offline OUT.txt] [--list]
vizdaw-play --write-demo <dir/demo.vizdaw>
```

Exit codes: 0 played to the end (or Ctrl-C), 1 error, 2 bad arguments or
no `--seq` when the project has several top-level sequences (they are
listed on stderr).

`--offline` renders at 48 kHz in 512-frame blocks without audio hardware
and writes one line per MIDI message: `frame tTARGET hex-bytes`. With
`--loop` it renders three passes and the tail.

## Manual smoke test

Run from the repository root with the toolchain environment exported
(`export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH" DEVELOPER_DIR=/Library/Developer/CommandLineTools`).

1. `cargo run -p vizdaw-play --release -- fixtures/demo.vizdaw --seq Song`
   — drums, bass and chords are audible; bars 5–8 are a fourth higher (the
   drum kit becomes toms there); the ritardando over the last two bars is
   audible; the process exits 0 about a second after the last chord.
2. Add `--loop`, let it wrap twice, press Ctrl-C — no hanging notes.
3. Add `--to virtual` and watch the events in MIDI Monitor (or
   `receivemidi dev "VizDAW Out"`).
4. While it plays, switch the output device in Control Center — playback
   continues on the new device or stops with a reported fault; it never
   crashes.
5. `cargo run -p vizdaw-play --release --features rt-check -- fixtures/demo.vizdaw`
   — the last line reads `rt-check: 0 allocation(s) on the render path`.
````

## Self-Review

**Spec coverage** (spec section → task):

| Spec | Requirement | Task |
|---|---|---|
| §1, §2 | three crates, edition 2024, lints, cfg gates, `// RT`, `// SAFETY:` | 1, 10, 13 (Global Constraints) |

| §11 | `EngineError`, `BackendError`, fault bits clear on read, report not silent | 1, 5, 7 |

| §12 tempo | analytic ramp ±1 frame + closed form, proptest frames and table, nested timing | 3, 5 |

| §12 manual | README smoke test | 14 |

**Placeholder scan:** every code step shows complete file contents; generated files (`fixtures/demo.vizdaw/project.json`, `fixtures/demo.offline.txt`) are produced by the commands in Task 13 Step 4 and pinned by the FNV hash and by Task 8's digest snapshot.

**Type consistency:** names were checked across tasks — `TargetIdx(u8)`, `TargetKind::MidiDestination(MidiPortInfo)`, `Engine::new(EngineConfig, Box<dyn SinkFactory>) -> (Engine, Renderer)`, `SinkFactory::make(&mut self, &TargetKind, f64, u32)`, `Renderer::process(&mut self, &mut [&mut [f32]], u32, BlockTime)`, `OfflineSinks::new(usize)`, `LogRecord::line()`, `fixtures::Builder::notes(seq, lane, &[(i64, u8, u16, i64)])`.

**Review Focus:** each of the five lines names its pinning tests, which exist in Tasks 3, 5, 6, 8, 13 and 14 as written.

**Validation:** this plan was replayed task by task onto a scratch copy of the 2a crate built from the 2a plan; after every task `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` and `cargo test --workspace` were green, the DLS, CoreMIDI and HAL tests ran on macOS, and `vizdaw-play` played the demo audibly for 2.5 s and exited 0 on Ctrl-C with `rt-check: 0 allocation(s)`. The whole plan was then rebuilt against the real `crates/core-model` on branch `overnight` (commit `2cacb29`, including its post-plan fixes): every test, clippy and fmt were green and the generated fixtures were byte-identical.
